--@name SOLACE — Visual Animation & Effects Studio
--@author SOLACE Team
--@shared
--[[
    SOLACE — Visual Animation & Effects Studio for Garry's Mod (StarfallEx)
    Create. Animate. Visualize. Export.

    Self-contained single-file StarfallEx application (Phases 1-14).
    Self-contained: zero external includes or project dependencies.

    Controls:
      - F6            : Toggle SOLACE Studio open / minimized
      - Hold RMB      : Look around in 3D Freecam (release to restore cursor)
      - WASD          : Move Freecam horizontally
      - Space / Ctrl  : Move Freecam up / down
      - Shift         : Boost Freecam movement speed (or Snap during gizmo drag)
      - Mouse Wheel   : Adjust Freecam speed (viewport) / scroll / zoom timeline
      - Left Click    : Select 3D object (Ctrl+Click for multi-select; empty to clear)
      - F             : Focus Freecam on selected object
      - Delete        : Delete selected object(s)
      - G             : Toggle 3D reference grid
      - Q/1, 2, E/3, R/4, T/5 : Select / Move / Rotate / Scale / Pivot tools
      - X             : Toggle World / Local gizmo space
      - Ctrl+D        : Duplicate selected object(s) / hierarchy
      - Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y  : Undo / Redo edits (Esc cancels active drag/input)
]]

local SOLACE = {}

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 1: Bootstrap and permissions
-- ============================================================================
if SERVER then
    -- Singleplayer / Sandbox-first: automatically attach owner HUD on spawn/use
    local function attachOwnerHud(ply, reason)
        if isValid(ply) and ply == owner() then
            local ok, err = pcall(enableHud, ply, true)
            printConsole(string.format("[SOLACE SV] enableHud(%s): ok=%s%s", tostring(reason or "auto"), tostring(ok), ok and "" or (" err=" .. tostring(err))))
            return ok, err
        end
        return false, "not owner"
    end

    hook.add("ClientInitialized", "solace_sv_init_hud", function(ply)
        attachOwnerHud(ply, "ClientInitialized")
    end)

    hook.add("StarfallUsed", "solace_sv_used_hud", function(activator, used)
        if used == chip() then
            local ok, err = attachOwnerHud(activator, "StarfallUsed")
            if isValid(activator) and activator == owner() then
                print("[SOLACE SV] Chip used -> HUD attach=" .. tostring(ok) .. (ok and "" or (" (" .. tostring(err) .. ")")))
            end
        end
    end)

    timer.simple(0.1, function()
        local ok, err = attachOwnerHud(owner(), "startup")
        print("[SOLACE SV] Ready (HUD attach=" .. tostring(ok) .. (ok and "" or (" err=" .. tostring(err))) .. ")")
    end)

    return
end

local permOk, permErr = pcall(setupPermissionRequest, {
    "render.hud",
    "render.calcview",
    "hologram.create",
    "entities.setRenderProperty",
    "material.load",
    "input",
    "input.enablecursor",
    "file.read",
    "file.write",
    "file.findInGame",
    "light.create",
    "particle.create",
    "find",
}, "SOLACE Studio requires HUD rendering, Freecam view control, cursor input, clientside holograms, VFX, and asset/project file access.", true)

if not permOk then
    local fallbackOk, fallbackErr = pcall(setupPermissionRequest, {
        "render.hud",
        "render.calcview",
        "hologram.create",
        "material.load",
        "input",
        "input.enablecursor",
        "file.read",
        "file.write",
        "file.findInGame",
    }, "SOLACE Studio requires HUD rendering, Freecam view control, cursor input, clientside holograms, and asset access.", true)
    printConsole("[SOLACE CL] Full setupPermissionRequest failed (" .. tostring(permErr) .. "), fallback=" .. tostring(fallbackOk) .. (fallbackOk and "" or (" " .. tostring(fallbackErr))))
else
    printConsole("[SOLACE CL] Permission request registered.")
end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 3: Core utilities
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Utils = {}
    local _modelExistsCache = {}

    function Utils.sanitizeExportName(name)
        local clean = string.gsub(string.lower(name or "node"), "[^%w_]+", "_")
        clean = string.gsub(clean, "^_+", "")
        clean = string.gsub(clean, "_+$", "")
        if clean == "" or string.match(clean, "^%d") then
            clean = "obj_" .. clean
        end
        return clean
    end

    function Utils.cycleOption(options, current, dir)
        local idx = 1
        for i, v in ipairs(options) do
            if v == current then idx = i break end
        end
        idx = ((idx - 1 + dir) % #options) + 1
        return options[idx]
    end

    function Utils.fmtVec3(v, dec)
        local f = "%." .. (dec or 1) .. "f, %." .. (dec or 1) .. "f, %." .. (dec or 1) .. "f"
        return string.format(f, v[1] or 0, v[2] or 0, v[3] or 0)
    end

    function Utils.formatTimecode(t)
        local mins = math.floor(t / 60)
        local secs = t - mins * 60
        return string.format("%02d:%05.2f", mins, secs)
    end

    function Utils.checkModelMounted(path)
        if not path or path == "" then return false end
        local cached = _modelExistsCache[path]
        if cached ~= nil then return cached end
        local ok = game.modelExists(path)
        _modelExistsCache[path] = ok
        return ok
    end

    SOLACE.Utils = Utils
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 4: Event system
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)

    local Events = {}
    Events.__index = Events

    function Events.new()
        return setmetatable({
            _listeners = {},
            _log = {},
        }, Events)
    end

    function Events:log(level, msg)
        local entry = {
            time = timer and timer.realtime and timer.realtime() or 0,
            level = level or "info",
            text = tostring(msg or ""),
        }
        self._log[#self._log + 1] = entry
        while #self._log > 120 do
            table.remove(self._log, 1)
        end
    end

    function Events:getLogs()
        return self._log
    end

    function Events:clearLogs()
        self._log = {}
    end

    function Events:on(eventName, id, fn)
        local bucket = self._listeners[eventName]
        if not bucket then
            bucket = {}
            self._listeners[eventName] = bucket
        end
        bucket[id] = fn
    end

    function Events:off(eventName, id)
        local bucket = self._listeners[eventName]
        if bucket then
            bucket[id] = nil
        end
    end

    function Events:emit(eventName, ...)
        if eventName ~= "nodeUpdated" then
            self:log("info", "Event: " .. tostring(eventName))
        end
        local bucket = self._listeners[eventName]
        if not bucket then return end
        for _, fn in pairs(bucket) do
            fn(...)
        end
    end


    SOLACE.Events = Events
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 5: Command and undo/redo system
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)

    local Commands = {}
    Commands.__index = Commands

    function Commands.new(events, maxHistory)
        return setmetatable({
            _events = events,
            _undoStack = {},
            _redoStack = {},
            _maxHistory = maxHistory or 100,
            _version = 1,
        }, Commands)
    end

    function Commands:execute(cmd)
        if not cmd or type(cmd.redo) ~= "function" or type(cmd.undo) ~= "function" then
            return false
        end

        cmd:redo()
        self._undoStack[#self._undoStack + 1] = cmd
        self._redoStack = {}
        self._version = self._version + 1

        if #self._undoStack > self._maxHistory then
            table.remove(self._undoStack, 1)
        end

        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("historyChanged", self)
        end
        return true
    end

    function Commands:canUndo()
        return #self._undoStack > 0
    end

    function Commands:canRedo()
        return #self._redoStack > 0
    end

    function Commands:getUndoLabel()
        local top = self._undoStack[#self._undoStack]
        return top and top.label or nil
    end

    function Commands:getRedoLabel()
        local top = self._redoStack[#self._redoStack]
        return top and top.label or nil
    end

    function Commands:undo()
        local idx = #self._undoStack
        if idx == 0 then return false end
        local cmd = self._undoStack[idx]
        self._undoStack[idx] = nil
        cmd:undo()
        self._redoStack[#self._redoStack + 1] = cmd
        self._version = self._version + 1
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("historyChanged", self)
        end
        return true
    end

    function Commands:redo()
        local idx = #self._redoStack
        if idx == 0 then return false end
        local cmd = self._redoStack[idx]
        self._redoStack[idx] = nil
        cmd:redo()
        self._undoStack[#self._undoStack + 1] = cmd
        self._version = self._version + 1
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("historyChanged", self)
        end
        return true
    end

    function Commands:clear()
        self._undoStack = {}
        self._redoStack = {}
        self._version = self._version + 1
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("historyChanged", self)
        end
    end

    local function applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, id, snap)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.transform or not snap then return end
        local t = node.transform
        t.pos[1], t.pos[2], t.pos[3] = snap.pos[1], snap.pos[2], snap.pos[3]
        t.ang[1], t.ang[2], t.ang[3] = snap.ang[1], snap.ang[2], snap.ang[3]
        t.scale[1], t.scale[2], t.scale[3] = snap.scale[1], snap.scale[2], snap.scale[3]
        if snap.pivot and t.pivot then
            t.pivot[1], t.pivot[2], t.pivot[3] = snap.pivot[1], snap.pivot[2], snap.pivot[3]
        end
        if snap.pivotAng then
            t.pivotAng = t.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }
            t.pivotAng[1], t.pivotAng[2], t.pivotAng[3] = snap.pivotAng[1], snap.pivotAng[2], snap.pivotAng[3]
        end
        sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
    end

    function Commands.createActionCommand(label, undoFn, redoFn)
        return {
            label = label or "Edit",
            undo = undoFn,
            redo = redoFn,
        }
    end

    -- Creates a single reversible command for one or more node transform edits
    function Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, label, entries)
        return {
            label = label or "Transform",
            undo = function()
                for i = 1, #entries do
                    local e = entries[i]
                    applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, e.id, e.before)
                end
            end,
            redo = function()
                for i = 1, #entries do
                    local e = entries[i]
                    applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, e.id, e.after)
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Reparent one or more nodes while restoring exact parent/index/local-transform on Undo/Redo
    function Commands.createReparentCommand(sceneGraph, label, entries, prevSelIds, prevPrimaryId)
        return {
            label = label or "Reparent",
            undo = function()
                for i = #entries, 1, -1 do
                    local e = entries[i]
                    sceneGraph:reparentNode(e.id, e.oldParentId, e.oldIndex, false)
                    if e.oldTransform then
                        applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, e.id, e.oldTransform)
                    end
                end
                if prevSelIds then
                    sceneGraph:setSelectionList(prevSelIds, prevPrimaryId)
                end
            end,
            redo = function()
                for i = 1, #entries do
                    local e = entries[i]
                    sceneGraph:reparentNode(e.id, e.newParentId, e.newIndex, false)
                    if e.newTransform then
                        applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, e.id, e.newTransform)
                    end
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Duplicate one or more subtrees in one atomic command
    function Commands.createDuplicateCommand(sceneGraph, label, specs, prevSelIds, prevPrimaryId)
        return {
            label = label or "Duplicate",
            undo = function()
                for i = #specs, 1, -1 do
                    sceneGraph:removeNode(specs[i].rootId)
                end
                sceneGraph:setSelectionList(prevSelIds or {}, prevPrimaryId)
            end,
            redo = function()
                local newIds = {}
                for i = 1, #specs do
                    local sp = specs[i]
                    local insertedRoot = sceneGraph:insertSubtreeSnapshot(sp.snapshot, sp.parentId, sp.insertIndex)
                    if insertedRoot then
                        newIds[#newIds + 1] = insertedRoot.id
                    end
                end
                if #newIds > 0 then
                    sceneGraph:setSelectionList(newIds, newIds[#newIds])
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Edit appearance (color RGBA, material, visibility) for one or more nodes
    function Commands.createAppearanceCommand(sceneGraph, label, entries)
        local function applyApp(id, snap)
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if not node or not snap then return end
            if snap.color ~= nil and node.appearance and node.appearance.color then
                local c = node.appearance.color
                c[1], c[2], c[3], c[4] = snap.color[1], snap.color[2], snap.color[3], snap.color[4]
            end
            if snap.material ~= nil and node.appearance then
                node.appearance.material = snap.material
            end
            if node.appearance then
                if snap.renderMode ~= nil then node.appearance.renderMode = snap.renderMode end
                if snap.renderFX ~= nil then node.appearance.renderFX = snap.renderFX end
                if snap.cullMode ~= nil then node.appearance.cullMode = snap.cullMode end
                if snap.suppressEngineLighting ~= nil then node.appearance.suppressEngineLighting = snap.suppressEngineLighting end
                if snap.holoAnim ~= nil then node.appearance.holoAnim = snap.holoAnim end
                if snap.holoAnimRate ~= nil then node.appearance.holoAnimRate = snap.holoAnimRate end
                if snap.alpha ~= nil then node.appearance.alpha = snap.alpha end
            end
            if snap.visible ~= nil and id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                node.visible = snap.visible
            end
            sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
        end
        return {
            label = label or "Appearance",
            undo = function()
                for i = 1, #entries do
                    local e = entries[i]
                    applyApp(e.id, e.before)
                end
            end,
            redo = function()
                for i = 1, #entries do
                    local e = entries[i]
                    applyApp(e.id, e.after)
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Group selected top-level nodes into a newly created Group in one undoable command
    function Commands.createGroupSelectedCommand(sceneGraph, label, groupSnapshot, groupParentId, moveEntries, prevSelIds, prevPrimaryId)
        return {
            label = label or "Group Selected",
            undo = function()
                for i = #moveEntries, 1, -1 do
                    local e = moveEntries[i]
                    sceneGraph:reparentNode(e.id, e.oldParentId, e.oldIndex, false)
                    if e.oldTransform then
                        applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, e.id, e.oldTransform)
                    end
                end
                sceneGraph:removeNode(groupSnapshot.root.id)
                sceneGraph:setSelectionList(prevSelIds or {}, prevPrimaryId)
            end,
            redo = function()
                local grp = sceneGraph:insertSubtreeSnapshot(groupSnapshot, groupParentId)
                for i = 1, #moveEntries do
                    local e = moveEntries[i]
                    sceneGraph:reparentNode(e.id, grp.id, nil, false)
                    if e.newTransform then
                        applyTransformSnapshot(sceneGraph, e.id, e.newTransform)
                    end
                end
                if grp then
                    sceneGraph:setSelectedId(grp.id)
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Add a single node/subtree (e.g. Create Folder / Group) with Undo/Redo
    function Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, label, snapshot, parentId, insertIndex, prevSelIds, prevPrimaryId)
        return {
            label = label or "Create Node",
            undo = function()
                sceneGraph:removeNode(snapshot.root.id)
                sceneGraph:setSelectionList(prevSelIds or {}, prevPrimaryId)
            end,
            redo = function()
                local node = sceneGraph:insertSubtreeSnapshot(snapshot, parentId, insertIndex)
                if node then
                    sceneGraph:setSelectedId(node.id)
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Delete one or more subtrees with full Undo/Redo restoration
    function Commands.createDeleteCommand(sceneGraph, label, deletedSpecs, prevSelIds, prevPrimaryId)
        return {
            label = label or "Delete",
            undo = function()
                for i = #deletedSpecs, 1, -1 do
                    local sp = deletedSpecs[i]
                    sceneGraph:insertSubtreeSnapshot(sp.snapshot, sp.parentId, sp.index)
                end
                sceneGraph:setSelectionList(prevSelIds or {}, prevPrimaryId)
            end,
            redo = function()
                for i = 1, #deletedSpecs do
                    sceneGraph:removeNode(deletedSpecs[i].rootId)
                end
            end,
        }
    end

    -- Rename a node with Undo/Redo
    function Commands.createRenameCommand(sceneGraph, id, oldName, newName)
        return {
            label = "Rename",
            undo = function()
                sceneGraph:renameNode(id, oldName)
            end,
            redo = function()
                sceneGraph:renameNode(id, newName)
            end,
        }
    end


    SOLACE.Commands = Commands
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 6: Scene IR
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants



    local IR = {}

    local function vec3(x, y, z)
        return { x or 0, y or 0, z or 0 }
    end

    local function ang3(p, y, r)
        return { p or 0, y or 0, r or 0 }
    end

    local function rgba(r, g, b, a)
        return { r or 255, g or 255, b or 255, a or 255 }
    end

    function IR.cloneVec3(v)
        return { v[1] or 0, v[2] or 0, v[3] or 0 }
    end

    function IR.cloneColor(c)
        return { c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, c[4] or 255 }
    end

    -- Runtime Starfall object conversions (used only by adapters/viewport, never stored in IR)
    function IR.toVector(v)
        return Vector(v[1] or 0, v[2] or 0, v[3] or 0)
    end

    function IR.toAngle(a)
        return Angle(a[1] or 0, a[2] or 0, a[3] or 0)
    end

    function IR.toColor(c)
        return Color(c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, c[4] or 255)
    end

    function IR.fromVector(v)
        return { v.x, v.y, v.z }
    end

    function IR.fromAngle(a)
        return { a.p, a.y, a.r }
    end

    function IR.fromColor(c)
        return { c.r, c.g, c.b, c.a }
    end

    function IR.createTransform(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        return {
            pos      = opts.pos      and IR.cloneVec3(opts.pos)      or vec3(0, 0, 0),
            ang      = opts.ang      and IR.cloneVec3(opts.ang)      or ang3(0, 0, 0),
            scale    = opts.scale    and IR.cloneVec3(opts.scale)    or vec3(1, 1, 1),
            pivot    = opts.pivot    and IR.cloneVec3(opts.pivot)    or vec3(0, 0, 0),
            pivotAng = opts.pivotAng and IR.cloneVec3(opts.pivotAng) or ang3(0, 0, 0),
        }
    end

    function IR.createAppearance(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        return {
            model = opts.model or "",
            material = opts.material or "",
            submaterials = opts.submaterials or {},
            color = opts.color and IR.cloneColor(opts.color) or rgba(255, 255, 255, 255),
            renderMode = opts.renderMode or 0,
            renderFX = opts.renderFX or 0,
            cullMode = opts.cullMode or 0,
            skin = opts.skin or 0,
            bodygroups = opts.bodygroups or {},
            clips = opts.clips or {},
            suppressLighting = opts.suppressLighting or false,
            holoAnim = opts.holoAnim or "",
            holoAnimRate = opts.holoAnimRate or 1.0,
        }
    end

    function IR.createKeyframe(time, value, easing)
        return {
            time = time or 0,
            value = value,
            easing = easing or Constants.EASING.LINEAR,
        }
    end

    function IR.createTrack(propertyPath)
        return {
            property = propertyPath,
            enabled = true,
            keyframes = {},
        }
    end

    function IR.createEffectPayload(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        return {
            preset = opts.preset or opts.id or "spark",
            mode = opts.mode or "particles", -- "particles" | "beam" | "orb" | "light"
            material = opts.material or opts.mat or "sprites/light_glow02_add",
            rate = opts.rate or 12,
            speed = opts.speed or 60,
            spread = opts.spread or 25,
            gravity = type(opts.gravity) == "table" and (opts.gravity[3] or -120) or (tonumber(opts.gravity) or -120),
            startSize = opts.startSize or 6,
            endSize = opts.endSize or 0,
            startAlpha = opts.startAlpha or 220,
            endAlpha = opts.endAlpha or 0,
            lifetime = opts.lifetime or 1.0,
            delay = opts.delay or 0,
            duration = opts.duration or 0, -- 0 = continuous
            beamLen = opts.beamLen or opts.beamLength or 64,
            lightSize = opts.lightSize or 180,
            lightBrightness = opts.lightBrightness or 2,
        }
    end

    function IR.createNode(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        local kind = opts.kind or Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL

        local node = {
            id = opts.id or "",
            name = opts.name or "Node",
            exportName = opts.exportName or "",
            kind = kind,
            parentId = opts.parentId,       -- nil only on root
            children = opts.children or {}, -- ordered array of child node IDs
            visible = opts.visible ~= false,
            locked = opts.locked or false,
            expanded = opts.expanded ~= false,
            description = opts.description or "",
            tags = opts.tags or {},

            transform = IR.createTransform(opts.transform),
            appearance = IR.createAppearance(opts.appearance),

            physics = {
                enabled = false,
                frozen = true,
                mass = 50,
                gravity = true,
                material = "",
            },

            -- Specialized payload blocks (populated for sprite, light, effect, ragdoll, camera)
            sprite = opts.sprite,
            light = opts.light,
            effect = opts.effect,
            bones = opts.bones,
            camera = opts.camera,

            -- Animation tracks keyed by property path (e.g. "transform.pos", "appearance.color")
            tracks = opts.tracks or {},
        }

        if kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA then
            local c = opts.camera or {}
            node.camera = {
                fov = c.fov or 80,
                lookAtNodeId = c.lookAtNodeId or nil,
                focusDist = c.focusDist or 250,
                shakeAmplitude = c.shakeAmplitude or 0,
                shakeFrequency = c.shakeFrequency or 8,
                shakeDuration = c.shakeDuration or 0,
            }
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE then
            local sp = opts.sprite or {}
            node.sprite = {
                width = sp.width or 32,
                height = sp.height or 32,
                roll = sp.roll or 0,
                spawnTime = sp.spawnTime or 0,
                lifetime = sp.lifetime or 0, -- 0 = persistent
                fadeIn = sp.fadeIn or 0,
                fadeOut = sp.fadeOut or 0,
                additive = sp.additive or false,
            }
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT then
            local lt = opts.light or {}
            node.light = {
                size = lt.size or 256,
                brightness = lt.brightness or 2,
                decay = lt.decay or 1000,
                projected = lt.projected or false,
                fov = lt.fov or 60,
            }
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE then
            node.effect = IR.createEffectPayload(opts.effect)
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL then
            node.bones = opts.bones or {}
        end

        return node
    end

    function IR.createProject(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        local rootId = "root"
        local rootNode = IR.createNode({
            id = rootId,
            name = opts.name or "Untitled Scene",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.ROOT,
            parentId = nil,
            expanded = true,
            description = "Root container for SOLACE scene hierarchy",
        })

        return {
            schemaVersion = Constants.IR_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            app = Constants.APP_NAME,
            name = opts.name or "Untitled Scene",
            author = opts.author or "",
            createdAt = opts.createdAt or os.time(),
            modifiedAt = opts.modifiedAt or os.time(),
            nextId = 1,
            timeline = {
                fps = 30,
                duration = 10.0,
            },
            origin = { 0, 0, 0 },
            rootId = rootId,
            nodes = {
                [rootId] = rootNode,
            },
            presets = {},
        }
    end


    SOLACE.IR = IR
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 7: Scene graph
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Utils = SOLACE.Utils
    local IR = SOLACE.IR



    local SceneGraph = {}
    SceneGraph.__index = SceneGraph

    function SceneGraph.new(events)
        local self = setmetatable({
            _events = events,
            _project = IR.createProject({ name = "Untitled Scene" }),
            _selectedIds = {},
            _selectedMap = {},
            _primarySelectedId = nil,
            _dirty = false,
            _version = 1,
            _flatCacheVersion = -1,
            _flatCacheQuery = nil,
            _flatCache = nil,
            _nodeCountVersion = -1,
            _nodeCountCache = 0,
        }, SceneGraph)

        -- Create initial StudioCamera IR node
        local cam = self:addNode({
            name = "StudioCamera",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA,
            parentId = self._project.rootId,
            description = "Primary editor & cinematic camera actor",
        })
        self:setSelectedId(cam.id)

        return self
    end

    function SceneGraph:getProject()
        return self._project
    end

    function SceneGraph:getRootId()
        return self._project.rootId
    end

    function SceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not id then return nil end
        return self._project.nodes[id]
    end

    function SceneGraph:getAllNodes()
        return self._project.nodes
    end

    -- Selection API (supports both primary single selection and multi-selection)
    function SceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return self._primarySelectedId
    end

    function SceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        return self._primarySelectedId and self._project.nodes[self._primarySelectedId] or nil
    end

    function SceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
        return self._selectedIds
    end

    function SceneGraph:isSelected(id)
        return id ~= nil and self._selectedMap[id] == true
    end

    function SceneGraph:clearSelection()
        if #self._selectedIds == 0 and self._primarySelectedId == nil then return end
        self._selectedIds = {}
        self._selectedMap = {}
        self._primarySelectedId = nil
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("selectionChanged", nil, nil, self._selectedIds)
        end
    end

    function SceneGraph:setSelectedId(id)
        if id == nil then
            self:clearSelection()
            return
        end
        if not self._project.nodes[id] then return end
        if #self._selectedIds == 1 and self._primarySelectedId == id then return end

        self._selectedIds = { id }
        self._selectedMap = { [id] = true }
        self._primarySelectedId = id
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("selectionChanged", id, self:getNode(id), self._selectedIds)
        end
    end

    function SceneGraph:toggleSelectedId(id)
        if not id or not self._project.nodes[id] then return end
        if self._selectedMap[id] then
            self._selectedMap[id] = nil
            for i = #self._selectedIds, 1, -1 do
                if self._selectedIds[i] == id then
                    table.remove(self._selectedIds, i)
                    break
                end
            end
            if self._primarySelectedId == id then
                self._primarySelectedId = self._selectedIds[#self._selectedIds]
            end
        else
            self._selectedMap[id] = true
            self._selectedIds[#self._selectedIds + 1] = id
            self._primarySelectedId = id
        end

        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("selectionChanged", self._primarySelectedId, self:getSelectedNode(), self._selectedIds)
        end
    end

    function SceneGraph:setSelectionList(ids, primaryId)
        local clean = {}
        local map = {}
        if ids then
            for i = 1, #ids do
                local id = ids[i]
                if id and self._project.nodes[id] and not map[id] then
                    map[id] = true
                    clean[#clean + 1] = id
                end
            end
        end
        if #clean == 0 then
            self:clearSelection()
            return
        end
        local prim = (primaryId and map[primaryId]) and primaryId or clean[#clean]
        self._selectedIds = clean
        self._selectedMap = map
        self._primarySelectedId = prim
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("selectionChanged", prim, self:getNode(prim), self._selectedIds)
        end
    end

    -- Returns selected non-root IDs excluding any node whose ancestor is also selected
    function SceneGraph:getTopLevelSelectedIds()
        local rootId = self._project.rootId
        local selMap = {}
        for i = 1, #self._selectedIds do
            local id = self._selectedIds[i]
            if id ~= rootId and self._project.nodes[id] then
                selMap[id] = true
            end
        end
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, #self._selectedIds do
            local id = self._selectedIds[i]
            if selMap[id] then
                local node = self._project.nodes[id]
                local cur = node and node.parentId and self._project.nodes[node.parentId] or nil
                local hasSelAnc = false
                local guard = 0
                while cur and cur.id ~= rootId and guard < 64 do
                    if selMap[cur.id] then
                        hasSelAnc = true
                        break
                    end
                    cur = cur.parentId and self._project.nodes[cur.parentId] or nil
                    guard = guard + 1
                end
                if not hasSelAnc then
                    out[#out + 1] = id
                end
            end
        end
        return out
    end

    function SceneGraph:isDirty()
        return self._dirty
    end

    function SceneGraph:markDirty()
        self._dirty = true
        self._version = (self._version or 0) + 1
        self._project.modifiedAt = os.time()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("sceneDirty", self._project)
        end
    end

    function SceneGraph:bumpHierarchyVersion()
        self._version = (self._version or 0) + 1
    end

    function SceneGraph:generateId(prefix)
        local idx = self._project.nextId
        self._project.nextId = idx + 1
        return string.format("%s_%04d", prefix or "node", idx)
    end

    function SceneGraph:addNode(opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        local parentId = opts.parentId or self._project.rootId
        local parent = self._project.nodes[parentId]
        if not parent then
            parentId = self._project.rootId
            parent = self._project.nodes[parentId]
        end

        local id = opts.id
        if not id or id == "" or self._project.nodes[id] then
            id = self:generateId(opts.kind or "node")
        end

        local node = IR.createNode(opts)
        node.id = id
        node.parentId = parentId
        if node.exportName == "" then
            node.exportName = Utils.sanitizeExportName(node.name) .. "_" .. string.sub(id, -4)
        end

        self._project.nodes[id] = node
        parent.children[#parent.children + 1] = id
        if parentId ~= self._project.rootId then
            parent.expanded = true
        end

        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("nodeAdded", node)
        end
        return node
    end

    function SceneGraph:createModel(name, modelPath, posTbl, angTbl, parentId)
        return self:addNode({
            name = name or "Model",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL,
            parentId = parentId or self._project.rootId,
            transform = {
                pos = posTbl or { 0, 0, 0 },
                ang = angTbl or { 0, 0, 0 },
                scale = { 1, 1, 1 },
                pivot = { 0, 0, 0 },
            },
            appearance = {
                model = modelPath,
            },
        })
    end

    function SceneGraph:createGroup(name, posTbl, parentId)
        return self:addNode({
            name = name or "Group",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP,
            parentId = parentId or self._project.rootId,
            transform = {
                pos = posTbl or { 0, 0, 0 },
            },
        })
    end

    function SceneGraph:createFolder(name, parentId)
        return self:addNode({
            name = name or "Folder",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER,
            parentId = parentId or self._project.rootId,
        })
    end

    function SceneGraph:createSprite(name, materialPath, posTbl, parentId)
        return self:addNode({
            name = name or "Sprite",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE,
            parentId = parentId or self._project.rootId,
            transform = {
                pos = posTbl or { 0, 0, 0 },
            },
            appearance = {
                material = materialPath or "sprites/light_glow02_add",
            },
        })
    end

    function SceneGraph:removeNode(id)
        if not id or id == self._project.rootId then return false end
        local node = self._project.nodes[id]
        if not node then return false end

        -- Gather entire subtree IDs post-order
        local removedIds = {}
        local function collect(nid)
            local n = self._project.nodes[nid]
            if not n then return end
            for i = 1, #n.children do
                collect(n.children[i])
            end
            removedIds[#removedIds + 1] = nid
        end
        collect(id)

        -- Detach from parent's children array
        local parent = node.parentId and self._project.nodes[node.parentId]
        if parent then
            for i = #parent.children, 1, -1 do
                if parent.children[i] == id then
                    table.remove(parent.children, i)
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        local selChanged = false
        for _, rid in ipairs(removedIds) do
            self._project.nodes[rid] = nil
            if self._selectedMap[rid] then
                self._selectedMap[rid] = nil
                selChanged = true
            end
        end

        if selChanged then
            for i = #self._selectedIds, 1, -1 do
                if not self._selectedMap[self._selectedIds[i]] then
                    table.remove(self._selectedIds, i)
                end
            end
            if not self._selectedMap[self._primarySelectedId] then
                self._primarySelectedId = self._selectedIds[#self._selectedIds]
            end
        end

        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("nodeRemoved", removedIds)
            if selChanged then
                self._events:emit("selectionChanged", self._primarySelectedId, self:getSelectedNode(), self._selectedIds)
            end
        end
        return true
    end

    function SceneGraph:renameNode(id, newName)
        local node = self:getNode(id)
        if not node or not newName then return false end
        local trimmed = string.trim(newName)
        if trimmed == "" then return false end
        node.name = trimmed
        if id == self._project.rootId then
            self._project.name = trimmed
        else
            node.exportName = Utils.sanitizeExportName(trimmed) .. "_" .. string.sub(id, -4)
        end
        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("nodeUpdated", node)
        end
        return true
    end

    function SceneGraph:toggleNodeVisible(id)
        if not id or id == self._project.rootId then return false end
        local node = self:getNode(id)
        if not node then return false end
        node.visible = not node.visible
        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("nodeUpdated", node)
        end
        return true
    end

    function SceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
        local node = self:getNode(id)
        if not node then return end
        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("nodeUpdated", node)
        end
    end

    function SceneGraph:getNodeCount()
        if self._nodeCountVersion == self._version then
            return self._nodeCountCache
        end
        local count = 0
        for id in pairs(self._project.nodes) do
            if id ~= self._project.rootId then
                count = count + 1
            end
        end
        self._nodeCountVersion = self._version
        self._nodeCountCache = count
        return count
    end

    -- Flattened depth-first list for Explorer rendering (respects expanded state & optional search filter)
    function SceneGraph:getFlatHierarchy(searchQuery)
        local q = searchQuery and string.lower(string.trim(searchQuery)) or ""
        if self._flatCache and self._flatCacheVersion == self._version and self._flatCacheQuery == q then
            return self._flatCache
        end

        local out = {}
        local hasQuery = q ~= ""

        local function matches(node)
            if not hasQuery then return true end
            return string.find(string.lower(node.name), q, 1, true) ~= nil
                or string.find(string.lower(node.kind), q, 1, true) ~= nil
        end

        local function subtreeMatches(node)
            if matches(node) then return true end
            for _, childId in ipairs(node.children) do
                local child = self._project.nodes[childId]
                if child and subtreeMatches(child) then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function walk(nodeId, depth)
            local node = self._project.nodes[nodeId]
            if not node then return end
            if hasQuery and not subtreeMatches(node) then return end

            out[#out + 1] = {
                node = node,
                depth = depth,
                hasChildren = #node.children > 0,
            }

            if node.expanded or hasQuery then
                for _, childId in ipairs(node.children) do
                    walk(childId, depth + 1)
                end
            end
        end

        walk(self._project.rootId, 0)
        self._flatCacheVersion = self._version
        self._flatCacheQuery = q
        self._flatCache = out
        return out
    end

    function SceneGraph:setOrigin(vecTbl)
        self._project.origin = IR.cloneVec3(vecTbl)
        self:markDirty()
    end

    function SceneGraph:setProjectName(name)
        self:renameNode(self._project.rootId, name)
    end

    local function deepCopyTable(val, seen)
        if type(val) ~= "table" then return val end
        seen = seen or {}
        if seen[val] then return seen[val] end
        local copy = {}
        seen[val] = copy
        for k, v in pairs(val) do
            copy[deepCopyTable(k, seen)] = deepCopyTable(v, seen)
        end
        return copy
    end

    function SceneGraph:isDescendant(candidateId, ancestorId)
        if not candidateId or not ancestorId then return false end
        if candidateId == ancestorId then return true end
        local rootId = self._project.rootId
        local cur = self._project.nodes[candidateId]
        local guard = 0
        while cur and guard < 64 do
            if cur.id == ancestorId then return true end
            if cur.id == rootId or not cur.parentId then break end
            cur = self._project.nodes[cur.parentId]
            guard = guard + 1
        end
        return false
    end

    function SceneGraph:canAcceptChild(parentId)
        local parent = self:getNode(parentId)
        if not parent then return false end
        local k = parent.kind
        return k == Constants.NODE_KINDS.ROOT
            or k == Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP
            or k == Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER
    end

    function SceneGraph:canReparent(nodeId, newParentId)
        if not nodeId or not newParentId then return false, "Missing node" end
        local rootId = self._project.rootId
        if nodeId == rootId then return false, "Cannot reparent Scene Root" end
        if nodeId == newParentId then return false, "Cannot parent object to itself" end
        local node = self:getNode(nodeId)
        local newParent = self:getNode(newParentId)
        if not node or not newParent then return false, "Node not found" end
        if not self:canAcceptChild(newParentId) then
            return false, "Target node cannot contain children"
        end
        if self:isDescendant(newParentId, nodeId) then
            return false, "Circular hierarchy not allowed"
        end
        return true, nil
    end

    function SceneGraph:getChildIndex(parentId, childId)
        local parent = self:getNode(parentId)
        if not parent then return nil end
        for i = 1, #parent.children do
            if parent.children[i] == childId then
                return i
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    function SceneGraph:reparentNode(nodeId, newParentId, insertIndex, preserveWorld)
        local ok, reason = self:canReparent(nodeId, newParentId)
        if not ok then return false, reason end

        local node = self:getNode(nodeId)
        local oldParent = node.parentId and self:getNode(node.parentId) or nil
        local newParent = self:getNode(newParentId)

        local wPos, wAng, wScale
        if preserveWorld and SOLACE.Math3D and SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform then
            wPos, wAng, wScale = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(self, nodeId)
        end

        local oldIndex = nil
        if oldParent then
            for i = #oldParent.children, 1, -1 do
                if oldParent.children[i] == nodeId then
                    oldIndex = i
                    table.remove(oldParent.children, i)
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        local targetIdx = insertIndex or (#newParent.children + 1)
        if oldParent == newParent and oldIndex and insertIndex and oldIndex < insertIndex then
            targetIdx = targetIdx - 1
        end
        targetIdx = math.clamp(targetIdx, 1, #newParent.children + 1)
        table.insert(newParent.children, targetIdx, nodeId)
        node.parentId = newParentId
        if newParentId ~= self._project.rootId then
            newParent.expanded = true
        end

        if preserveWorld and wPos and SOLACE.Math3D and SOLACE.Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal then
            local lPos, lAng, lScale = SOLACE.Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(self, newParentId, wPos, wAng, wScale)
            if node.transform then
                node.transform.pos = lPos
                node.transform.ang = lAng
                node.transform.scale = lScale
            end
        end

        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("nodeUpdated", node)
        end
        return true, nil
    end

    -- Deep-clones a node subtree into a standalone snapshot (preserveIds=true for Delete/Undo, false for Duplicate)
    function SceneGraph:cloneSubtreeSnapshot(rootNodeId, preserveIds, topNameSuffix)
        local srcRoot = self:getNode(rootNodeId)
        if not srcRoot or rootNodeId == self._project.rootId then return nil end

        local list = {}
        local idMap = {}

        local function collect(nid, isTop)
            local src = self:getNode(nid)
            if not src then return nil end
            local newId = preserveIds and src.id or self:generateId(src.kind)
            idMap[src.id] = newId

            local copy = deepCopyTable(src)
            copy.id = newId
            if isTop and topNameSuffix and topNameSuffix ~= "" then
                copy.name = src.name .. topNameSuffix
            end
            if not preserveIds then
                copy.exportName = Utils.sanitizeExportName(copy.name) .. "_" .. string.sub(newId, -4)
            end

            local newChildren = {}
            for i = 1, #src.children do
                local childCopy = collect(src.children[i], false)
                if childCopy then
                    childCopy.parentId = newId
                    newChildren[#newChildren + 1] = childCopy.id
                end
            end
            copy.children = newChildren
            list[#list + 1] = copy
            return copy
        end

        local clonedRoot = collect(rootNodeId, true)
        return {
            root = clonedRoot,
            nodes = list, -- post-order; children added before parents or in any order
        }
    end

    function SceneGraph:insertSubtreeSnapshot(snapshot, parentId, insertIndex)
        if not snapshot or not snapshot.root or not snapshot.nodes then return nil end
        local pId = parentId or self._project.rootId
        local parent = self:getNode(pId)
        if not parent then
            pId = self._project.rootId
            parent = self:getNode(pId)
        end

        -- Insert copies so snapshot stays immutable across repeated undo/redo cycles
        local inserted = {}
        for i = 1, #snapshot.nodes do
            local copy = deepCopyTable(snapshot.nodes[i])
            self._project.nodes[copy.id] = copy
            inserted[#inserted + 1] = copy
        end

        local rootCopy = self._project.nodes[snapshot.root.id]
        if not rootCopy then return nil end
        rootCopy.parentId = pId

        local idx = math.clamp(insertIndex or (#parent.children + 1), 1, #parent.children + 1)
        table.insert(parent.children, idx, rootCopy.id)
        if pId ~= self._project.rootId then
            parent.expanded = true
        end

        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            for i = 1, #inserted do
                self._events:emit("nodeAdded", inserted[i])
            end
        end
        return rootCopy
    end

    function SceneGraph:replaceProject(newProject)
        if not newProject or not newProject.nodes or not newProject.rootId then return false end
        self._project = newProject
        self._selectedIds = {}
        self._selectedMap = {}
        self._primarySelectedId = nil
        self:markDirty()
        if self._events then
            self._events:emit("projectReplaced", self._project)
        end
        return true
    end

    function SceneGraph:getNodesByKind(kind)
        local out = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(self:getFlatHierarchy("")) do
            if item.node.kind == kind then
                out[#out + 1] = item.node
            end
        end
        return out
    end

    function SceneGraph:createCamera(name, posTbl, angTbl, parentId, fov)
        return self:addNode({
            name = name or "Camera",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA,
            parentId = parentId,
            transform = { pos = posTbl or { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = angTbl or { 0, 0, 0 } },
            camera = { fov = fov or 80 },
        })
    end

    function SceneGraph:createEffect(name, effectOpts, matPath, colorTbl, posTbl, parentId)
        return self:addNode({
            name = name or "Effect",
            kind = Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT,
            parentId = parentId,
            transform = { pos = posTbl or { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = { 0, 0, 0 } },
            appearance = {
                material = matPath or "sprites/light_glow02_add",
                color = colorTbl or { 255, 200, 90, 255 },
            },
            effect = effectOpts,
        })
    end

    function SceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(kind, name, posTbl, angTbl, extra)
        local id = self:generateId(kind)
        local opts = {}
        if extra then
            for k, v in pairs(extra) do opts[k] = v end
        end
        opts.kind = kind
        opts.id = id
        opts.name = name
        opts.exportName = opts.exportName or (Utils.sanitizeExportName(name) .. "_" .. string.sub(id, -4))
        opts.transform = opts.transform or { pos = posTbl, ang = angTbl }
        local node = IR.createNode(opts)
        return {
            root = node,
            nodes = { node },
        }
    end


    SOLACE.SceneGraph = SceneGraph
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 8: Runtime rendering adapter
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local IR = SOLACE.IR



    local SceneRuntime = {}
    SceneRuntime.__index = SceneRuntime

    local DEFAULT_MINS = Vector(-8, -8, -8)
    local DEFAULT_MAXS = Vector(8, 8, 8)

    function SceneRuntime.new(sceneGraph, events)
        local self = setmetatable({
            _scene = sceneGraph,
            _events = events,
            _entries = {},       -- [nodeId] = { kind, holo, material, matPath, worldPos, worldAng, worldScale, boundsMin, boundsMax, visible, errorMsg }
            _dirtyNodes = {},    -- [nodeId] = true
            _pendingRemove = {}, -- array of nodeIds
            _matCache = {},      -- [matPath] = LockedMaterial | false
            _lastError = nil,
        }, SceneRuntime)

        events:on("nodeAdded", "runtime_sync", function(node)
            self:markDirty(node.id)
        end)
        events:on("nodeUpdated", "runtime_sync", function(node)
            self:markSubtreeDirty(node.id)
        end)
        events:on("nodeRemoved", "runtime_sync", function(removedIds)
            for _, id in ipairs(removedIds) do
                self._dirtyNodes[id] = nil
                self._pendingRemove[#self._pendingRemove + 1] = id
            end
        end)
        events:on("projectReplaced", "runtime_sync", function()
            self:cleanup()
            for id in pairs(sceneGraph:getAllNodes()) do
                if id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                    self._dirtyNodes[id] = true
                end
            end
        end)

        -- Queue any pre-existing nodes
        for id in pairs(sceneGraph:getAllNodes()) do
            if id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                self._dirtyNodes[id] = true
            end
        end

        return self
    end

    function SceneRuntime:markDirty(nodeId)
        if nodeId and nodeId ~= self._scene:getRootId() then
            self._dirtyNodes[nodeId] = true
        end
    end

    function SceneRuntime:markSubtreeDirty(nodeId)
        local node = self._scene:getNode(nodeId)
        if not node then return end
        if node.id ~= self._scene:getRootId() then
            self._dirtyNodes[node.id] = true
        end
        for _, childId in ipairs(node.children) do
            self:markSubtreeDirty(childId)
        end
    end

    function SceneRuntime:getLastError()
        return self._lastError
    end

    function SceneRuntime:getEntries()
        return self._entries
    end

    function SceneRuntime:getEntry(nodeId)
        return self._entries[nodeId]
    end

    function SceneRuntime:_loadMaterialCached(matPath)
        if not matPath or matPath == "" then return nil end
        local cached = self._matCache[matPath]
        if cached ~= nil then
            return cached ~= false and cached or nil
        end
        if not hasPermission("material.load") then
            return nil
        end
        local ok, mat = pcall(material.load, matPath)
        if ok and mat then
            self._matCache[matPath] = mat
            return mat
        end
        self._matCache[matPath] = false
        return nil
    end

    function SceneRuntime:_destroyEntry(id)
        local entry = self._entries[id]
        if not entry then return end
        if entry.holo and isValid(entry.holo) then
            pcall(function() entry.holo:remove() end)
        end
        if entry.emitter then
            pcall(function()
                if entry.emitter.isValid and entry.emitter:isValid() then
                    entry.emitter:destroy()
                end
            end)
            entry.emitter = nil
        end
        if entry.dynLight then
            pcall(function() entry.dynLight:destroy() end)
            entry.dynLight = nil
        end
        entry.softParticles = nil
        self._entries[id] = nil
    end

    local function applyBoneOverrides(ent, node, entry)
        if not ent or not isValid(ent) then return end
        if type(ent.getBoneCount) ~= "function" or type(ent.manipulateBoneAngles) ~= "function" then return end
        if not hasPermission("entities.setRenderProperty") then return end

        local okCnt, boneCount = pcall(function() return ent:getBoneCount() end)
        if not okCnt or not boneCount or boneCount <= 0 then return end

        local bonesTbl = node.bones or {}
        local applied = entry._appliedBones or {}
        entry._appliedBones = applied

        -- Reset previously modified bones that were removed
        for idx in pairs(applied) do
            if not bonesTbl[idx] then
                if idx >= 0 and idx < boneCount then
                    pcall(function()
                        ent:manipulateBonePosition(idx, Vector(0, 0, 0))
                        ent:manipulateBoneAngles(idx, Angle(0, 0, 0))
                        ent:manipulateBoneScale(idx, Vector(1, 1, 1))
                    end)
                end
                applied[idx] = nil
            end
        end

        for idx, b in pairs(bonesTbl) do
            local bi = tonumber(idx)
            if bi and bi >= 0 and bi < boneCount and type(b) == "table" then
                local bp = b.pos or { 0, 0, 0 }
                local ba = b.ang or { 0, 0, 0 }
                local bs = b.scale or { 1, 1, 1 }
                pcall(function()
                    ent:manipulateBonePosition(bi, Vector(bp[1] or 0, bp[2] or 0, bp[3] or 0))
                    ent:manipulateBoneAngles(bi, Angle(ba[1] or 0, ba[2] or 0, ba[3] or 0))
                    ent:manipulateBoneScale(bi, Vector(bs[1] or 1, bs[2] or 1, bs[3] or 1))
                end)
                applied[bi] = true
            end
        end
    end

    function SceneRuntime:getNodeBones(nodeId)
        local entry = self._entries[nodeId]
        local ent = entry and ((entry.holo and isValid(entry.holo) and entry.holo) or (entry.linkedEnt and isValid(entry.linkedEnt) and entry.linkedEnt)) or nil
        if not ent or type(ent.getBoneCount) ~= "function" then return {} end
        if entry._cachedBoneList and entry._cachedBoneEnt == ent then
            return entry._cachedBoneList
        end
        local list = {}
        local okCnt, cnt = pcall(function() return ent:getBoneCount() end)
        if okCnt and cnt and cnt > 0 then
            local limit = math.min(cnt, 128)
            for i = 0, limit - 1 do
                local okName, bName = pcall(function() return ent:getBoneName(i) end)
                local okPar, bPar = pcall(function() return ent:getBoneParent(i) end)
                local name = (okName and bName and bName ~= "") and bName or ("bone_" .. i)
                if name ~= "__INVALIDBONE__" then
                    list[#list + 1] = {
                        index = i,
                        name = name,
                        parent = okPar and bPar or -1,
                    }
                end
            end
        end
        entry._cachedBoneEnt = ent
        entry._cachedBoneList = list
        return list
    end

    function SceneRuntime:getNodeMaterials(nodeId)
        local entry = self._entries[nodeId]
        local ent = entry and entry.holo
        if not ent or not isValid(ent) or type(ent.getMaterials) ~= "function" then return {} end
        if entry._cachedMats and entry._cachedMatsModel == entry.model then
            return entry._cachedMats
        end
        local ok, mats = pcall(function() return ent:getMaterials() end)
        if ok and type(mats) == "table" then
            entry._cachedMats = mats
            entry._cachedMatsModel = entry.model
            return mats
        end
        return {}
    end

    function SceneRuntime:linkWorldEntity(nodeId, ent)
        local entry = self._entries[nodeId]
        if not entry then
            local node = self._scene:getNode(nodeId)
            if not node then return false end
            entry = { kind = node.kind }
            self._entries[nodeId] = entry
        end
        entry.linkedEnt = ent
        entry._cachedBoneEnt = nil
        entry._cachedBoneList = nil
        self:markDirty(nodeId)
        return true
    end

    function SceneRuntime:_syncNode(node)
        local id = node.id
        local kind = node.kind
        local entry = self._entries[id]

        local wPos, wAng, wScale = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(self._scene, id)
        local vis = SOLACE.Math3D.isNodeHierarchyVisible(self._scene, id)

        if kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL then
            local modelPath = node.appearance.model or ""
            if modelPath == "" then
                if entry and entry.linkedEnt and isValid(entry.linkedEnt) then
                    entry.worldPos = entry.linkedEnt:getPos()
                    entry.worldAng = entry.linkedEnt:getAngles()
                    entry.worldScale = wScale
                    entry.visible = vis
                    entry.boundsMin = entry.linkedEnt:obbMins() or DEFAULT_MINS
                    entry.boundsMax = entry.linkedEnt:obbMaxs() or DEFAULT_MAXS
                    applyBoneOverrides(entry.linkedEnt, node, entry)
                end
                return
            end

            if not entry then
                entry = { kind = kind }
                self._entries[id] = entry
            end

            entry.worldPos = wPos
            entry.worldAng = wAng
            entry.worldScale = wScale
            entry.visible = vis

            -- Create or recreate hologram if missing/invalid
            if not entry.holo or not isValid(entry.holo) then
                if not hasPermission("hologram.create") then
                    entry.errorMsg = "Missing hologram.create permission"
                    self._lastError = entry.errorMsg
                    return
                end
                if not hologram.canSpawn() then
                    entry.errorMsg = "Hologram limit/burst reached"
                    self._lastError = entry.errorMsg
                    return
                end
                if not game.modelExists(modelPath) then
                    entry.errorMsg = "Model not found: " .. modelPath
                    self._lastError = entry.errorMsg
                    return
                end

                local ok, holo = pcall(hologram.create, wPos, wAng, modelPath, wScale)
                if not ok or not isValid(holo) then
                    entry.errorMsg = "Failed to spawn hologram: " .. tostring(holo)
                    self._lastError = entry.errorMsg
                    return
                end

                entry.holo = holo
                entry.model = modelPath
                entry.errorMsg = nil
                entry._cachedBoneEnt = nil
                entry._cachedBoneList = nil
                entry._cachedMats = nil
                entry.boundsMin = holo:obbMins() or DEFAULT_MINS
                entry.boundsMax = holo:obbMaxs() or DEFAULT_MAXS
            else
                if entry.model ~= modelPath and game.modelExists(modelPath) then
                    pcall(function() entry.holo:setModel(modelPath) end)
                    entry.model = modelPath
                    entry._cachedBoneEnt = nil
                    entry._cachedBoneList = nil
                    entry._cachedMats = nil
                    entry.boundsMin = entry.holo:obbMins() or DEFAULT_MINS
                    entry.boundsMax = entry.holo:obbMaxs() or DEFAULT_MAXS
                end
                entry.holo:setPos(wPos)
                entry.holo:setAngles(wAng)
                entry.holo:setScale(wScale)
            end

            local holo = entry.holo
            if isValid(holo) then
                local okVis, errVis = pcall(function() holo:setNoDraw(not vis) end)
                local okCol, errCol = pcall(function() holo:setColor(IR.toColor(node.appearance.color)) end)
                local desiredMat = node.appearance.material or ""
                if (entry.appliedMat or "") ~= desiredMat then
                    local okMat, errMat = pcall(function() holo:setMaterial(desiredMat) end)
                    if okMat then
                        entry.appliedMat = desiredMat
                        if entry.errorMsg and string.sub(entry.errorMsg, 1, 9) == "Material " then
                            entry.errorMsg = nil
                        end
                    else
                        entry.errorMsg = "Material error: " .. tostring(errMat)
                        self._lastError = entry.errorMsg
                    end
                end

                -- Phase 7 extended hologram properties
                if hasPermission("entities.setRenderProperty") then
                    local rm = node.appearance.renderMode or 0
                    local rfx = node.appearance.renderFX or 0
                    if entry.appliedRM ~= rm and type(holo.setRenderMode) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() holo:setRenderMode(rm) end)
                        entry.appliedRM = rm
                    end
                    if entry.appliedRFX ~= rfx and type(holo.setRenderFX) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() holo:setRenderFX(rfx) end)
                        entry.appliedRFX = rfx
                    end
                    local cm = node.appearance.cullMode or 0
                    if entry.appliedCM ~= cm and type(holo.setCullMode) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() holo:setCullMode(cm) end)
                        entry.appliedCM = cm
                    end
                    local sl = node.appearance.suppressLighting == true
                    if entry.appliedSL ~= sl and type(holo.suppressEngineLighting) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() holo:suppressEngineLighting(sl) end)
                        entry.appliedSL = sl
                    end
                    local subs = node.appearance.submaterials
                    if type(subs) == "table" and type(holo.setSubMaterial) == "function" then
                        entry.appliedSubs = entry.appliedSubs or {}
                        for sIdx, sMat in pairs(subs) do
                            local idxNum = tonumber(sIdx)
                            if idxNum and entry.appliedSubs[idxNum] ~= sMat then
                                pcall(function() holo:setSubMaterial(idxNum, sMat or "") end)
                                entry.appliedSubs[idxNum] = sMat
                            end
                        end
                        for sIdx in pairs(entry.appliedSubs) do
                            if subs[sIdx] == nil then
                                pcall(function() holo:setSubMaterial(sIdx, "") end)
                                entry.appliedSubs[sIdx] = nil
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                local anim = node.appearance.holoAnim or ""
                local rate = node.appearance.holoAnimRate or 1.0
                if (entry.appliedAnim or "") ~= anim or entry.appliedAnimRate ~= rate then
                    if type(holo.setAnimation) == "function" and anim ~= "" then
                        pcall(function() holo:setAnimation(anim, 0, rate) end)
                    end
                    entry.appliedAnim = anim
                    entry.appliedAnimRate = rate
                end

                applyBoneOverrides(holo, node, entry)

                if not okVis or not okCol then
                    entry.errorMsg = "RenderProp error: " .. tostring(errVis or errCol)
                    self._lastError = entry.errorMsg
                end
            end
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE then
            if not entry then
                entry = { kind = kind }
                self._entries[id] = entry
            end
            entry.worldPos = wPos
            entry.worldAng = wAng
            entry.worldScale = wScale
            entry.visible = vis
            local spr = node.sprite or { width = 32, height = 32 }
            local hw = (spr.width or 32) * 0.5
            local hh = (spr.height or 32) * 0.5
            entry.boundsMin = Vector(-2, -hw, -hh)
            entry.boundsMax = Vector(2, hw, hh)
            local matPath = node.appearance.material or "sprites/light_glow02_add"
            if entry.matPath ~= matPath then
                entry.matPath = matPath
                entry.material = self:_loadMaterialCached(matPath)
            end
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT then
            if not entry then
                entry = { kind = kind, softParticles = {}, emitAccum = 0 }
                self._entries[id] = entry
            end
            entry.worldPos = wPos
            entry.worldAng = wAng
            entry.worldScale = wScale
            entry.visible = vis
            entry.boundsMin = Vector(-10, -10, -10)
            entry.boundsMax = Vector(10, 10, 10)
            local matPath = node.appearance.material or "sprites/light_glow02_add"
            if entry.matPath ~= matPath then
                entry.matPath = matPath
                entry.material = self:_loadMaterialCached(matPath)
            end
        elseif kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA then
            if not entry then
                entry = { kind = kind }
                self._entries[id] = entry
            end
            entry.worldPos = wPos
            entry.worldAng = wAng
            entry.worldScale = wScale
            entry.visible = vis
            entry.boundsMin = DEFAULT_MINS
            entry.boundsMax = DEFAULT_MAXS
        end
    end

    -- Must ONLY be called outside render hooks (e.g. inside Think)
    function SceneRuntime:flush()
        if #self._pendingRemove > 0 then
            for i = 1, #self._pendingRemove do
                self:_destroyEntry(self._pendingRemove[i])
            end
            self._pendingRemove = {}
        end

        if next(self._dirtyNodes) ~= nil then
            local batch = {}
            for id in pairs(self._dirtyNodes) do
                batch[#batch + 1] = id
            end
            self._dirtyNodes = {}

            for _, id in ipairs(batch) do
                local node = self._scene:getNode(id)
                if node then
                    self:_syncNode(node)
                else
                    self:_destroyEntry(id)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Returns world transform + scaled bounds for raycasting, outlines & camera focus
    function SceneRuntime:getNodeBounds(nodeId)
        local node = self._scene:getNode(nodeId)
        if not node then return nil end

        local entry = self._entries[nodeId]
        local pos, ang, scale
        if entry and entry.worldPos and not self._dirtyNodes[nodeId] then
            pos, ang, scale = entry.worldPos, entry.worldAng, entry.worldScale
        else
            pos, ang, scale = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(self._scene, nodeId)
        end

        local rawMin = (entry and entry.boundsMin) or DEFAULT_MINS
        local rawMax = (entry and entry.boundsMax) or DEFAULT_MAXS
        local sMin, sMax = SOLACE.Math3D.getScaledBounds(rawMin, rawMax, scale)
        return pos, ang, sMin, sMax, scale, entry
    end

    local function evalSpriteAlphaScale(spr, t)
        local lt = spr.lifetime or 0
        if lt <= 0 then return 1.0, true end
        local age = t - (spr.spawnTime or 0)
        if age < 0 or age > lt then return 0, false end
        local a = 1.0
        local fi = spr.fadeIn or 0
        local fo = spr.fadeOut or 0
        if fi > 0 and age < fi then
            a = math.min(a, math.clamp(age / fi, 0, 1))
        end
        if fo > 0 and age > (lt - fo) then
            a = math.min(a, math.clamp((lt - age) / fo, 0, 1))
        end
        return a, true
    end

    function SceneRuntime:stepVFX(state, dt)
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        local t = (state.timeline and state.timeline.currentTime) or 0
        for id, entry in pairs(self._entries) do
            if entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE or entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT then
                local node = self._scene:getNode(id)
                if not node or not entry.visible then
                    if entry.dynLight then
                        pcall(function() entry.dynLight:destroy() end)
                        entry.dynLight = nil
                    end
                else
                    local fx = node.effect or IR.createEffectPayload()
                    local mode = (node.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT) and "light" or (fx.mode or "particles")
                    local delay = fx.delay or 0
                    local dur = fx.duration or 0
                    local active = (t >= delay) and (dur <= 0 or t <= (delay + dur))

                    if mode == "light" then
                        if active and hasPermission("light.create") and light and light.create then
                            local lSize = (node.light and node.light.size) or fx.lightSize or 200
                            local lBright = (node.light and node.light.brightness) or fx.lightBrightness or 2
                            local c = node.appearance.color or { 255, 220, 165, 255 }
                            local col = IR.toColor(c)
                            if not entry.dynLight then
                                local ok, lObj = pcall(light.create, entry.worldPos, lSize, lBright, col)
                                if ok and lObj then entry.dynLight = lObj end
                            end
                            if entry.dynLight then
                                pcall(function()
                                    entry.dynLight:setPos(entry.worldPos)
                                    entry.dynLight:setSize(lSize)
                                    entry.dynLight:setBrightness(lBright)
                                    entry.dynLight:setColor(col)
                                    entry.dynLight:setDieTime(timer.curtime() + 0.1)
                                    entry.dynLight:draw()
                                end)
                            end
                        elseif entry.dynLight then
                            pcall(function() entry.dynLight:destroy() end)
                            entry.dynLight = nil
                        end
                    elseif mode == "particles" then
                        -- Step fallback billboard particles (always works regardless of particle.create quota)
                        local spList = entry.softParticles
                        if not spList then
                            spList = {}
                            entry.softParticles = spList
                        end
                        local grav = fx.gravity or -120
                        for i = #spList, 1, -1 do
                            local p = spList[i]
                            p.age = p.age + dt
                            if p.age >= p.life then
                                table.remove(spList, i)
                            else
                                p.vel[3] = p.vel[3] + grav * dt
                                p.pos[1] = p.pos[1] + p.vel[1] * dt
                                p.pos[2] = p.pos[2] + p.vel[2] * dt
                                p.pos[3] = p.pos[3] + p.vel[3] * dt
                            end
                        end

                        if active then
                            local rate = math.clamp(fx.rate or 12, 0.5, 40)
                            entry.emitAccum = (entry.emitAccum or 0) + dt * rate
                            local spawnCnt = math.min(4, math.floor(entry.emitAccum))
                            if spawnCnt > 0 then
                                entry.emitAccum = entry.emitAccum - spawnCnt
                                local wp = entry.worldPos or Vector(0, 0, 0)
                                local dir = entry.worldAng and entry.worldAng:getForward() or Vector(0, 0, 1)
                                local spd = fx.speed or 60
                                local spr = (fx.spread or 25) * 0.02
                                local life = math.clamp(fx.lifetime or 1.0, 0.1, 8.0)

                                -- Use native StarfallEx ParticleEmitter when permitted & material loaded; mirror into softParticles fallback otherwise
                                local usedNative = false
                                if hasPermission("particle.create") and particle and particle.create and entry.material then
                                    if not entry.emitter or (entry.emitter.isValid and not entry.emitter:isValid()) then
                                        if not particle.particleEmittersLeft or particle.particleEmittersLeft() > 0 then
                                            local okEm, em = pcall(particle.create, wp, false)
                                            if okEm and em then entry.emitter = em end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if entry.emitter and (not entry.emitter.isValid or entry.emitter:isValid()) then
                                        pcall(function() entry.emitter:setPos(wp) end)
                                        local col = IR.toColor(node.appearance.color)
                                        for _ = 1, spawnCnt do
                                            if not entry.emitter.getParticlesLeft or entry.emitter:getParticlesLeft() > 0 then
                                                local okP, pt = pcall(function()
                                                    return entry.emitter:add(entry.material, wp, fx.startSize or 6, fx.endSize or 0, 0, 0, fx.startAlpha or 220, fx.endAlpha or 0, life)
                                                end)
                                                if okP and pt then
                                                    local vx = (dir[1] + (math.random() - 0.5) * spr) * spd
                                                    local vy = (dir[2] + (math.random() - 0.5) * spr) * spd
                                                    local vz = (dir[3] + (math.random() - 0.5) * spr) * spd + spd * 0.35
                                                    pcall(function()
                                                        pt:setVelocity(Vector(vx, vy, vz))
                                                        pt:setGravity(Vector(0, 0, grav))
                                                        pt:setColor(col)
                                                    end)
                                                    usedNative = true
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if not usedNative and #spList < 32 then
                                    for _ = 1, spawnCnt do
                                        if #spList >= 32 then break end
                                        spList[#spList + 1] = {
                                            pos = Vector(wp[1], wp[2], wp[3]),
                                            vel = {
                                                (dir[1] + (math.random() - 0.5) * spr) * spd,
                                                (dir[2] + (math.random() - 0.5) * spr) * spd,
                                                (dir[3] + (math.random() - 0.5) * spr) * spd + spd * 0.35,
                                            },
                                            age = 0,
                                            life = life,
                                        }
                                    end
