                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function SceneRuntime:triggerVFXBurst(nodeId, count)
        local entry = self._entries[nodeId]
        local node = self._scene:getNode(nodeId)
        if not entry or not node or not entry.worldPos then return end
        local fx = node.effect or IR.createEffectPayload()
        entry.softParticles = entry.softParticles or {}
        local n = math.min(count or 10, 24)
        local wp = entry.worldPos
        local spd = fx.speed or 70
        local life = math.clamp(fx.lifetime or 1.0, 0.1, 6.0)
        for _ = 1, n do
            if #entry.softParticles >= 48 then break end
            entry.softParticles[#entry.softParticles + 1] = {
                pos = Vector(wp[1], wp[2], wp[3]),
                vel = { (math.random() - 0.5) * spd * 2, (math.random() - 0.5) * spd * 2, math.random() * spd * 1.5 },
                age = 0,
                life = life,
            }
        end
    end

    function SceneRuntime:drawVFX(state)
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        local t = (state.timeline and state.timeline.currentTime) or 0
        for id, entry in pairs(self._entries) do
            if (entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE or entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT) and entry.visible then
                local node = self._scene:getNode(id)
                if node and entry.worldPos then
                    local fx = node.effect or IR.createEffectPayload()
                    local mode = (node.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT) and "light" or (fx.mode or "particles")
                    local delay = fx.delay or 0
                    local dur = fx.duration or 0
                    local active = (t >= delay) and (dur <= 0 or t <= (delay + dur))
                    local c = node.appearance.color or { 255, 200, 90, 255 }

                    if mode == "beam" and active then
                        local len = (fx.beamLen or 64) * (entry.worldScale and entry.worldScale[1] or 1)
                        local endPos = entry.worldPos + (entry.worldAng and entry.worldAng:getForward() or Vector(1, 0, 0)) * len
                        local w = fx.startSize or 8
                        render.setMaterial(entry.material)
                        render.setColor(Color(c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, fx.startAlpha or c[4] or 255))
                        render.draw3DBeam(entry.worldPos, endPos, w, 0, 1)
                    elseif mode == "orb" and active then
                        local phase = math.sin(timer.realtime() * 4.0) * 0.5 + 0.5
                        local rad = math.lerp(phase, fx.startSize or 12, fx.endSize or 24) * (entry.worldScale and entry.worldScale[1] or 1)
                        local al = math.lerp(phase, fx.startAlpha or 220, fx.endAlpha or 80)
                        render.setMaterial(entry.material)
                        render.setColor(Color(c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, math.clamp(al, 10, 255)))
                        if render.draw3DSphere then
                            render.draw3DSphere(entry.worldPos, math.max(1, rad * 0.5), 14, 14)
                        else
                            render.draw3DSprite(entry.worldPos, rad, rad, Color(c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, math.clamp(al, 10, 255)))
                        end
                    elseif mode == "light" and active then
                        render.setMaterial(entry.material)
                        render.draw3DSprite(entry.worldPos, 16, 16, Color(c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, 220))
                    end

                    -- Draw soft fallback particles if populated
                    if entry.softParticles and #entry.softParticles > 0 then
                        render.setMaterial(entry.material)
                        for i = 1, #entry.softParticles do
                            local p = entry.softParticles[i]
                            local frac = math.clamp(p.age / math.max(0.01, p.life), 0, 1)
                            local sz = math.max(1, math.lerp(frac, fx.startSize or 6, fx.endSize or 0))
                            local al = math.clamp(math.lerp(frac, fx.startAlpha or 220, fx.endAlpha or 0), 0, 255)
                            render.draw3DSprite(p.pos, sz, sz, Color(c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, al))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        render.setMaterial(nil)
    end

    -- Draw translucent 3D sprites in PostDrawTranslucentRenderables
    function SceneRuntime:drawSprites(state, camPos)
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        local t = (state.timeline and state.timeline.currentTime) or 0
        for id, entry in pairs(self._entries) do
            if entry.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE and entry.visible then
                local node = self._scene:getNode(id)
                if node and entry.worldPos then
                    local spr = node.sprite or { width = 32, height = 32 }
                    local alphaMult, alive = evalSpriteAlphaScale(spr, t)
                    if alive and alphaMult > 0.005 then
                        local w = (spr.width or 32) * (entry.worldScale and entry.worldScale[1] or 1)
                        local h = (spr.height or 32) * (entry.worldScale and entry.worldScale[2] or 1)
                        local nc = node.appearance.color or { 255, 255, 255, 255 }
                        local col = Color(nc[1] or 255, nc[2] or 255, nc[3] or 255, math.clamp(math.floor((nc[4] or 255) * alphaMult), 0, 255))

                        local roll = spr.roll or 0
                        if entry.material then
                            render.setMaterial(entry.material)
                            if math.abs(roll) > 0.05 and render.draw3DQuadEasy and camPos then
                                local norm = (camPos - entry.worldPos):getNormalized()
                                render.setColor(col)
                                render.draw3DQuadEasy(entry.worldPos, norm, w, h, roll)
                            else
                                render.draw3DSprite(entry.worldPos, w, h, col)
                            end
                        else
                            render.setMaterial(nil)
                            render.setColor(col)
                            render.draw3DSprite(entry.worldPos, w * 0.45, h * 0.45, col)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        render.setMaterial(nil)
    end

    function SceneRuntime:cleanup()
        for id in pairs(self._entries) do
            self:_destroyEntry(id)
        end
        self._entries = {}
        self._dirtyNodes = {}
        self._pendingRemove = {}
    end


    SOLACE.SceneRuntime = SceneRuntime
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 9: Math and transformations
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants

    local Math3D = {}

    -- Compose local matrix: T(pos) * R(ang) * T(pivot) * S(scale) * T(-pivot)
    function Math3D.composeLocalMatrix(t)
        local m = Matrix()
        local pos = t.pos
        local ang = t.ang
        local scale = t.scale
        local pivot = t.pivot

        m:translate(Vector(pos[1] or 0, pos[2] or 0, pos[3] or 0))
        m:rotate(Angle(ang[1] or 0, ang[2] or 0, ang[3] or 0))

        local px, py, pz = pivot[1] or 0, pivot[2] or 0, pivot[3] or 0
        local hasPivot = (px ~= 0 or py ~= 0 or pz ~= 0)
        if hasPivot then
            m:translate(Vector(px, py, pz))
        end

        local sx, sy, sz = scale[1] or 1, scale[2] or 1, scale[3] or 1
        if sx ~= 1 or sy ~= 1 or sz ~= 1 then
            m:scale(Vector(sx, sy, sz))
        end

        if hasPivot then
            m:translate(Vector(-px, -py, -pz))
        end

        return m
    end

    -- Walk parent chain to compute world transform of a Scene IR node.
    -- Returns: worldPos (Vector), worldAng (Angle), worldScale (Vector)
    function Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local chain = {}
        local cur = sceneGraph:getNode(nodeId)
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local guard = 0

        while cur and cur.id ~= rootId and guard < 64 do
            chain[#chain + 1] = cur
            cur = cur.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(cur.parentId) or nil
            guard = guard + 1
        end

        if #chain == 0 then
            return Vector(0, 0, 0), Angle(0, 0, 0), Vector(1, 1, 1)
        end

        local worldMat = Matrix()
        local sx, sy, sz = 1, 1, 1
        for i = #chain, 1, -1 do
            local n = chain[i]
            local t = n.transform
            -- Compose translation/rotation hierarchy without baking scale into rotation matrix
            local step = Matrix()
            step:translate(Vector(t.pos[1] or 0, t.pos[2] or 0, t.pos[3] or 0))
            step:rotate(Angle(t.ang[1] or 0, t.ang[2] or 0, t.ang[3] or 0))
            worldMat = worldMat * step

            local ns = t.scale
            sx = sx * (ns[1] or 1)
            sy = sy * (ns[2] or 1)
            sz = sz * (ns[3] or 1)
        end

        return worldMat:getTranslation(), worldMat:getAngles(), Vector(sx, sy, sz)
    end

    -- Compute hierarchical visibility (false if any ancestor is hidden)
    function Math3D.isNodeHierarchyVisible(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local cur = sceneGraph:getNode(nodeId)
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local guard = 0
        while cur and guard < 64 do
            if cur.visible == false then return false end
            if cur.id == rootId then break end
            cur = cur.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(cur.parentId) or nil
            guard = guard + 1
        end
        return true
    end

    -- Scale local OBB mins/maxs safely (preserving min <= max even for negative scales)
    function Math3D.getScaledBounds(mins, maxs, scale)
        local sx, sy, sz = scale[1] or 1, scale[2] or 1, scale[3] or 1
        local x1, x2 = (mins[1] or -8) * sx, (maxs[1] or 8) * sx
        local y1, y2 = (mins[2] or -8) * sy, (maxs[2] or 8) * sy
        local z1, z2 = (mins[3] or -8) * sz, (maxs[3] or 8) * sz

        return Vector(math.min(x1, x2), math.min(y1, y2), math.min(z1, z2)),
               Vector(math.max(x1, x2), math.max(y1, y2), math.max(z1, z2))
    end

    -- World center & approximate bounding radius of an OBB
    function Math3D.getOBBCenterAndRadius(pos, ang, sMin, sMax)
        local localCenter = (sMin + sMax) * 0.5
        local halfExt = (sMax - sMin) * 0.5
        local radius = math.max(8, halfExt:getLength())

        local m = Matrix()
        m:translate(pos)
        m:rotate(ang)
        m:translate(localCenter)
        return m:getTranslation(), radius
    end

    -- Returns inverse world rotation Matrix of node's parent chain (nil when parent is root)
    function Math3D.getParentInverseRotation(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(nodeId)
        if not node then return nil end
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local chain = {}
        local cur = node.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(node.parentId) or nil
        local guard = 0
        while cur and cur.id ~= rootId and guard < 64 do
            chain[#chain + 1] = cur
            cur = cur.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(cur.parentId) or nil
            guard = guard + 1
        end
        if #chain == 0 then return nil end

        local rotMat = Matrix()
        for i = #chain, 1, -1 do
            local a = chain[i].transform.ang
            rotMat:rotate(Angle(a[1] or 0, a[2] or 0, a[3] or 0))
        end
        return rotMat:getInverseTR()
    end

    function Math3D.worldDeltaToParentLocal(parentInvRot, worldDelta)
        if not parentInvRot then return worldDelta end
        return parentInvRot * worldDelta
    end

    function Math3D.worldAngleToParentLocal(parentInvRot, worldAng)
        if not parentInvRot then
            return { worldAng[1], worldAng[2], worldAng[3] }
        end
        local m = Matrix()
        m:setAngles(worldAng)
        local localAng = (parentInvRot * m):getAngles()
        return { localAng[1], localAng[2], localAng[3] }
    end

    -- Converts a world-space transform (Vector, Angle, Vector) into local { pos, ang, scale } under parentId
    function Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, parentId, worldPos, worldAng, worldScale)
        local minS = (SOLACE.Constants and SOLACE.Constants.MIN_SCALE) or 0.05
        local maxS = (SOLACE.Constants and SOLACE.Constants.MAX_SCALE) or 100.0
        local ws = worldScale or Vector(1, 1, 1)
        local wa = worldAng or Angle(0, 0, 0)
        local wp = worldPos or Vector(0, 0, 0)

        local rootId = sceneGraph and sceneGraph:getRootId() or "root"
        if not parentId or parentId == rootId then
            return { math.round(wp[1], 3), math.round(wp[2], 3), math.round(wp[3], 3) },
                   { math.round(math.normalizeAngle(wa[1]), 2), math.round(math.normalizeAngle(wa[2]), 2), math.round(math.normalizeAngle(wa[3]), 2) },
                   { math.clamp(math.round(ws[1], 3), minS, maxS), math.clamp(math.round(ws[2], 3), minS, maxS), math.clamp(math.round(ws[3], 3), minS, maxS) }
        end

        local pPos, pAng, pScale = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, parentId)
        local pMat = Matrix()
        pMat:translate(pPos)
        pMat:rotate(pAng)
        local pInv = pMat:getInverseTR()

        local lPos = pInv * wp
        local mWorld = Matrix()
        mWorld:setAngles(wa)
        local lAng = (pInv * mWorld):getAngles()

        local function safeDiv(a, b)
            if math.abs(b or 1) < 1e-4 then return a end
            return a / b
        end

        return { math.round(lPos[1], 3), math.round(lPos[2], 3), math.round(lPos[3], 3) },
               { math.round(math.normalizeAngle(lAng[1]), 2), math.round(math.normalizeAngle(lAng[2]), 2), math.round(math.normalizeAngle(lAng[3]), 2) },
               {
                   math.clamp(math.round(safeDiv(ws[1], pScale[1]), 3), minS, maxS),
                   math.clamp(math.round(safeDiv(ws[2], pScale[2]), 3), minS, maxS),
                   math.clamp(math.round(safeDiv(ws[3], pScale[3]), 3), minS, maxS),
               }
    end

    -- Local orthonormal gizmo basis in world space (Source: +X Forward, +Y Left/-Right, +Z Up)
    function Math3D.getGizmoAxes(worldAng)
        if not worldAng then
            return Vector(1, 0, 0), Vector(0, 1, 0), Vector(0, 0, 1)
        end
        return worldAng:getForward(), -worldAng:getRight(), worldAng:getUp()
    end

    -- Closest distance between infinite ray R(t)=rayOrigin+rayDir*t (t>=0) and segment [segA, segB]
    -- Returns: dist, rayT, segU, segClosestPoint
    function Math3D.closestRayToSegment(rayOrigin, rayDir, segA, segB)
        local ux, uy, uz = rayDir[1], rayDir[2], rayDir[3]
        local vx, vy, vz = segB[1] - segA[1], segB[2] - segA[2], segB[3] - segA[3]
        local wx, wy, wz = rayOrigin[1] - segA[1], rayOrigin[2] - segA[2], rayOrigin[3] - segA[3]

        local a = ux * ux + uy * uy + uz * uz
        local b = ux * vx + uy * vy + uz * vz
        local c = vx * vx + vy * vy + vz * vz
        local d = ux * wx + uy * wy + uz * wz
        local e = vx * wx + vy * wy + vz * wz
        local D = a * c - b * b

        local sc, sN, sD = 0, 0, D
        local tc, tN, tD = 0, 0, D

        if D < 1e-6 then
            sN = 0
            sD = 1
            tN = e
            tD = c
        else
            sN = (b * e - c * d)
            tN = (a * e - b * d)
            if sN < 0 then
                sN = 0
                tN = e
                tD = c
            end
        end

        if tN < 0 then
            tN = 0
            if -d < 0 then
                sN = 0
            else
                sN = -d
                sD = a
            end
        elseif tN > tD then
            tN = tD
            if (-d + b) < 0 then
                sN = 0
            else
                sN = (-d + b)
                sD = a
            end
        end

        sc = (math.abs(sN) < 1e-6) and 0 or (sN / sD)
        tc = (math.abs(tN) < 1e-6) and 0 or (tN / tD)
        if sc < 0 then sc = 0 end
        tc = math.clamp(tc, 0, 1)

        local dx = wx + sc * ux - tc * vx
        local dy = wy + sc * uy - tc * vy
        local dz = wz + sc * uz - tc * vz
        local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
        local segPt = Vector(segA[1] + tc * vx, segA[2] + tc * vy, segA[3] + tc * vz)
        return dist, sc, tc, segPt
    end

    -- Closest distance between ray R(t)=rayOrigin+rayDir*t (t>=0) and 3D point pt
    function Math3D.closestRayToPoint(rayOrigin, rayDir, pt)
        local px, py, pz = pt[1] - rayOrigin[1], pt[2] - rayOrigin[2], pt[3] - rayOrigin[3]
        local t = px * rayDir[1] + py * rayDir[2] + pz * rayDir[3]
        if t < 0 then t = 0 end
        local dx = rayOrigin[1] + rayDir[1] * t - pt[1]
        local dy = rayOrigin[2] + rayDir[2] * t - pt[2]
        local dz = rayOrigin[3] + rayDir[3] * t - pt[3]
        return math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz), t
    end

    -- Phase 5: Pivot world pose (worldPivotPos, worldPivotAng) from node transform.pivot + transform.pivotAng
    function Math3D.getPivotWorldPose(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local wPos, wAng, wScale = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(nodeId)
        if not node or not node.transform then
            return wPos, wAng, wScale
        end
        local pv = node.transform.pivot or { 0, 0, 0 }
        local pa = node.transform.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }
        local m = Matrix()
        m:translate(wPos)
        m:rotate(wAng)
        m:translate(Vector(pv[1] or 0, pv[2] or 0, pv[3] or 0))
        m:rotate(Angle(pa[1] or 0, pa[2] or 0, pa[3] or 0))
        return m:getTranslation(), m:getAngles(), wScale
    end

    -- Convert a world-space point into a node's own local coordinates (relative to node origin)
    function Math3D.worldPointToNodeLocal(sceneGraph, nodeId, worldPt)
        local wPos, wAng = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local m = Matrix()
        m:translate(wPos)
        m:rotate(wAng)
        return m:getInverseTR() * worldPt
    end

    -- Convert a world-space angle into a node's local pivot orientation (relative to node worldAng)
    function Math3D.worldAngleToNodeLocal(sceneGraph, nodeId, worldAng)
        local _, wAng = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local m = Matrix()
        m:setAngles(wAng)
        local mWorld = Matrix()
        mWorld:setAngles(worldAng)
        return (m:getInverseTR() * mWorld):getAngles()
    end

    -- Rotate a world point around a world pivot along axisDir by deg (Rodrigues formula — zero VMatrix quirks)
    function Math3D.rotatePointAroundAxis(pt, pivotPt, axisDir, deg)
        if math.abs(deg) < 1e-6 then return pt end
        local rad = math.rad(deg)
        local c = math.cos(rad)
        local s = math.sin(rad)
        local p = pt - pivotPt
        local k = axisDir:getNormalized()
        local kDotP = k:dot(p)
        local kCrossP = k:cross(p)
        local rotated = p * c + kCrossP * s + k * (kDotP * (1 - c))
        return pivotPt + rotated
    end

    -- Compute local-space center of a node's geometry bounds (or combined descendant bounds for Groups/Folders)
    function Math3D.computeLocalBoundsCenter(sceneGraph, runtime, nodeId)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(nodeId)
        if not node then return { 0, 0, 0 } end
        if node.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP and node.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER then
            local _, _, sMin, sMax = runtime:getNodeBounds(nodeId)
            if sMin and sMax then
                local c = (sMin + sMax) * 0.5
                return { math.round(c[1], 3), math.round(c[2], 3), math.round(c[3], 3) }
            end
            return { 0, 0, 0 }
        end

        -- Aggregate child centers in node's local frame
        local wPos, wAng = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, nodeId)
        local m = Matrix()
        m:translate(wPos)
        m:rotate(wAng)
        local inv = m:getInverseTR()

        local sum = Vector(0, 0, 0)
        local count = 0
        local function visit(cid)
            local cn = sceneGraph:getNode(cid)
            if not cn then return end
            if cn.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP and cn.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER then
                local cp, ca, cMin, cMax = runtime:getNodeBounds(cid)
                if cp and cMin and cMax then
                    local worldC = Math3D.getOBBCenterAndRadius(cp, ca, cMin, cMax)
                    sum = sum + (inv * worldC)
                    count = count + 1
                end
            end
            for i = 1, #(cn.children or {}) do
                visit(cn.children[i])
            end
        end
        for i = 1, #(node.children or {}) do
            visit(node.children[i])
        end
        if count == 0 then return { 0, 0, 0 } end
        local avg = sum / count
        return { math.round(avg[1], 3), math.round(avg[2], 3), math.round(avg[3], 3) }
    end


    SOLACE.Math3D = Math3D
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 10: Freecam
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Math3D = SOLACE.Math3D



    local Freecam = {}
    Freecam.__index = Freecam

    function Freecam.new(state)
        return setmetatable({
            state = state,
            initialized = false,
            camPos = Vector(0, 0, 100),
            camAng = Angle(15, 0, 0),
            targetPos = Vector(0, 0, 100),
            targetAng = Angle(15, 0, 0),
            _viewOut = { origin = nil, angles = nil, fov = 80, drawviewer = true },
        }, Freecam)
    end

    function Freecam:initFromPlayer()
        local pos = eyePos()
        local ang = eyeAngles()
        -- Offset slightly up & back so user sees the studio origin/chip cleanly
        local initPos = pos + Vector(0, 0, 16)
        self.camPos = initPos:clone()
        self.targetPos = initPos:clone()
        self.camAng = Angle(ang[1], ang[2], 0)
        self.targetAng = Angle(ang[1], ang[2], 0)
        self.initialized = true
    end

    function Freecam:resetToPlayer()
        self:initFromPlayer()
    end

    function Freecam:getPos()
        if not self.initialized then self:initFromPlayer() end
        return self.camPos
    end

    function Freecam:getAng()
        if not self.initialized then self:initFromPlayer() end
        return self.camAng
    end

    function Freecam:setPose(pos, ang, immediate)
        if not pos then return end
        self.targetPos = pos:clone()
        if ang then self.targetAng = Angle(ang[1], ang[2], ang[3] or 0) end
        if immediate ~= false then
            self.camPos = self.targetPos:clone()
            if ang then self.camAng = Angle(self.targetAng[1], self.targetAng[2], self.targetAng[3]) end
        end
        self.initialized = true
    end

    function Freecam:onMouseMoved(dx, dy)
        if not self.state.studioOpen or not self.state.viewportLooking then return end
        local sens = self.state.camera.sensitivity
        self.targetAng[2] = math.normalizeAngle(self.targetAng[2] - dx * sens)
        self.targetAng[1] = math.clamp(self.targetAng[1] + dy * sens, -89.0, 89.0)
        self.targetAng[3] = 0
    end

    function Freecam:onMouseWheelInViewport(delta)
        if not self.state.studioOpen then return end
        local c = self.state.camera
        local factor = delta > 0 and 1.15 or (1 / 1.15)
        c.speed = math.clamp(math.round(c.speed * factor), Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.minSpeed, Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.maxSpeed)
    end

    function Freecam:focusNode(sceneGraph, runtime, nodeId)
        if not self.initialized then self:initFromPlayer() end
        local targetId = nodeId or sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        if not targetId or targetId == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end

        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(targetId)
        if not node then return false end

        local pos, ang, sMin, sMax = runtime:getNodeBounds(targetId)
        if not pos then return false end

        local center, radius = Math3D.getOBBCenterAndRadius(pos, ang, sMin, sMax)
        local halfFovRad = math.rad(math.clamp(self.state.camera.fov, 30, 120) * 0.5)
        local dist = math.max(55, (radius / math.max(0.15, math.tan(halfFovRad))) * Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.focusPadding)

        local fwd = self.targetAng:getForward()
        self.targetPos = center - fwd * dist
        return true
    end

    function Freecam:getSpawnTransform(distance)
        if not self.initialized then self:initFromPlayer() end
        local d = distance or self.state.spawnDistance or 140
        local p = self.camPos + self.camAng:getForward() * d
        return { math.round(p[1], 2), math.round(p[2], 2), math.round(p[3], 2) },
               { 0, math.round(self.camAng[2], 1), 0 }
    end

    function Freecam:update(dt, ui, vpRect)
        if not self.initialized then
            self:initFromPlayer()
        end

        if not self.state.studioOpen then return end
        if not hasPermission("input") then return end

        -- Adjust speed with mouse wheel when cursor is inside the viewport (or while RMB-looking)
        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and (self.state.viewportLooking or ui:pointInRect(vpRect.x, vpRect.y, vpRect.w, vpRect.h)) then
            self:onMouseWheelInViewport(ui.wheelDelta)
        end

        -- Keyboard movement (disabled while typing into any TextBox or dragging a gizmo)
        local gizmoDragging = SOLACE.Gizmos and SOLACE.Gizmos.dragging
        if not ui.focusedInputId and not gizmoDragging then
            local fwd = 0
            local right = 0
            local up = 0

            if input.isKeyDown(KEY.W) then fwd = fwd + 1 end
            if input.isKeyDown(KEY.S) then fwd = fwd - 1 end
            if input.isKeyDown(KEY.D) then right = right + 1 end
            if input.isKeyDown(KEY.A) then right = right - 1 end
            if input.isKeyDown(KEY.SPACE) then up = up + 1 end
            local ctrlDown = input.isKeyDown(KEY.LCONTROL) or input.isKeyDown(KEY.RCONTROL)
            if ctrlDown then
                local ctrlChord = (not self.state.viewportLooking)
                    and (ui.leftDown or ui.leftPressed or input.isKeyDown(KEY.Z) or input.isKeyDown(KEY.Y))
                if not ctrlChord then
                    up = up - 1
                end
            end

            if fwd ~= 0 or right ~= 0 or up ~= 0 then
                local moveDir = self.targetAng:getForward() * fwd
                    + self.targetAng:getRight() * right
                    + Vector(0, 0, up)

                local len = moveDir:getLength()
                if len > 0.001 then
                    moveDir = moveDir / len
                    local spd = self.state.camera.speed
                    if input.isShiftDown() then
                        spd = spd * self.state.camera.boostMult
                    end
                    self.targetPos = self.targetPos + moveDir * (spd * dt)
                end
            end
        end

        -- Smooth exponential interpolation (frame-rate independent)
        local smooth = self.state.camera.smoothing
        if smooth >= 40 then
            self.camPos = self.targetPos:clone()
            self.camAng = self.targetAng:clone()
        else
            local t = 1 - math.exp(-smooth * dt)
            self.camPos = math.lerpVector(t, self.camPos, self.targetPos)
            self.camAng = math.lerpAngle(t, self.camAng, self.targetAng)
            self.camAng[3] = 0
        end
    end

    function Freecam:calcView(origin, angles, fov, znear, zfar, sceneGraph)
        if not self.state.studioOpen then return nil end
        if not self.initialized then
            self:initFromPlayer()
        end
        local v = self._viewOut
        local camId = self.state.activeCameraId
        if camId and sceneGraph and SOLACE.Animation then
            local t = (self.state.timeline and self.state.timeline.currentTime) or 0
            local cPos, cAng, cFov = SOLACE.Animation.getCinematicCameraPose(sceneGraph, camId, t)
            if cPos and cAng then
                v.origin = cPos
                v.angles = cAng
                v.fov = cFov or self.state.camera.fov
                v.drawviewer = true
                return v
            else
                self.state.activeCameraId = nil
            end
        end
        v.origin = self.camPos
        v.angles = self.camAng
        v.fov = self.state.camera.fov
        v.drawviewer = true
        return v
    end


    SOLACE.Freecam = Freecam
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 11: Object selection
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants



    local Selection = {
        hoveredId = nil,
    }

    local RAY_LEN = 16384

    -- Reusable corner scratch vectors
    local p0 = Vector(0, 0, 0)
    local p1 = Vector(0, 0, 0)
    local p2 = Vector(0, 0, 0)
    local p3 = Vector(0, 0, 0)

    local function drawCornerBrackets(pos, ang, sMin, sMax, col, alpha)
        local m = Matrix()
        m:translate(pos)
        m:rotate(ang)

        local x1, y1, z1 = sMin[1], sMin[2], sMin[3]
        local x2, y2, z2 = sMax[1], sMax[2], sMax[3]

        -- Expand slightly (0.5u) to prevent Z-fighting on flat box surfaces
        local pad = 0.5
        x1, y1, z1 = x1 - pad, y1 - pad, z1 - pad
        x2, y2, z2 = x2 + pad, y2 + pad, z2 + pad

        local dx = (x2 - x1) * 0.22
        local dy = (y2 - y1) * 0.22
        local dz = (z2 - z1) * 0.22

        SOLACE.Theme.setColor(col, alpha)

        local xs = { x1, x2 }
        local ys = { y1, y2 }
        local zs = { z1, z2 }

        for ix = 1, 2 do
            local cx = xs[ix]
            local sx = (ix == 1) and dx or -dx
            for iy = 1, 2 do
                local cy = ys[iy]
                local sy = (iy == 1) and dy or -dy
                for iz = 1, 2 do
                    local cz = zs[iz]
                    local sz = (iz == 1) and dz or -dz

                    p0[1], p0[2], p0[3] = cx, cy, cz
                    p1[1], p1[2], p1[3] = cx + sx, cy, cz
                    p2[1], p2[2], p2[3] = cx, cy + sy, cz
                    p3[1], p3[2], p3[3] = cx, cy, cz + sz

                    local w0 = m * p0
                    render.draw3DLine(w0, m * p1, true)
                    render.draw3DLine(w0, m * p2, true)
                    render.draw3DLine(w0, m * p3, true)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local _lastPickKey = nil

    function Selection.pickAtCursor(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime, vpRect)
        if not state.studioOpen or state.viewportLooking then
            Selection.hoveredId = nil
            _lastPickKey = nil
            return nil
        end
        if not ui:pointInRect(vpRect.x, vpRect.y, vpRect.w, vpRect.h) then
            Selection.hoveredId = nil
            _lastPickKey = nil
            return nil
        end

        local cp, ca = camera.camPos, camera.camAng
        local key = ui.mx .. ":" .. ui.my .. ":" .. cp[1] .. ":" .. cp[2] .. ":" .. cp[3]
            .. ":" .. ca[1] .. ":" .. ca[2] .. ":" .. state.camera.fov .. ":" .. (sceneGraph._version or 0)
        if not ui.leftPressed and key == _lastPickKey then
            return Selection.hoveredId
        end
        _lastPickKey = key

        local aimDir = trace.aimVector(camera.camAng, state.camera.fov, ui.mx, ui.my, ui.scrW, ui.scrH)
        local rayDelta = aimDir * RAY_LEN

        local bestId = nil
        local bestFrac = 2.0

        for id, entry in pairs(runtime:getEntries()) do
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if node and entry.visible and not node.locked then
                local kind = node.kind
                if kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT
                    or kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA
                then
                    local pos, ang, sMin, sMax = runtime:getNodeBounds(id)
                    if pos then
                        local testAng = (kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE) and camera.camAng or ang
                        local _, _, frac = trace.intersectRayWithOBB(camera.camPos, rayDelta, pos, testAng, sMin, sMax)
                        if frac and frac < bestFrac then
                            bestFrac = frac
                            bestId = id
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        Selection.hoveredId = bestId
        return bestId
    end

    function Selection.handleClick(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime, vpRect)
        if not state.studioOpen or state.viewportLooking then return end
        if ui.activeId ~= nil then return end
        if not ui.leftPressed then return end
        if not ui:pointInRect(vpRect.x, vpRect.y, vpRect.w, vpRect.h) then return end

        -- If a HUD widget inside the viewport (none in P2, future gizmos in P3) didn't capture the click:
        local hitId = Selection.pickAtCursor(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime, vpRect)
        local ctrl = hasPermission("input") and input.isControlDown() or false

        if hitId then
            if ctrl then
                sceneGraph:toggleSelectedId(hitId)
            else
                sceneGraph:setSelectedId(hitId)
            end
        else
            if not ctrl then
                sceneGraph:clearSelection()
            end
        end
    end

    function Selection.draw3D(state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime)
        if not state.studioOpen then return end

        local primaryId = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local selectedIds = sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()

        -- Draw subtle hover box if cursor is over an unselected object
        local hovId = Selection.hoveredId
        if hovId and not sceneGraph:isSelected(hovId) then
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(hovId)
            if node then
                local pos, ang, sMin, sMax = runtime:getNodeBounds(hovId)
                if pos then
                    local testAng = (node.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE) and camera.camAng or ang
                    SOLACE.Theme.setColor(SOLACE.Theme.C.textPrimary, 95)
                    render.draw3DWireframeBox(pos, testAng, sMin, sMax, true)
                end
            end
        end

        -- Draw selected object highlights
        for _, id in ipairs(selectedIds) do
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if node and id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                local pos, ang, sMin, sMax = runtime:getNodeBounds(id)
                if pos then
                    local isPrimary = (id == primaryId)
                    local col = isPrimary and SOLACE.Theme.C.accentBright or SOLACE.Theme.C.accent
                    local testAng = (node.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE) and camera.camAng or ang

                    -- Subtle wireframe cage + crisp corner brackets
                    SOLACE.Theme.setColor(col, isPrimary and 120 or 80)
                    render.draw3DWireframeBox(pos, testAng, sMin, sMax, true)
                    drawCornerBrackets(pos, testAng, sMin, sMax, col, isPrimary and 255 or 190)
                end
            end
        end

        -- Draw 3D frustum gizmos for all visible Camera nodes (except when looking directly through one)
        local camNodes = sceneGraph.getNodesByKind and sceneGraph:getNodesByKind(Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA) or {}
        for i = 1, #camNodes do
            local cn = camNodes[i]
            if cn.visible ~= false and state.activeCameraId ~= cn.id then
                local cPos, cAng = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, cn.id)
                local fov = (cn.camera and cn.camera.fov) or 80
                local fwd = cAng:getForward()
                local right = cAng:getRight()
                local up = cAng:getUp()
                local d = 22
                local halfH = d * math.tan(math.rad(math.clamp(fov, 20, 130) * 0.5))
                local halfW = halfH * 1.4
                local fc = cPos + fwd * d
                local tl = fc - right * halfW + up * halfH
                local tr = fc + right * halfW + up * halfH
                local br = fc + right * halfW - up * halfH
                local bl = fc - right * halfW - up * halfH
                local isSel = sceneGraph:isSelected(cn.id)
                SOLACE.Theme.setColor(isSel and SOLACE.Theme.C.accentBright or SOLACE.Theme.C.warning, isSel and 235 or 160)
                render.draw3DLine(cPos, tl, true)
                render.draw3DLine(cPos, tr, true)
                render.draw3DLine(cPos, br, true)
                render.draw3DLine(cPos, bl, true)
                render.draw3DLine(tl, tr, true)
                render.draw3DLine(tr, br, true)
                render.draw3DLine(br, bl, true)
                render.draw3DLine(bl, tl, true)
                -- Look-at target tether
                if cn.camera and cn.camera.lookAtNodeId then
                    local tNode = sceneGraph:getNode(cn.camera.lookAtNodeId)
                    if tNode then
                        local tPos = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, tNode.id)
                        SOLACE.Theme.setColor(SOLACE.Theme.C.accent, 140)
                        render.draw3DLine(cPos, tPos, true)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end


    SOLACE.Selection = Selection
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 12: Transform gizmos (Phase 3)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local IR        = SOLACE.IR
    local Math3D    = SOLACE.Math3D
    local Commands  = SOLACE.Commands

    local Gizmos = {
        ready = true,
        phase = 5,
        hoveredAxis = nil, -- "x" | "y" | "z" | "uniform" | nil
        activeAxis = nil,  -- "x" | "y" | "z" | "uniform" | nil
        dragging = false,
        dragData = nil,
    }

    local RAY_LEN = 16384
    local MIN_SCALE = 0.05
    local MAX_SCALE = 100.0
    local RING_SEGS = 32
    local COS_TABLE = {}
    local SIN_TABLE = {}
    for i = 0, RING_SEGS do
        local a = (i / RING_SEGS) * math.pi * 2
        COS_TABLE[i + 1] = math.cos(a)
        SIN_TABLE[i + 1] = math.sin(a)
    end

    local COL_HILITE  = { 255, 224, 96, 255 }
    local COL_UNIFORM = { 236, 240, 248, 255 }
    local COL_GUIDE   = { 255, 224, 96, 135 }

    local WORLD_X = Vector(1, 0, 0)
    local WORLD_Y = Vector(0, 1, 0)
    local WORLD_Z = Vector(0, 0, 1)

    local _anchorKey = nil
    local _anchorOrigin = nil
    local _anchorAng = nil
    local _lastPickKey = nil
    local _lastPickAxis = nil

    local function axisColor(axis)
        local C = SOLACE.Theme.C
        if axis == "x" then return C.axisX end
        if axis == "y" then return C.axisY end
        if axis == "z" then return C.axisZ end
        return COL_UNIFORM
    end

    local function getEffectiveTargets(sceneGraph)
        local selIds = sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
        if #selIds == 0 then return nil, nil end

        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local selMap = {}
        for i = 1, #selIds do
            local id = selIds[i]
            if id ~= rootId then
                selMap[id] = true
            end
        end

        local targets = {}
        local primaryId = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local anchorId = nil

        for i = 1, #selIds do
            local id = selIds[i]
            if id ~= rootId then
                local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
                if node and not node.locked then
                    -- Skip descendants whose ancestor is also selected (prevents double-transforming)
                    local hasSelectedAncestor = false
                    local cur = node.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(node.parentId) or nil
                    local guard = 0
                    while cur and cur.id ~= rootId and guard < 64 do
                        if selMap[cur.id] then
                            hasSelectedAncestor = true
                            break
                        end
                        cur = cur.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(cur.parentId) or nil
                        guard = guard + 1
                    end
                    if not hasSelectedAncestor then
                        targets[#targets + 1] = id
                        if id == primaryId then
                            anchorId = id
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if #targets == 0 then return nil, nil end
        if not anchorId then anchorId = targets[1] end
        return anchorId, targets
    end

    local function getEffectiveSubTool(state)
        if state.activeTool == "pivot" then
            return (state.pivotGizmoMode == "rotate") and "rotate" or "move"
        end
        return state.activeTool
    end

    local function getAnchorTransform(sceneGraph, anchorId, targets, state)
        local pMode = (state and state.pivotMode) or "custom"
        local key = anchorId .. ":" .. pMode .. ":" .. tostring(targets and #targets or 1) .. ":" .. tostring(sceneGraph._version or 0)
        if key == _anchorKey and _anchorOrigin then
            return _anchorOrigin, _anchorAng
        end
        local wPos, wAng
        if pMode == "median" and targets and #targets > 1 and state.activeTool ~= "pivot" then
            local sum = Vector(0, 0, 0)
            for i = 1, #targets do
                local p = Math3D.getPivotWorldPose(sceneGraph, targets[i])
                sum = sum + p
            end
            wPos = sum / #targets
            local _, a = Math3D.getPivotWorldPose(sceneGraph, anchorId)
            wAng = a
        else
            wPos, wAng = Math3D.getPivotWorldPose(sceneGraph, anchorId)
        end
        _anchorKey = key
        _anchorOrigin = wPos
        _anchorAng = wAng
        return wPos, wAng
    end

    local function getHandleLength(camera, origin)
        local dist = (origin - camera.camPos):getLength()
        local fov = (camera.state and camera.state.camera and camera.state.camera.fov) or 80
        local halfFov = math.rad(math.clamp(fov, 30, 120) * 0.5)
        return math.clamp(dist * math.tan(halfFov) * 0.22, 18, 220)
    end

    local function getToolAxes(tool, worldAng, state)
        local sub = (tool == "pivot" and state) and getEffectiveSubTool(state) or tool
        local space = (state and state.transformSpace) or "world"
        if sub == "scale" then
            return Math3D.getGizmoAxes(worldAng)
        end
        if space == "local" then
            return Math3D.getGizmoAxes(worldAng)
        end
        return WORLD_X, WORLD_Y, WORLD_Z
    end

    local function getAxisVector(axis, ax, ay, az)
        if axis == "x" then return ax end
        if axis == "y" then return ay end
        if axis == "z" then return az end
        return nil
    end

    local function computeDragPlaneNormal(camera, origin, axisDir)
        local camToOrigin = origin - camera.camPos
        local len = camToOrigin:getLength()
        if len < 0.001 then
            camToOrigin = camera.camAng:getForward()
        else
            camToOrigin = camToOrigin / len
        end

        local tangent = axisDir:cross(camToOrigin)
        if tangent:getLength() < 0.001 then
            local fallback = (math.abs(axisDir[3]) < 0.9) and WORLD_Z or WORLD_X
            tangent = axisDir:cross(fallback)
        end
        tangent = tangent:getNormalized()
        return tangent:cross(axisDir):getNormalized()
    end

    local function pickHandle(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, vpRect)
        local rawTool = state.activeTool
        if rawTool ~= "move" and rawTool ~= "rotate" and rawTool ~= "scale" and rawTool ~= "pivot" then
            return nil
        end
        if not state.studioOpen or state.viewportLooking then
            return nil
        end
        if not ui:pointInRect(vpRect.x, vpRect.y, vpRect.w, vpRect.h) then
            return nil
        end

        local anchorId, targets = getEffectiveTargets(sceneGraph)
        if not anchorId then return nil end
        local tool = getEffectiveSubTool(state)

        local cp, ca = camera.camPos, camera.camAng
        local key = ui.mx .. ":" .. ui.my .. ":" .. cp[1] .. ":" .. cp[2] .. ":" .. cp[3]
            .. ":" .. ca[1] .. ":" .. ca[2] .. ":" .. state.camera.fov
            .. ":" .. rawTool .. ":" .. tool .. ":" .. tostring(state.transformSpace) .. ":" .. tostring(state.pivotMode)
            .. ":" .. anchorId .. ":" .. (sceneGraph._version or 0)
        if not ui.leftPressed and key == _lastPickKey then
            return _lastPickAxis
        end
        _lastPickKey = key

        local origin, worldAng = getAnchorTransform(sceneGraph, anchorId, targets, state)
        local handleLen = getHandleLength(camera, origin)
        local ax, ay, az = getToolAxes(rawTool, worldAng, state)
        local rayDir = trace.aimVector(ca, state.camera.fov, ui.mx, ui.my, ui.scrW, ui.scrH)
        local rayOrigin = cp

        local bestAxis = nil

        if tool == "scale" then
            local ptDist = Math3D.closestRayToPoint(rayOrigin, rayDir, origin)
            if ptDist <= math.max(5.5, handleLen * 0.18) then
                _lastPickAxis = "uniform"
                return "uniform"
            end
        end

        if tool == "move" or tool == "scale" then
            local startFrac = (tool == "move") and 0.14 or 0.18
            local endFrac = (tool == "move") and 1.12 or 1.06
            local thresh = math.max(4.5, handleLen * 0.14)
            local bestDist = thresh

            local axes = { { "x", ax }, { "y", ay }, { "z", az } }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local name, dir = axes[i][1], axes[i][2]
                local segA = origin + dir * (handleLen * startFrac)
                local segB = origin + dir * (handleLen * endFrac)
                local d = Math3D.closestRayToSegment(rayOrigin, rayDir, segA, segB)
                if d <= bestDist then
                    bestDist = d
                    bestAxis = name
                end
            end
        elseif tool == "rotate" then
            local radius = handleLen * 0.90
            local thresh = math.max(4.5, handleLen * 0.13)
            local bestScore = 1e9
            local ringDefs = {
                { "x", ay, az },
                { "y", az, ax },
                { "z", ax, ay },
            }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local name, u, v = ringDefs[i][1], ringDefs[i][2], ringDefs[i][3]
                local minRingDist = thresh
                local minRingRayT = 1e9
                local prevPt = origin + u * radius
                for s = 1, RING_SEGS do
                    local c = COS_TABLE[s + 1] * radius
                    local sn = SIN_TABLE[s + 1] * radius
                    local nextPt = origin + u * c + v * sn
                    local d, rayT = Math3D.closestRayToSegment(rayOrigin, rayDir, prevPt, nextPt)
                    if d <= minRingDist then
                        if d < minRingRayT or d < minRingDist - 0.5 or rayT < minRingRayT then
                            minRingDist = d
                            minRingRayT = rayT
                        end
                    end
                    prevPt = nextPt
                end
                if minRingDist < thresh then
                    local score = minRingDist + minRingRayT * 0.015
                    if score < bestScore then
                        bestScore = score
                        bestAxis = name
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        _lastPickAxis = bestAxis
        return bestAxis
    end

    local function sameVec3(a, b)
        if not a and not b then return true end
        if not a or not b then return false end
        return math.abs((a[1] or 0) - (b[1] or 0)) <= 1e-4
            and math.abs((a[2] or 0) - (b[2] or 0)) <= 1e-4
            and math.abs((a[3] or 0) - (b[3] or 0)) <= 1e-4
    end

    local function rotateBasisAngleAroundAxis(startAng, axisDir, deg)
        if math.abs(deg) < 1e-6 then return startAng:clone() end
        local f = Math3D.rotatePointAroundAxis(startAng:getForward(), Vector(0, 0, 0), axisDir, deg)
        local r = Math3D.rotatePointAroundAxis(startAng:getRight(), Vector(0, 0, 0), axisDir, deg)
        local u = f:cross(-r):getNormalized()
        local l = u:cross(f):getNormalized()
        -- Construct rotation matrix from Forward (+X), Left (+Y), Up (+Z)
        local m = Matrix()
        if m._m then
            m._m[1][1], m._m[1][2], m._m[1][3] = f[1], l[1], u[1]
            m._m[2][1], m._m[2][2], m._m[2][3] = f[2], l[2], u[2]
            m._m[3][1], m._m[3][2], m._m[3][3] = f[3], l[3], u[3]
            return m:getAngles()
        end
        if type(startAng.rotateAroundAxis) == "function" then
            return startAng:rotateAroundAxis(axisDir, deg)
        end
        return startAng:clone()
    end

    local function beginDrag(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, axis)
        local anchorId, targets = getEffectiveTargets(sceneGraph)
        if not anchorId or not targets then return false end

        local origin, worldAng = getAnchorTransform(sceneGraph, anchorId, targets, state)
        local handleLen = getHandleLength(camera, origin)
        local rawTool = state.activeTool
        local subTool = getEffectiveSubTool(state)
        local ax, ay, az = getToolAxes(rawTool, worldAng, state)
        local axisDir = getAxisVector(axis, ax, ay, az)
        local rayDir = trace.aimVector(camera.camAng, state.camera.fov, ui.mx, ui.my, ui.scrW, ui.scrH)

        local entries = {}
        local pMode = state.pivotMode or "custom"
        for i = 1, #targets do
            local id = targets[i]
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if node and node.transform then
                local wPos, wAng = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, id)
                local pivPos, pivAng = Math3D.getPivotWorldPose(sceneGraph, id)
                local pv = node.transform.pivot or { 0, 0, 0 }
                local hasNonZeroPivot = (math.abs(pv[1] or 0) > 1e-4) or (math.abs(pv[2] or 0) > 1e-4) or (math.abs(pv[3] or 0) > 1e-4)
                entries[#entries + 1] = {
                    id = id,
                    node = node,
                    before = {
                        pos      = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pos),
                        ang      = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.ang),
                        scale    = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.scale),
                        pivot    = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pivot or { 0, 0, 0 }),
                        pivotAng = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }),
                    },
                    parentInvRot = Math3D.getParentInverseRotation(sceneGraph, id),
                    startWorldPos = wPos,
                    startWorldAng = wAng,
                    startPivotPos = pivPos,
                    startPivotAng = pivAng,
                    orbitCenter = (pMode == "median" and #targets > 1) and origin:clone() or pivPos,
                    useOrbit = hasNonZeroPivot or (pMode == "median" and #targets > 1),
                }
            end
        end
        if #entries == 0 then return false end

        local dd = {
            tool = rawTool,
            subTool = subTool,
            pivotMode = pMode,
            axis = axis,
            origin = origin:clone(),
            worldAng = worldAng:clone(),
            ax = ax,
            ay = ay,
            az = az,
            axisDir = axisDir,
            handleLen = handleLen,
            startMouseX = ui.mx,
            startMouseY = ui.my,
            entries = entries,
            feedbackText = "",
            axisVisualFactor = 1.0,
            totalDeg = 0,
        }

        if subTool == "move" or (subTool == "scale" and axis ~= "uniform") then
            local pNormal = computeDragPlaneNormal(camera, origin, axisDir)
            dd.planeNormal = pNormal
            local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, origin, pNormal)
            local validHit = hit and (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) > 0 and (hit - origin):getLength() <= 25000
            dd.startProj = validHit and (hit - origin):dot(axisDir) or 0
        elseif subTool == "rotate" then
            local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, origin, axisDir)
            local validHit = hit and (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) > 0
            if math.abs(rayDir:dot(axisDir)) < 0.16 or not validHit then
                dd.edgeOn = true
            else
                local v0 = hit - origin
                if v0:getLength() < 0.001 then
                    v0 = (math.abs(axisDir[3]) < 0.9) and WORLD_Z:cross(axisDir) or WORLD_X:cross(axisDir)
                end
                v0 = v0:getNormalized()
                dd.edgeOn = false
                dd.startVec = v0
                dd.prevVec = v0
                dd.curVec = v0
            end
        end

        Gizmos.dragging = true
        Gizmos.activeAxis = axis
        Gizmos.hoveredAxis = axis
        Gizmos.dragData = dd
        return true
    end

    local function updateDrag(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph)
        local dd = Gizmos.dragData
        if not dd then return end

        local shift = hasPermission("input") and input.isShiftDown() or false
        local rayDir = trace.aimVector(camera.camAng, state.camera.fov, ui.mx, ui.my, ui.scrW, ui.scrH)
        local rawTool = dd.tool
        local subTool = dd.subTool
        local axis = dd.axis
        local axisLabel = string.upper(axis)

        if rawTool == "pivot" and subTool == "move" then
            local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, dd.origin, dd.planeNormal)
            if not hit or (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) <= 0 or (hit - dd.origin):getLength() > 25000 then return end
            local delta = (hit - dd.origin):dot(dd.axisDir) - dd.startProj
            if shift then
                local snap = math.max(1, (state.grid and state.grid.spacing) or 32)
                delta = math.round(delta / snap) * snap
            end
            local worldDelta = dd.axisDir * delta
            for i = 1, #dd.entries do
                local e = dd.entries[i]
                local newWorldPivot = e.startPivotPos + worldDelta
                local localPivot = Math3D.worldPointToNodeLocal(sceneGraph, e.id, newWorldPivot)
                local pv = e.node.transform.pivot
                pv[1] = math.round(localPivot[1], 3)
                pv[2] = math.round(localPivot[2], 3)
                pv[3] = math.round(localPivot[3], 3)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(e.id)
            end
            dd.feedbackText = string.format("Pivot %s: %+.1f u%s", axisLabel, delta, shift and " [Snap]" or "")
            return
        elseif rawTool == "pivot" and subTool == "rotate" then
            local deltaDeg
            if dd.edgeOn then
                deltaDeg = ((ui.mx - dd.startMouseX) - (ui.my - dd.startMouseY)) * 0.6
            else
                local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, dd.origin, dd.axisDir)
                if hit and (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) > 0 then
                    local v1 = hit - dd.origin
                    if v1:getLength() > 0.001 then
                        v1 = v1:getNormalized()
                        local sinStep = dd.axisDir:dot(dd.prevVec:cross(v1))
                        local cosStep = math.clamp(dd.prevVec:dot(v1), -1, 1)
                        local stepDeg = math.deg(math.atan2(sinStep, cosStep))
                        dd.totalDeg = dd.totalDeg + stepDeg
                        dd.prevVec = v1
                        dd.curVec = v1
                    end
                end
                deltaDeg = dd.totalDeg
            end
            if shift then deltaDeg = math.round(deltaDeg / 15) * 15 end
            dd.displayDeg = deltaDeg
            for i = 1, #dd.entries do
                local e = dd.entries[i]
                local newWorldPivAng = rotateBasisAngleAroundAxis(e.startPivotAng, dd.axisDir, deltaDeg)
                local localPivAng = Math3D.worldAngleToNodeLocal(sceneGraph, e.id, newWorldPivAng)
                e.node.transform.pivotAng = e.node.transform.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }
                local pa = e.node.transform.pivotAng
                pa[1] = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(localPivAng[1]), 2)
                pa[2] = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(localPivAng[2]), 2)
                pa[3] = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(localPivAng[3]), 2)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(e.id)
            end
            dd.feedbackText = string.format("Pivot Rot %s: %+.1f deg%s", axisLabel, deltaDeg, shift and " [15 deg]" or "")
            return
        end

        if subTool == "move" then
            local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, dd.origin, dd.planeNormal)
            if not hit or (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) <= 0 or (hit - dd.origin):getLength() > 25000 then return end
            local delta = (hit - dd.origin):dot(dd.axisDir) - dd.startProj
            if shift then
                local snap = math.max(1, (state.grid and state.grid.spacing) or 32)
                delta = math.round(delta / snap) * snap
            end
            local worldDelta = dd.axisDir * delta
            for i = 1, #dd.entries do
                local e = dd.entries[i]
                local localDelta = Math3D.worldDeltaToParentLocal(e.parentInvRot, worldDelta)
                local nx = math.round(e.before.pos[1] + localDelta[1], 3)
                local ny = math.round(e.before.pos[2] + localDelta[2], 3)
                local nz = math.round(e.before.pos[3] + localDelta[3], 3)
                local p = e.node.transform.pos
                if p[1] ~= nx or p[2] ~= ny or p[3] ~= nz then
                    p[1], p[2], p[3] = nx, ny, nz
                    sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(e.id)
                end
            end
            dd.feedbackText = string.format("Move %s: %+.1f u%s", axisLabel, delta, shift and " [Snap]" or "")

        elseif subTool == "rotate" then
            local deltaDeg
            if dd.edgeOn then
                deltaDeg = ((ui.mx - dd.startMouseX) - (ui.my - dd.startMouseY)) * 0.6
            else
                local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, dd.origin, dd.axisDir)
                if hit and (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) > 0 then
                    local v1 = hit - dd.origin
                    if v1:getLength() > 0.001 then
                        v1 = v1:getNormalized()
                        local sinStep = dd.axisDir:dot(dd.prevVec:cross(v1))
                        local cosStep = math.clamp(dd.prevVec:dot(v1), -1, 1)
                        local stepDeg = math.deg(math.atan2(sinStep, cosStep))
                        dd.totalDeg = dd.totalDeg + stepDeg
                        dd.prevVec = v1
                        dd.curVec = v1
                    end
                end
                deltaDeg = dd.totalDeg
            end
            if shift then
                deltaDeg = math.round(deltaDeg / 15) * 15
            end
            dd.displayDeg = deltaDeg

            for i = 1, #dd.entries do
                local e = dd.entries[i]
                local newWorldAng = rotateBasisAngleAroundAxis(e.startWorldAng, dd.axisDir, deltaDeg)
                local localAng = Math3D.worldAngleToParentLocal(e.parentInvRot, newWorldAng)
                local np = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(localAng[1]), 2)
                local ny = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(localAng[2]), 2)
                local nr = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(localAng[3]), 2)
                local a = e.node.transform.ang
                local changed = (a[1] ~= np or a[2] ~= ny or a[3] ~= nr)
                a[1], a[2], a[3] = np, ny, nr

                if e.useOrbit then
                    local newWorldPos = Math3D.rotatePointAroundAxis(e.startWorldPos, e.orbitCenter, dd.axisDir, deltaDeg)
                    local posDelta = newWorldPos - e.startWorldPos
                    local localDelta = Math3D.worldDeltaToParentLocal(e.parentInvRot, posDelta)
                    local px = math.round(e.before.pos[1] + localDelta[1], 3)
                    local py = math.round(e.before.pos[2] + localDelta[2], 3)
                    local pz = math.round(e.before.pos[3] + localDelta[3], 3)
                    local p = e.node.transform.pos
                    if p[1] ~= px or p[2] ~= py or p[3] ~= pz then
                        p[1], p[2], p[3] = px, py, pz
                        changed = true
                    end
                end
                if changed then
                    sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(e.id)
                end
            end
            dd.feedbackText = string.format("Rotate %s: %+.1f deg%s", axisLabel, deltaDeg, shift and " [15 deg]" or "")

        elseif subTool == "scale" then
            local factor = 1.0
            if axis == "uniform" then
                local pxDelta = (ui.mx - dd.startMouseX) - (ui.my - dd.startMouseY)
                factor = 1.0 + pxDelta * 0.008
            else
                local hit = trace.intersectRayWithPlane(camera.camPos, rayDir * RAY_LEN, dd.origin, dd.planeNormal)
                if not hit or (hit - camera.camPos):dot(rayDir) <= 0 or (hit - dd.origin):getLength() > 25000 then return end
                local delta = (hit - dd.origin):dot(dd.axisDir) - dd.startProj
                factor = 1.0 + (delta / math.max(10, dd.handleLen))
            end
            if shift then
                factor = math.max(0.1, math.round(factor / 0.1) * 0.1)
            end
            factor = math.clamp(factor, MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
            dd.axisVisualFactor = factor

            local compIdx = (axis == "x") and 1 or ((axis == "y") and 2 or ((axis == "z") and 3 or 0))
            for i = 1, #dd.entries do
                local e = dd.entries[i]
                local bs = e.before.scale
                local sx, sy, sz = bs[1], bs[2], bs[3]
                if compIdx == 0 then
                    sx = math.clamp(math.round(bs[1] * factor, 3), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
                    sy = math.clamp(math.round(bs[2] * factor, 3), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
                    sz = math.clamp(math.round(bs[3] * factor, 3), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
                elseif compIdx == 1 then
                    sx = math.clamp(math.round(bs[1] * factor, 3), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
                elseif compIdx == 2 then
                    sy = math.clamp(math.round(bs[2] * factor, 3), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
                elseif compIdx == 3 then
                    sz = math.clamp(math.round(bs[3] * factor, 3), MIN_SCALE, MAX_SCALE)
                end
                local s = e.node.transform.scale
                local changed = (s[1] ~= sx or s[2] ~= sy or s[3] ~= sz)
                s[1], s[2], s[3] = sx, sy, sz

                if e.useOrbit and compIdx == 0 then
                    local offset = e.startWorldPos - e.orbitCenter
                    local newWorldPos = e.orbitCenter + offset * factor
                    local posDelta = newWorldPos - e.startWorldPos
                    local localDelta = Math3D.worldDeltaToParentLocal(e.parentInvRot, posDelta)
                    local p = e.node.transform.pos
                    p[1] = math.round(e.before.pos[1] + localDelta[1], 3)
                    p[2] = math.round(e.before.pos[2] + localDelta[2], 3)
                    p[3] = math.round(e.before.pos[3] + localDelta[3], 3)
                    changed = true
                end
                if changed then
                    sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(e.id)
                end
            end
            local lbl = (axis == "uniform") and "Uniform" or axisLabel
            dd.feedbackText = string.format("Scale %s: %.2fx%s", lbl, factor, shift and " [Snap]" or "")
        end
    end

    function Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
        if not Gizmos.dragging or not Gizmos.dragData then
            Gizmos.dragging = false
            Gizmos.activeAxis = nil
            Gizmos.dragData = nil
            return false
        end

        local dd = Gizmos.dragData
        local cmdEntries = {}
        for i = 1, #dd.entries do
            local e = dd.entries[i]
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(e.id)
            if node and node.transform then
                local cur = node.transform
                local changed = (not sameVec3(e.before.pos, cur.pos))
                    or (not sameVec3(e.before.ang, cur.ang))
                    or (not sameVec3(e.before.scale, cur.scale))
                    or (not sameVec3(e.before.pivot, cur.pivot))
                    or (not sameVec3(e.before.pivotAng, cur.pivotAng))
                if changed then
                    cmdEntries[#cmdEntries + 1] = {
                        id = e.id,
                        before = e.before,
                        after = {
                            pos      = IR.cloneVec3(cur.pos),
                            ang      = IR.cloneVec3(cur.ang),
                            scale    = IR.cloneVec3(cur.scale),
                            pivot    = IR.cloneVec3(cur.pivot or { 0, 0, 0 }),
                            pivotAng = IR.cloneVec3(cur.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }),
                        },
                    }
                end
            end
        end

        Gizmos.dragging = false
        Gizmos.activeAxis = nil
        Gizmos.dragData = nil
        _lastPickKey = nil

        if #cmdEntries > 0 and commands then
            local toolName = string.upper(string.sub(dd.tool, 1, 1)) .. string.sub(dd.tool, 2)
            local axisName = (dd.axis == "uniform") and "Uniform" or string.upper(dd.axis)
            local cmd = Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, toolName .. " " .. axisName, cmdEntries)
            commands:execute(cmd)
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    function Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
        if not Gizmos.dragging or not Gizmos.dragData then
            Gizmos.dragging = false
            Gizmos.activeAxis = nil
            Gizmos.dragData = nil
            return false
        end

        local dd = Gizmos.dragData
        for i = 1, #dd.entries do
            local e = dd.entries[i]
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(e.id)
            if node and node.transform then
                local t = node.transform
                t.pos[1], t.pos[2], t.pos[3] = e.before.pos[1], e.before.pos[2], e.before.pos[3]
                t.ang[1], t.ang[2], t.ang[3] = e.before.ang[1], e.before.ang[2], e.before.ang[3]
                t.scale[1], t.scale[2], t.scale[3] = e.before.scale[1], e.before.scale[2], e.before.scale[3]
                if e.before.pivot and t.pivot then
                    t.pivot[1], t.pivot[2], t.pivot[3] = e.before.pivot[1], e.before.pivot[2], e.before.pivot[3]
                end
                if e.before.pivotAng then
                    t.pivotAng = t.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }
                    t.pivotAng[1], t.pivotAng[2], t.pivotAng[3] = e.before.pivotAng[1], e.before.pivotAng[2], e.before.pivotAng[3]
                end
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(e.id)
            end
        end

        Gizmos.dragging = false
        Gizmos.activeAxis = nil
        Gizmos.dragData = nil
        _lastPickKey = nil
        return true
    end

    function Gizmos.update(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, commands, vpRect, paused)
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            local dd = Gizmos.dragData
            if not state.studioOpen or state.viewportLooking or paused
                or not dd or state.activeTool ~= dd.tool then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
                ui:release("gizmo_drag")
                return
            end

            ui.hotId = "gizmo_" .. tostring(Gizmos.activeAxis)
            if ui.leftDown then
                updateDrag(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph)
            else
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
                ui:release("gizmo_drag")
            end
            return
        end

        if paused or not state.studioOpen or state.viewportLooking
            or (ui.hotId ~= nil) or (ui.activeId ~= nil)
            or ui.autoScroll or ui._autoScrollConsumed then
            Gizmos.hoveredAxis = nil
            return
        end

        local axis = pickHandle(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, vpRect)
        Gizmos.hoveredAxis = axis
        if axis then
            ui.hotId = "gizmo_" .. axis
            local tipAxis = (axis == "uniform") and "Uniform" or string.upper(axis)
            if state.activeTool == "pivot" then
                ui:setTooltip("Edit Pivot " .. tipAxis .. " (Drag | Shift: Snap)")
            elseif state.activeTool == "move" then
                ui:setTooltip("Move " .. tipAxis .. " Axis (Drag | Shift: Grid Snap)")
            elseif state.activeTool == "rotate" then
                ui:setTooltip("Rotate " .. tipAxis .. " Ring (Drag | Shift: 15 deg Snap)")
            elseif state.activeTool == "scale" then
                ui:setTooltip("Scale " .. tipAxis .. " (Drag | Shift: 0.1x Snap)")
            end
            if ui.leftPressed then
                if beginDrag(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, axis) then
                    ui:capture("gizmo_drag")
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function drawConeTip(tipPos, dir, u, v, coneLen, coneRad)
        local base = tipPos - dir * coneLen
        local pA = base + u * coneRad
        local pB = base - u * coneRad
        local pC = base + v * coneRad
        local pD = base - v * coneRad
        render.draw3DLine(tipPos, pA, false)
        render.draw3DLine(tipPos, pB, false)
        render.draw3DLine(tipPos, pC, false)
        render.draw3DLine(tipPos, pD, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pA, pC, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pC, pB, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pB, pD, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pD, pA, false)
    end

    local function drawPivotDiamond(origin, size, col)
        SOLACE.Theme.setColor(col)
        local px1, px2 = origin + WORLD_X * size, origin - WORLD_X * size
        local py1, py2 = origin + WORLD_Y * size, origin - WORLD_Y * size
        local pz1, pz2 = origin + WORLD_Z * size, origin - WORLD_Z * size
        render.draw3DLine(pz1, px1, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz1, px2, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz1, py1, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz1, py2, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz2, px1, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz2, px2, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz2, py1, false)
        render.draw3DLine(pz2, py2, false)
        render.draw3DLine(px1, py1, false)
        render.draw3DLine(py1, px2, false)
        render.draw3DLine(px2, py2, false)
        render.draw3DLine(py2, px1, false)
    end

    function Gizmos.draw3D(state, camera, sceneGraph)
        if not state.studioOpen then return end
        local rawTool = state.activeTool
        local anchorId, targets = getEffectiveTargets(sceneGraph)
        if not anchorId then return end

        local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
        local origin, worldAng = getAnchorTransform(sceneGraph, anchorId, targets, state)
        local handleLen = getHandleLength(camera, origin)

        -- Draw pivot diamond marker when Pivot tool is active or node has custom non-zero pivot
        local aNode = sceneGraph:getNode(anchorId)
        local pv = aNode and aNode.transform and aNode.transform.pivot
        local hasCustomPivot = pv and (math.abs(pv[1] or 0) > 1e-3 or math.abs(pv[2] or 0) > 1e-3 or math.abs(pv[3] or 0) > 1e-3)
        if rawTool == "pivot" or hasCustomPivot then
            drawPivotDiamond(origin, handleLen * 0.10, (rawTool == "pivot") and COL_HILITE or Theme.C.warning)
            if hasCustomPivot then
                local nodeWPos = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, anchorId)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.warning, 140)
                render.draw3DLine(nodeWPos, origin, false)
            end
        end

        if rawTool ~= "move" and rawTool ~= "rotate" and rawTool ~= "scale" and rawTool ~= "pivot" then return end

        local tool = getEffectiveSubTool(state)
        local ax, ay, az = getToolAxes(rawTool, worldAng, state)
        local hiAxis = Gizmos.dragging and Gizmos.activeAxis or Gizmos.hoveredAxis

        if tool == "move" then
            local defs = {
                { "x", ax, ay, az },
                { "y", ay, az, ax },
                { "z", az, ax, ay },
            }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local name, dir, u, v = defs[i][1], defs[i][2], defs[i][3], defs[i][4]
                local isHi = (hiAxis == name)
                local col = isHi and COL_HILITE or axisColor(name)
                if Gizmos.dragging and isHi then
                    Theme.setColor(COL_GUIDE)
                    render.draw3DLine(origin - dir * (handleLen * 2.5), origin + dir * (handleLen * 2.5), false)
                end
                Theme.setColor(col)
                local tip = origin + dir * handleLen
                render.draw3DLine(origin, tip, false)
                if isHi then
                    local off = u * math.max(0.4, handleLen * 0.014)
                    render.draw3DLine(origin + off, tip + off, false)
                    render.draw3DLine(origin - off, tip - off, false)
                end
                drawConeTip(tip + dir * (handleLen * 0.14), dir, u, v, handleLen * 0.22, handleLen * (isHi and 0.075 or 0.058))
            end

        elseif tool == "rotate" then
            local radius = handleLen * 0.90
            local ringDefs = {
                { "x", ax, ay, az },
                { "y", ay, az, ax },
                { "z", az, ax, ay },
            }
            for i = 1, 3 do
                local name, normal, u, v = ringDefs[i][1], ringDefs[i][2], ringDefs[i][3], ringDefs[i][4]
                local isHi = (hiAxis == name)
                Theme.setColor(isHi and COL_HILITE or axisColor(name))
                local prevPt = origin + u * radius
                for s = 1, RING_SEGS do
                    local nextPt = origin + u * (COS_TABLE[s + 1] * radius) + v * (SIN_TABLE[s + 1] * radius)
                    render.draw3DLine(prevPt, nextPt, false)
                    if isHi then
                        local off = normal * math.max(0.35, handleLen * 0.012)
                        render.draw3DLine(prevPt + off, nextPt + off, false)
                    end
                    prevPt = nextPt
                end
            end

            if Gizmos.dragging and Gizmos.dragData and not Gizmos.dragData.edgeOn then
                local dd = Gizmos.dragData
                if dd.startVec and dd.curVec then
                    Theme.setColor(COL_GUIDE)
                    render.draw3DLine(dd.origin, dd.origin + dd.startVec * radius, false)
                    Theme.setColor(COL_HILITE)
                    render.draw3DLine(dd.origin, dd.origin + dd.curVec * radius, false)
                end
            end

        elseif tool == "scale" then
            local defs = {
                { "x", ax, ay, az },
                { "y", ay, az, ax },
                { "z", az, ax, ay },
            }
            local boxHalf = handleLen * 0.065
            local boxMin = Vector(-boxHalf, -boxHalf, -boxHalf)
            local boxMax = Vector(boxHalf, boxHalf, boxHalf)
            local drawAng = worldAng or Angle(0, 0, 0)

            for i = 1, 3 do
                local name, dir, u = defs[i][1], defs[i][2], defs[i][3]
                local isHi = (hiAxis == name)
                local stretch = (Gizmos.dragging and isHi and Gizmos.dragData)
                    and math.clamp(Gizmos.dragData.axisVisualFactor or 1, 0.35, 1.8) or 1.0
                local tip = origin + dir * (handleLen * stretch)
                Theme.setColor(isHi and COL_HILITE or axisColor(name))
                render.draw3DLine(origin, tip, false)
                if isHi then
                    local off = u * math.max(0.4, handleLen * 0.014)
                    render.draw3DLine(origin + off, tip + off, false)
                end
                render.draw3DWireframeBox(tip, drawAng, boxMin, boxMax, false)
            end

            local uHalf = handleLen * ((hiAxis == "uniform") and 0.115 or 0.09)
            Theme.setColor((hiAxis == "uniform") and COL_HILITE or COL_UNIFORM)
            render.draw3DWireframeBox(origin, drawAng, Vector(-uHalf, -uHalf, -uHalf), Vector(uHalf, uHalf, uHalf), false)
        end
    end

    function Gizmos.drawOverlay(ui, state, vpRect)
        if not state.studioOpen or not Gizmos.dragging or not Gizmos.dragData then return end
        local dd = Gizmos.dragData
        local text = dd.feedbackText
        if not text or text == "" then return end

        local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.BodyBold)
        local tw = render.getTextSize(text) or 120
        local hint = "Shift: Snap  |  Esc: Cancel"
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        local hw = render.getTextSize(hint) or 130
        local pw = math.max(tw, hw) + 22
        local ph = 42

        local minX = vpRect and (vpRect.x + 8) or 8
        local maxX = vpRect and (vpRect.x + vpRect.w - pw - 8) or (ui.scrW - pw - 8)
        local minY = vpRect and (vpRect.y + 8) or 8
        local maxY = vpRect and (vpRect.y + vpRect.h - ph - 8) or (ui.scrH - ph - 8)

        local px = math.clamp(ui.mx + 18, minX, math.max(minX, maxX))
        local py = math.clamp(ui.my - 28, minY, math.max(minY, maxY))

        Theme.drawRoundedRect(4, px, py, pw, ph, Theme.C.tooltipBg, 240)
        Theme.setColor(axisColor(dd.axis))
        render.drawRectFast(px, py + 3, 3, ph - 6)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
        render.drawRectOutline(px, py, pw, ph, 1)

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.BodyBold)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
        render.drawSimpleText(px + 10, py + 12, text, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textMuted)
