                rowW = bw - 8,
                valid = okAll,
                reason = reason,
                targetName = rootNode and rootNode.name or "Scene Root",
            }
        end

        if rightClickedNode then
            local sx = ui.mx + (ui._rtOffsetX or 0)
            local sy = ui.my + (ui._rtOffsetY or 0)
            openExplorerContextMenu(ui, state, sceneGraph, actions, rightClickedNode, sx, sy)
        elseif not anyRowHovered and ui.rightPressed and ui:pointInRect(bx, by + 28, bw, bh - 28) and ui:canInteractCurrentWindow() then
            local sx = ui.mx + (ui._rtOffsetX or 0)
            local sy = ui.my + (ui._rtOffsetY or 0)
            openExplorerContextMenu(ui, state, sceneGraph, actions, sceneGraph:getSelectedNode(), sx, sy)
        end

        -- Visual drop indicator + drag ghost pill
        if Explorer._draggingId then
            if dropInfo then
                local col = dropInfo.valid and Theme.C.accentBright or Theme.C.danger
                if dropInfo.mode == "into" then
                    Theme.setColor(col, 38)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, dropInfo.drawY, dropInfo.rowW, rowH)
                    Theme.setColor(col, 230)
                    render.drawRectOutline(bx + 2, dropInfo.drawY, dropInfo.rowW, rowH, 1)
                else
                    Theme.setColor(col, 240)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 6, dropInfo.drawY - 1, dropInfo.rowW - 6, 2)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 4, dropInfo.drawY - 3, 5, 6)
                end
            end

            -- Floating drag label inside Explorer
            local dragCount = #(Explorer._dragIds or {})
            local dNode = sceneGraph:getNode(Explorer._draggingId)
            local label = dragCount > 1 and (dragCount .. " nodes") or (dNode and dNode.name or "Node")
            if dropInfo then
                label = dropInfo.valid and (label .. " -> " .. dropInfo.targetName) or (dropInfo.reason or "Invalid parent")
            end
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            local lw = (render.getTextSize(label) or 80) + 14
            local gx = math.clamp(ui.mx + 12, bx + 4, bx + bw - lw - 4)
            local gy = math.clamp(ui.my + 8, listY + 2, listY + listH - 20)
            Theme.drawRoundedRect(3, gx, gy, lw, 18, Theme.C.bgPopup, 235)
            Theme.setColor((dropInfo and not dropInfo.valid) and Theme.C.danger or Theme.C.accentBright)
            render.drawRectOutline(gx, gy, lw, 18, 1)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawSimpleText(gx + 7, gy + 9, label, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

            if ui.leftReleased then
                local dragIds = Explorer._dragIds
                Explorer.cancelDrag(ui)
                if dragIds and dropInfo and dropInfo.valid and actions.reparentNodes then
                    actions.reparentNodes(dragIds, dropInfo.parentId, dropInfo.insertIndex)
                end
            end
        end

        Explorer._contentH = (curY - startY) + 8
        maxScroll = math.max(0, Explorer._contentH - listH)

        if maxScroll > 0 then
            Explorer._scrollY = Widgets.scrollbar(
                ui, "exp_sb",
                bx + bw - 8, listY + 2, 6, listH - 4,
                Explorer._scrollY, Explorer._contentH, listH
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()
        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Explorer = Explorer

    -- 14.4 Right-Bottom Properties Inspector


    local Properties = {
        _scrollY = 0,
        _contentH = 0,
        _viewH = 0,
        _selCountLast = -1,
        _selCountLabel = "0 selected",
        _xformCache = {},
        _matError = nil,
        _matErrorNodeId = nil,
        _rtCacheTime = -1,
        _rtCpuStr = "",
        _rtRamStr = "",
        _rtHolosStr = "",
        _rtCalcPerm = false,
        _rtHoloPerm = false,
    }

    function Properties.parseNumber(raw)
        if type(raw) == "number" then
            if raw ~= raw or math.abs(raw) == math.huge then return nil end
            return raw
        end
        if type(raw) ~= "string" then return nil end
        local s = string.trim(raw)
        if s == "" then return nil end
        local low = string.lower(s)
        if string.find(low, "nan", 1, true) or string.find(low, "inf", 1, true) then
            return nil
        end
        local n = tonumber(s)
        if not n or n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then
            return nil
        end
        return n
    end

    function Properties.validateMaterialPath(rawPath)
        local clean = string.trim(tostring(rawPath or ""))
        if clean == "" then
            return true, ""
        end
        if #clean > 260 then
            return false, "Material path too long"
        end
        if string.sub(string.lower(clean), -4) == ".vmt" then
            return false, "Omit .vmt extension (Starfall material.load)"
        end
        if string.sub(clean, 1, 1) ~= "!" and material and material.load and hasPermission("material.load") then
            local ok, matOrErr = pcall(material.load, clean)
            if not ok or not matOrErr then
                return false, "Invalid or missing material"
            end
        end
        return true, clean
    end

    local function drawVec3EditRow(ui, idPrefix, x, y, w, h, label, vec, dec, axesLabels, axesCols, onAxisCommit, tip)
        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
        render.drawSimpleText(x, y + 7, label, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

        local fy = y + 15
        local gap = 4
        local fw = math.floor((w - gap * 2) / 3)
        local fmt = "%." .. tostring(dec) .. "f"
        for i = 1, 3 do
            local fx = x + (i - 1) * (fw + gap)
            local boxW = (i == 3) and (w - (fw + gap) * 2) or fw
            local val = vec[i] or 0
            local disp = string.format(fmt, val)
            local raw = tostring(math.round(val, 3))
            Widgets.editField(ui, idPrefix .. "_" .. i, fx, fy, boxW, h, disp, raw, {
                prefix = axesLabels[i],
                barColor = axesCols[i],
                tooltip = tip,
                onCommit = function(str)
                    local n = Properties.parseNumber(str)
                    if n ~= nil and onAxisCommit then
                        onAxisCommit(i, n)
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        return 15 + h + 5
    end

    local function findListLabel(list, idVal, fallback)
        for _, it in ipairs(list) do
            if it.id == idVal then return it.label end
        end
        return fallback or tostring(idVal)
    end

    local function cycleListValue(list, curVal, dir)
        local idx = 1
        for i, it in ipairs(list) do
            if it.id == curVal then idx = i break end
        end
        idx = ((idx - 1 + dir) % #list) + 1
        return list[idx].id
    end

    -- Phase 5: Pivot & Assembly section
    local function drawPivotSection(ui, state, sceneGraph, sel, bx, curY, innerW, headerW, pad, rowH, fieldH, by, visBottom, actions)
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.pivot = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_pivot", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Pivot & Assembly", state.sections.pivot)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if not state.sections.pivot then return curY end

        local axisCols = { Theme.C.axisX, Theme.C.axisY, Theme.C.axisZ }
        sel.transform.pivot = sel.transform.pivot or { 0, 0, 0 }
        sel.transform.pivotAng = sel.transform.pivotAng or { 0, 0, 0 }

        local pivRowH = 15 + fieldH + 5
        if curY + pivRowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            drawVec3EditRow(ui, "prop_piv_pos_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, fieldH,
                "Pivot Local Offset", sel.transform.pivot, 2, { "X", "Y", "Z" }, axisCols,
                function(idx, n)
                    if actions.setNodePivotAxis then actions.setNodePivotAxis(sel.id, "pivot", idx, n) end
                end, "Edit local pivot offset without moving mesh")
        end
        curY = curY + pivRowH

        if curY + pivRowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            drawVec3EditRow(ui, "prop_piv_ang_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, fieldH,
                "Pivot Local Angle", sel.transform.pivotAng, 1, { "P", "Y", "R" }, axisCols,
                function(idx, n)
                    if actions.setNodePivotAxis then actions.setNodePivotAxis(sel.id, "pivotAng", idx, n) end
                end, "Edit local pivot basis orientation")
        end
        curY = curY + pivRowH

        if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            local hw = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
            if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_piv_center_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, hw, rowH, "Center Bounds", {
                tooltip = "Set pivot to node bounds center without moving geometry (Undoable)",
            }) then
                if actions.centerNodePivot then actions.centerNodePivot(sel.id) end
            end
            if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_piv_reset_" .. sel.id, bx + pad + hw + 4, curY, innerW - hw - 4, rowH, "Reset Pivot", {
                tooltip = "Reset pivot offset & rotation to local origin (Undoable)",
            }) then
                if actions.resetNodePivot then actions.resetNodePivot(sel.id) end
            end
        end
        curY = curY + rowH + 4

        if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            local isPivTool = (state.activeTool == "pivot")
            local hw = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
            if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_piv_tool", bx + pad, curY, hw, rowH, isPivTool and "Pivot Gizmo: ON" or "Edit Pivot (T)", {
                active = isPivTool,
                tooltip = "Toggle Pivot Edit Gizmo (Hotkey: T)",
            }) then
                if actions.setTool then
                    actions.setTool(isPivTool and "translate" or "pivot")
                end
            end
            local canDetach = (sel.parentId ~= nil and sel.parentId ~= sceneGraph:getRootId())
            if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_detach_" .. sel.id, bx + pad + hw + 4, curY, innerW - hw - 4, rowH, "Detach", {
                disabled = not canDetach,
                tooltip = "Detach node from parent to Scene Root while keeping world pose",
            }) then
                if actions.detachNodes then actions.detachNodes({ sel.id }) end
            end
        end
        curY = curY + rowH + 6
        return curY
    end

    -- Phase 7: Extended Appearance (RenderMode, RenderFX, Cull, Submaterials, Sprite parameters)
    local function drawAppearanceExtra(ui, state, runtime, sel, bx, curY, innerW, pad, rowH, by, visBottom, actions)
        local app = sel.appearance
        if sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL then
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local rm = app.renderMode or 0
                local p, n = Widgets.stepper(ui, "prop_rm_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Render Mode", findListLabel(Constants.RENDER_MODES, rm, "Normal"), "Engine RENDERMODE")
                if (p or n) and actions.setNodeAppearanceProp then
                    actions.setNodeAppearanceProp(sel.id, "renderMode", cycleListValue(Constants.RENDER_MODES, rm, p and -1 or 1), "Change RenderMode")
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local rfx = app.renderFX or 0
                local p, n = Widgets.stepper(ui, "prop_rfx_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Render FX", findListLabel(Constants.RENDER_FX_LIST, rfx, "None"), "Engine RENDERFX")
                if (p or n) and actions.setNodeAppearanceProp then
                    actions.setNodeAppearanceProp(sel.id, "renderFX", cycleListValue(Constants.RENDER_FX_LIST, rfx, p and -1 or 1), "Change RenderFX")
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local hw = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
                local dbl = (app.cullMode == 1)
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_cull_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, hw, rowH, dbl and "Cull: 2-Sided" or "Cull: Back", {
                    active = dbl,
                    tooltip = "Hologram cull mode (0 = Backface cull, 1 = Double-sided)",
                }) then
                    if actions.setNodeAppearanceProp then
                        actions.setNodeAppearanceProp(sel.id, "cullMode", dbl and 0 or 1, "Toggle Cull Mode")
                    end
                end
                local unlit = (app.suppressEngineLighting == true)
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_unlit_" .. sel.id, bx + pad + hw + 4, curY, innerW - hw - 4, rowH, unlit and "Fullbright" or "Lit", {
                    active = unlit,
                    tooltip = "Suppress engine lighting on hologram",
                }) then
                    if actions.setNodeAppearanceProp then
                        actions.setNodeAppearanceProp(sel.id, "suppressEngineLighting", not unlit, "Toggle Suppress Lighting")
                    end
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            -- Submaterials slot 0..3 quick override
            local mats = runtime and runtime:getNodeMaterials(sel.id) or {}
            local slotCount = math.max(1, math.min(4, #mats))
            for si = 0, slotCount - 1 do
                if curY + 21 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    local curSub = (app.submaterials and (app.submaterials[si] or app.submaterials[tostring(si)])) or ""
                    Widgets.editField(ui, "prop_submat_" .. si .. "_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 21,
                        curSub, curSub, {
                            prefix = "S" .. si,
                            placeholder = mats[si + 1] or ("submaterial " .. si),
                            tooltip = "Override submaterial slot " .. si .. " (empty = default)",
                            onCommit = function(str)
                                if actions.setNodeSubmaterial then
                                    actions.setNodeSubmaterial(sel.id, si, str)
                                end
                            end,
                        })
                end
                curY = curY + 23
            end
        elseif sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE then
            local spr = sel.sprite or {}
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local wv, wc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_spr_w_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Sprite Width", spr.width or 24, 2, 256, 1, "%.0f", "3D Sprite width in units")
                if wc then spr.width = wv end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local hv, hc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_spr_h_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Sprite Height", spr.height or 24, 2, 256, 1, "%.0f", "3D Sprite height in units")
                if hc then spr.height = hv end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local rv, rc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_spr_roll_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Roll Speed", spr.rollSpeed or 0, -180, 180, 5, "%.0f d/s", "Continuous sprite rotation speed")
                if rc then spr.rollSpeed = rv end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local ltv, ltc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_spr_life_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Lifetime (0=inf)", spr.lifetime or 0, 0, 30, 0.25, "%.2fs", "0 = always visible, >0 = fades over lifetime")
                if ltc then spr.lifetime = ltv end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4
        end
        return curY
    end

    -- Phases 9, 10, 11: Bones / VFX / Camera Inspector
    local function drawSpecialInspector(ui, state, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, sel, bx, curY, innerW, headerW, pad, rowH, fieldH, by, visBottom, actions)
        -- Phase 11: Cinematic Camera Inspector
        if sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA then
            if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                state.sections.camNode = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_camnode", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Cinematic Camera", state.sections.camNode)
            end
            curY = curY + 26
            if state.sections.camNode then
                sel.camera = sel.camera or { fov = 80, focusDist = 180, shakeAmplitude = 0, shakeFrequency = 8, shakeDuration = 0 }
                local c = sel.camera
                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    local isPreview = (state.activeCameraId == sel.id)
                    local hw = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_cam_prev_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, hw, rowH, isPreview and "Exit Preview" or "Preview Cam", {
                        active = isPreview,
                        icon = "camera",
                        tooltip = "View viewport through this Cinematic Camera",
                    }) then
                        state.activeCameraId = isPreview and nil or sel.id
                    end
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_cam_match_" .. sel.id, bx + pad + hw + 4, curY, innerW - hw - 4, rowH, "Match View", {
                        tooltip = "Move this camera node to current Freecam position & angle (Undoable)",
                    }) then
                        if actions.matchCameraToFreecam then actions.matchCameraToFreecam(sel.id) end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 4

                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    local fv, fc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_camnode_fov_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                        "Camera FOV", c.fov or 80, 25, 125, 1, "%.0f deg", "Cinematic camera field of view")
                    if fc then c.fov = fv end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 4

                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    local targetName = "None"
                    if c.lookAtNodeId then
                        local tn = sceneGraph:getNode(c.lookAtNodeId)
                        targetName = tn and tn.name or "Missing"
                    end
                    local p, n = Widgets.stepper(ui, "prop_cam_look_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                        "Look-At", targetName, "Cycle target node for automatic camera aiming")
                    if p or n then
                        local candidates = { false }
                        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
                        for id, nd in pairs(sceneGraph:getAllNodes()) do
                            if id ~= rootId and id ~= sel.id then
                                candidates[#candidates + 1] = id
                            end
                        end
                        table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
                            if a == false then return true end
                            if b == false then return false end
                            return tostring(a) < tostring(b)
                        end)
                        local idx = 1
                        local curKey = c.lookAtNodeId or false
                        for i = 1, #candidates do
                            if candidates[i] == curKey then idx = i break end
                        end
                        idx = ((idx - 1 + (p and -1 or 1)) % #candidates) + 1
                        c.lookAtNodeId = candidates[idx] or nil
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 4

                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    local sa, sc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_cam_shk_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                        "Shake Amp", c.shakeAmplitude or 0, 0, 12, 0.25, "%.2f", "Procedural camera shake amplitude")
                    if sc then c.shakeAmplitude = sa end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 4

                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_cam_keyfov_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH, "+ Keyframe Camera FOV", {
                        tooltip = "Insert keyframe for camera.fov at current timeline time",
                    }) then
                        if actions.addKeyframe then actions.addKeyframe(sel.id, "camera.fov", state.timeline.currentTime) end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 6
            end
        end

        -- Phase 10: VFX & Light Inspector
        if sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT then
            if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                state.sections.vfx = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_vfx", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "VFX / Light Layer", state.sections.vfx)
            end
            curY = curY + 26
            if state.sections.vfx then
                sel.effect = sel.effect or SOLACE.IR.createEffectPayload()
                local fx = sel.effect
                local modes = { "particles", "beam", "orb", "light" }
                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    local p, n = Widgets.stepper(ui, "prop_vfx_mode_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                        "VFX Mode", fx.mode or "particles", "Cycle VFX layer rendering mode")
                    if p or n then
                        local idx = 1
                        for i, m in ipairs(modes) do if m == fx.mode then idx = i break end end
                        idx = ((idx - 1 + (p and -1 or 1)) % #modes) + 1
                        fx.mode = modes[idx]
                        sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(sel.id)
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 4

                if fx.mode == "light" then
                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local ls, lc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_vfx_lsz_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Light Radius", fx.lightSize or 256, 32, 1024, 16, "%.0f", "Dynamic light radius (requires light.create)")
                        if lc then fx.lightSize = ls sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(sel.id) end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4
                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local lb, lbc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_vfx_lbr_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Brightness", fx.lightBrightness or 2, 0.2, 8, 0.2, "%.1f", "Dynamic light intensity")
                        if lbc then fx.lightBrightness = lb sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(sel.id) end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4
                else
                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local rt, rc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_vfx_rate_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Emit Rate", fx.rate or 12, 1, 60, 1, "%.0f/s", "Particles spawned per second (quota-capped)")
                        if rc then fx.rate = rt end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4
                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local sp, sc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_vfx_spd_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Velocity", fx.speed or 60, 0, 500, 5, "%.0f", "Initial particle velocity")
                        if sc then fx.speed = sp end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4
                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local ss, ssc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_vfx_ssz_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Start Size", fx.startSize or 8, 1, 128, 1, "%.0f", "Start size (or beam width)")
                        if ssc then fx.startSize = ss end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4
                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local lt, ltc = Widgets.slider(ui, "prop_vfx_life_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Lifetime", fx.lifetime or 1.2, 0.1, 10, 0.1, "%.1fs", "Particle lifetime in seconds")
                        if ltc then fx.lifetime = lt end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4
                end

                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_vfx_burst_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH, "Trigger VFX Burst", {
                        icon = "sparkle",
                        tooltip = "Emit an immediate burst from this VFX node",
                    }) then
                        if runtime and runtime.triggerVFXBurst then
                            runtime:triggerVFXBurst(sel.id, 14)
                        end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 6
            end
        end

        -- Phase 9: Bones / Rigging Inspector
        if sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL then
            local bones = runtime and runtime:getNodeBones(sel.id) or {}
            if #bones > 0 then
                if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    state.sections.bones = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_bones", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Bones & Pose (" .. #bones .. ")", state.sections.bones)
                end
                curY = curY + 26
                if state.sections.bones then
                    local curIdx = state.selectedBoneIdx
                    local curBone = nil
                    for _, b in ipairs(bones) do
                        if b.index == curIdx then curBone = b break end
                    end
                    if not curBone then
                        curBone = bones[1]
                        state.selectedBoneIdx = curBone.index
                    end

                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local p, n = Widgets.stepper(ui, "prop_bone_sel_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                            "Bone", string.format("#%d %s", curBone.index, curBone.name), "Cycle model bones")
                        if p or n then
                            local pos = 1
                            for i, b in ipairs(bones) do if b.index == curBone.index then pos = i break end end
                            pos = ((pos - 1 + (p and -1 or 1)) % #bones) + 1
                            state.selectedBoneIdx = bones[pos].index
                            curBone = bones[pos]
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 4

                    sel.bones = sel.bones or {}
                    local bData = sel.bones[curBone.index] or { pos = { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = { 0, 0, 0 }, scale = { 1, 1, 1 } }
                    local axisCols = { Theme.C.axisX, Theme.C.axisY, Theme.C.axisZ }
                    local brH = 15 + fieldH + 5
                    if curY + brH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        drawVec3EditRow(ui, "prop_bone_ang_" .. sel.id .. "_" .. curBone.index, bx + pad, curY, innerW, fieldH,
                            "Bone Angle Offset", bData.ang, 1, { "P", "Y", "R" }, axisCols,
                            function(axis, val)
                                if actions.setNodeBoneAngleAxis then
                                    actions.setNodeBoneAngleAxis(sel.id, curBone.index, axis, val)
                                end
                            end, "Manipulate bone angle (works on holograms & permitted entities)")
                    end
                    curY = curY + brH

                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local hw = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_bone_key_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, hw, rowH, "+Key Bone", {
                            tooltip = "Insert bone angle keyframe at playhead",
                        }) then
                            if actions.addKeyframe then
                                actions.addKeyframe(sel.id, "bones." .. tostring(curBone.index) .. ".ang", state.timeline.currentTime)
                            end
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_bone_rst_" .. sel.id, bx + pad + hw + 4, curY, innerW - hw - 4, rowH, "Reset Bones", {
                            tooltip = "Reset all bone angle/position overrides on this node (Undoable)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.resetNodeBones then actions.resetNodeBones(sel.id) end
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 6
                end
            end
        end

        return curY
    end

    function Properties.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, rect, actions, opts)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end

        local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        local selCount = #sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
        if Properties._matErrorNodeId ~= (sel and sel.id or nil) then
            Properties._matError = nil
            Properties._matErrorNodeId = sel and sel.id or nil
        end

        local rightLabel
        if selCount > 1 then
            if selCount ~= Properties._selCountLast then
                Properties._selCountLast = selCount
                Properties._selCountLabel = selCount .. " selected"
            end
            rightLabel = Properties._selCountLabel
        else
            rightLabel = sel and sel.name or "No Selection"
        end
        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Properties", rightLabel, {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "prop_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.properties = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_properties") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 20 or bh < 40 then return end
        Properties._viewH = bh

        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(bx, by, bw, bh) then
            Properties._scrollY = Properties._scrollY - ui.wheelDelta * 28
        end
        local maxScroll = math.max(0, Properties._contentH - bh)
        Properties._scrollY = math.clamp(Properties._scrollY, 0, maxScroll)

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        local pad = Theme.Metrics.pad
        local rowH = Theme.Metrics.controlH
        local headerW = bw - (maxScroll > 0 and 12 or 0)
        local innerW = bw - pad * 2 - (maxScroll > 0 and 12 or 0)
        local curY = by + pad - Properties._scrollY
        local startY = curY
        local visBottom = by + bh

        -- 1. Selected Object
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.selection = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_selection", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Selection", state.sections.selection)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.selection then
            if sel then
                local isRoot = (sel.id == sceneGraph:getRootId())

                if not isRoot then
                    if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.editField(ui, "prop_node_name_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 22, sel.name, sel.name, {
                            mono = false,
                            placeholder = "Object name...",
                            tooltip = "Rename selected object (Enter to commit, Esc to cancel)",
                            onCommit = function(v)
                                if actions.renameNode then
                                    actions.renameNode(sel.id, v)
                                else
                                    sceneGraph:renameNode(sel.id, v)
                                end
                            end,
                        })
                    end
                    curY = curY + 26
                end

                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Kind", sel.kind, Theme.C.accentBright)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "IR ID", sel.id, Theme.C.textMuted)
                end
                curY = curY + 22

                if not isRoot then
                    local entry = runtime:getEntry(sel.id)
                    if entry and entry.errorMsg then
                        if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                            Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Runtime", entry.errorMsg, Theme.C.danger)
                        end
                        curY = curY + 20
                    end

                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local thirdW = math.floor((innerW - 8) / 3)
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_dup", bx + pad, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Dup", {
                            tooltip = "Duplicate selected object(s) (Ctrl+D)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.duplicateSelected then actions.duplicateSelected() end
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_focus", bx + pad + thirdW + 4, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Focus", {
                            tooltip = "Move Freecam to frame this object (F)",
                        }) then
                            actions.focusSelected()
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_del", bx + pad + (thirdW + 4) * 2, curY, innerW - (thirdW + 4) * 2, rowH, "Delete", {
                            tooltip = "Delete selected object(s) (Del)",
                        }) then
                            actions.deleteSelected()
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 6

                    if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        local thirdW = math.floor((innerW - 8) / 3)
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_tool_move", bx + pad, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Move", {
                            active = (state.activeTool == "move"),
                            tooltip = "Switch to 3D Move Gizmo (2)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.setTool then actions.setTool("move") else state.activeTool = "move" end
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_tool_rot", bx + pad + thirdW + 4, curY, thirdW, rowH, "Rotate", {
                            active = (state.activeTool == "rotate"),
                            tooltip = "Switch to 3D Rotate Gizmo (E / 3)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.setTool then actions.setTool("rotate") else state.activeTool = "rotate" end
                        end
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_tool_scl", bx + pad + (thirdW + 4) * 2, curY, innerW - (thirdW + 4) * 2, rowH, "Scale", {
                            active = (state.activeTool == "scale"),
                            tooltip = "Switch to 3D Scale Gizmo (R / 4)",
                        }) then
                            if actions.setTool then actions.setTool("scale") else state.activeTool = "scale" end
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + rowH + 8
                end
            else
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Selection", "None (Click object in viewport)", Theme.C.textMuted)
                end
                curY = curY + 24
            end
        end

        -- 2. Numeric Transform Editors (non-root selection)
        if sel and sel.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
            if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                state.sections.transform = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_transform", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Transform", state.sections.transform)
            end
            curY = curY + 26
            if state.sections.transform then
                local axisCols = { Theme.C.axisX, Theme.C.axisY, Theme.C.axisZ }
                local fieldH = 21

                local posRowH = 15 + fieldH + 5
                if curY + posRowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    drawVec3EditRow(
                        ui, "prop_num_pos_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, fieldH,
                        "Position (Local)", sel.transform.pos, 2, { "X", "Y", "Z" }, axisCols,
                        function(idx, n)
                            if actions.setNodeTransformAxis then
                                actions.setNodeTransformAxis(sel.id, "pos", idx, n)
                            end
                        end,
                        "Edit Position X/Y/Z (Enter/blur commits, Esc cancels)"
                    )
                end
                curY = curY + posRowH

                local rotRowH = 15 + fieldH + 5
                if curY + rotRowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    drawVec3EditRow(
                        ui, "prop_num_ang_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, fieldH,
                        "Rotation (Pitch / Yaw / Roll)", sel.transform.ang, 1, { "P", "Y", "R" }, axisCols,
                        function(idx, n)
                            if actions.setNodeTransformAxis then
                                actions.setNodeTransformAxis(sel.id, "ang", idx, n)
                            end
                        end,
                        "Edit Rotation Pitch/Yaw/Roll in degrees (Enter/blur commits)"
                    )
                end
                curY = curY + rotRowH

                local sclRowH = 15 + fieldH + 5
                if curY + sclRowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    drawVec3EditRow(
                        ui, "prop_num_scl_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, curY, innerW, fieldH,
                        string.format("Scale (%.2f .. %.0f)", Constants.MIN_SCALE, Constants.MAX_SCALE),
                        sel.transform.scale, 2, { "X", "Y", "Z" }, axisCols,
                        function(idx, n)
                            if actions.setNodeTransformAxis then
                                actions.setNodeTransformAxis(sel.id, "scale", idx, n)
                            end
                        end,
                        string.format("Edit Scale X/Y/Z (clamped %.2f - %.0f)", Constants.MIN_SCALE, Constants.MAX_SCALE)
                    )
                end
                curY = curY + sclRowH + 4
            end

            -- 3. Appearance & Model Info
            if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                state.sections.appearance = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_appearance", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Appearance & Info", state.sections.appearance)
            end
            curY = curY + 26
            if state.sections.appearance then
                -- Visibility toggle
                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_vis", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH, sel.visible and "Visible: ON" or "Visible: OFF", {
                        icon = "eye",
                        active = sel.visible,
                        tooltip = "Toggle node visibility (Undoable)",
                    }) then
                        if actions.toggleVisibility then
                            actions.toggleVisibility(sel.id)
                        else
                            sceneGraph:toggleNodeVisible(sel.id)
                        end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 6

                -- Color RGBA editor (swatch + R, G, B, A boxes)
                local col = sel.appearance.color or { 255, 255, 255, 255 }
                if curY + 38 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY + 7, "Color (RGBA 0-255)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

                    local fy = curY + 15
                    local swW = 20
                    Theme.setColor(Color(col[1] or 255, col[2] or 255, col[3] or 255, col[4] or 255))
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + pad, fy, swW, 21)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
                    render.drawRectOutline(bx + pad, fy, swW, 21, 1)

                    local chanX = bx + pad + swW + 4
                    local chanAreaW = innerW - swW - 4
                    local gap = 3
                    local cw = math.floor((chanAreaW - gap * 3) / 4)
                    local chanNames = { "R", "G", "B", "A" }
                    local chanCols = { Theme.C.axisX, Theme.C.axisY, Theme.C.axisZ, Theme.C.textSecondary }
                    for i = 1, 4 do
                        local fx = chanX + (i - 1) * (cw + gap)
                        local bw2 = (i == 4) and (chanAreaW - (cw + gap) * 3) or cw
                        local cv = math.clamp(math.floor((col[i] or 255) + 0.5), 0, 255)
                        Widgets.editField(ui, "prop_col_" .. i .. "_" .. sel.id, fx, fy, bw2, 21, tostring(cv), tostring(cv), {
                            prefix = chanNames[i],
                            barColor = chanCols[i],
                            tooltip = "Edit color " .. chanNames[i] .. " channel (0-255)",
                            onCommit = function(str)
                                local n = Properties.parseNumber(str)
                                if n ~= nil and actions.setNodeColorChannel then
                                    actions.setNodeColorChannel(sel.id, i, math.clamp(math.floor(n + 0.5), 0, 255))
                                end
                            end,
                        })
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + 41

                -- Material override input + Clear button
                if curY + 38 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY + 7, "Material Override", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

                    local fy = curY + 15
                    local clrW = 36
                    local matW = math.max(60, innerW - clrW - 4)
                    local matVal = sel.appearance.material or ""
                    Widgets.editField(ui, "prop_mat_" .. sel.id, bx + pad, fy, matW, 21, matVal, matVal, {
                        placeholder = "(default material)",
                        tooltip = "Material path (no .vmt; empty restores default)",
                        onCommit = function(str)
                            if actions.setNodeMaterial then
                                local ok, err = actions.setNodeMaterial(sel.id, str)
                                if ok then
                                    Properties._matError = nil
                                    Properties._matErrorNodeId = sel.id
                                else
                                    Properties._matError = err or "Invalid material"
                                    Properties._matErrorNodeId = sel.id
                                end
                            end
                        end,
                    })
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_mat_clr_" .. sel.id, bx + pad + matW + 4, fy, clrW, 21, "Clr", {
                        disabled = (matVal == ""),
                        tooltip = "Clear material override and restore default",
                    }) then
                        if actions.setNodeMaterial then
                            actions.setNodeMaterial(sel.id, "")
                            Properties._matError = nil
                        end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + 40

                if Properties._matError and Properties._matErrorNodeId == sel.id then
                    if curY + 16 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.danger)
                        render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY + 7, Properties._matError, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
                    end
                    curY = curY + 17
                end

                -- Model Info display
                if sel.appearance.model and sel.appearance.model ~= "" then
                    local shortMdl = string.getFileFromFilename(sel.appearance.model) or sel.appearance.model
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Model", shortMdl, Theme.C.textPrimary)
                    end
                    curY = curY + 19

                    local exists = game and game.modelExists and game.modelExists(sel.appearance.model)
                    if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Asset", exists and "Mounted" or "Not mounted", exists and Theme.C.success or Theme.C.warning)
                    end
                    curY = curY + 19

                    local entry = runtime:getEntry(sel.id)
                    if entry and entry.baseMin and entry.baseMax then
                        local bSize = entry.baseMax - entry.baseMin
                        if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                            Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Bounds",
                                string.format("%.0f x %.0f x %.0f", bSize[1], bSize[2], bSize[3]), Theme.C.textMuted)
                        end
                        curY = curY + 19
                    end
                end
                curY = drawAppearanceExtra(ui, state, runtime, sel, bx, curY, innerW, pad, rowH, by, visBottom, actions)
                curY = curY + 6
            end

            -- Phase 5: Pivot & Assembly
            curY = drawPivotSection(ui, state, sceneGraph, sel, bx, curY, innerW, headerW, pad, rowH, fieldH, by, visBottom, actions)

            -- Phases 9, 10, 11: Bones / VFX / Camera Inspector
            curY = drawSpecialInspector(ui, state, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, sel, bx, curY, innerW, headerW, pad, rowH, fieldH, by, visBottom, actions)
        end

        -- 2. Freecam Camera (live)
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.camera = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_camera", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Freecam Camera", state.sections.camera)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.camera then
            local c = state.camera
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local spd, spdChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_spd", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Move Speed", c.speed, Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.minSpeed, Constants.CAMERA_DEFAULTS.maxSpeed, 10, "%.0f u/s",
                    "Freecam base movement speed (Mouse wheel in viewport adjusts this)"
                )
                if spdChanged then c.speed = spd end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local bst, bstChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_bst", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Shift Boost", c.boostMult, 1.5, 6.0, 0.25, "x%.2f",
                    "Speed multiplier while holding Shift"
                )
                if bstChanged then c.boostMult = bst end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local sens, sensChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_sens", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Mouse Sens", c.sensitivity, 0.04, 0.50, 0.01, "%.2f",
                    "Mouse look sensitivity while holding Right Mouse Button"
                )
                if sensChanged then c.sensitivity = sens end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local fov, fovChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_fov", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Field of View", c.fov, 45, 115, 1, "%.0f deg",
                    "Viewport camera vertical field of view"
                )
                if fovChanged then c.fov = fov end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local sm, smChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_cam_sm", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Smoothing", c.smoothing, 4, 40, 1, "%.0f",
                    "Camera motion damping (40 = crisp / instant)"
                )
                if smChanged then c.smoothing = sm end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_cam_reset", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH, "Snap Camera to Player", {
                    icon = "camera",
                    tooltip = "Move Freecam back to your player's eye position",
                }) then
                    camera:resetToPlayer()
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 8
        end

        -- 3. Viewport Grid (live)
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.grid = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_grid", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Viewport Grid", state.sections.grid)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.grid then
            local g = state.grid

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_grid_en", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Ground Grid: " .. (g.enabled and "Visible" or "Hidden"),
                    { active = g.enabled, icon = "grid", tooltip = "Toggle 3D reference grid (Hotkey: G)" }
                ) then
                    g.enabled = not g.enabled
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local prevSp, nextSp = Widgets.stepper(
                    ui, "prop_grid_sp", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Cell Spacing", tostring(g.spacing) .. " u",
                    "Grid cell size in Hammer units"
                )
                if prevSp then g.spacing = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.GRID_SPACING_OPTIONS, g.spacing, -1) end
                if nextSp then g.spacing = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.GRID_SPACING_OPTIONS, g.spacing, 1) end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local newExt, extChanged = Widgets.slider(
                    ui, "prop_grid_ext", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Grid Radius", g.halfExtents, 4, 24, 1, "%.0f cells",
                    "Number of grid cells from origin in each direction"
                )
                if extChanged then g.halfExtents = newExt end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 8
        end

        -- 4. Studio Origin & HUD
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.origin = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_origin", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "Studio Origin & HUD", state.sections.origin)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.origin then
            local g = state.grid
            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local prevOr, nextOr = Widgets.stepper(
                    ui, "prop_origin_mode", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                    "Origin Anchor", string.upper(g.originMode),
                    "Center studio grid on Chip, Player, or World (0,0,0)"
                )
                if prevOr then
                    g.originMode = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.ORIGIN_MODES, g.originMode, -1)
                    actions.resolveOrigin()
                end
                if nextOr then
                    g.originMode = Utils.cycleOption(Constants.ORIGIN_MODES, g.originMode, 1)
                    actions.resolveOrigin()
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 4

            if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local halfW = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_triad", bx + pad, curY, halfW, rowH, "3D Triad", {
                    active = g.showOrientationGizmo,
                    tooltip = "Toggle top-right 3D axis orientation widget",
                }) then
                    g.showOrientationGizmo = not g.showOrientationGizmo
                end
                if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_telem", bx + pad + halfW + 4, curY, innerW - halfW - 4, rowH, "Telemetry", {
                    active = g.showTelemetry,
                    tooltip = "Toggle bottom-left performance telemetry pill",
                }) then
                    g.showTelemetry = not g.showTelemetry
                end
            end
            curY = curY + rowH + 8
        end

        -- 5. StarfallEx Quotas & Permissions
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            state.sections.quotas = Widgets.sectionHeader(ui, "sec_quotas", bx, curY, headerW, 22, "StarfallEx Runtime", state.sections.quotas)
        end
        curY = curY + 26
        if state.sections.quotas then
            if curY + 95 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local now = ui._frameTime
                if now - Properties._rtCacheTime >= 0.2 or Properties._rtCpuStr == "" then
                    Properties._rtCacheTime = now
                    local cpuAvgFn = cpuAverage or quotaAverage
                    local cpuMaxFn = cpuMax or quotaMax
                    local cpuMs = cpuAvgFn and (cpuAvgFn() * 1000) or 0
                    local maxMs = cpuMaxFn and (cpuMaxFn() * 1000) or 15
                    local ramMb = ramUsed and (ramUsed() / 1024) or 0
                    local holosLeft = (hologram and hologram.hologramsLeft) and hologram.hologramsLeft() or 0
                    Properties._rtCpuStr = string.format("%.2f / %.1f ms", cpuMs, maxMs)
                    Properties._rtRamStr = string.format("%.1f MB", ramMb)
                    Properties._rtHolosStr = tostring(holosLeft)
                    Properties._rtCalcPerm = hasPermission("render.calcview")
                    Properties._rtHoloPerm = hasPermission("hologram.create")
                end
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "CPU Avg / Cap", Properties._rtCpuStr)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "RAM Used", Properties._rtRamStr)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "Holograms Left", Properties._rtHolosStr)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "render.calcview", Properties._rtCalcPerm and "OK" or "Denied",
                        Properties._rtCalcPerm and Theme.C.success or Theme.C.danger)
                end
                curY = curY + 19
                if curY + 18 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Widgets.keyValueRow(bx + pad, curY, innerW, 18, "hologram.create", Properties._rtHoloPerm and "OK" or "Pending",
                        Properties._rtHoloPerm and Theme.C.success or Theme.C.warning)
                end
                curY = curY + 21
                if curY + rowH >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "prop_debug_mode", bx + pad, curY, innerW, rowH,
                        "Debug Overlay: " .. (state.debugMode and "On" or "Off"), {
                        active = state.debugMode,
                        tooltip = "Toggle compact viewport Debug Mode overlay (errors & subsystem status)",
                    }) then
                        state.debugMode = not state.debugMode
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + rowH + 6
            else
                curY = curY + 126
            end
        end

        Properties._contentH = (curY - startY) + pad
        maxScroll = math.max(0, Properties._contentH - bh)

        if maxScroll > 0 then
            Properties._scrollY = Widgets.scrollbar(
                ui, "prop_sb",
                bx + bw - 8, by + 2, 6, bh - 4,
                Properties._scrollY, Properties._contentH, bh
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Properties = Properties
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 15: Timeline
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Utils = SOLACE.Utils
    local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
    local Widgets = SOLACE.Widgets

    local Timeline = {
        _lastFrame = -1,
        _lastTotal = -1,
        _lastFps = -1,
        _badgeStr = "",
        _scrollY = 0,
        _dragKf = nil, -- { nodeId, trackKey, kfIdx, beforeTracks, origTime }
    }
    local SEC_LABELS = {}
    for s = 0, 120 do
        SEC_LABELS[s] = tostring(s) .. "s"
    end

    local function cycleFps(current, dir)
        local opts = Constants.FPS_OPTIONS
        local idx = 2
        for i, v in ipairs(opts) do
            if v == current then idx = i break end
        end
        idx = ((idx - 1 + dir) % #opts) + 1
        return opts[idx]
    end

    local function cycleEasing(cur)
        local list = Constants.EASING_LIST
        local idx = 1
        for i, it in ipairs(list) do
            if it.id == cur then idx = i break end
        end
        return list[(idx % #list) + 1]
    end

    local function easingLabel(id)
        for _, it in ipairs(Constants.EASING_LIST) do
            if it.id == id then return it.label end
        end
        return "Linear"
    end

    local function drawDiamond(cx, cy, r, fillCol, outlineCol)
        Theme.setColor(fillCol)
        render.drawRectFast(cx - r, cy - r, r * 2, r * 2)
        if outlineCol then
            Theme.setColor(outlineCol)
            render.drawRectOutline(cx - r, cy - r, r * 2, r * 2, 1)
        end
    end

    function Timeline.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, rect, opts, actions)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end
        actions = actions or (SOLACE._ctx and SOLACE._ctx.actions) or {}
        local Animation = SOLACE.Animation

        local tl = state.timeline
        local frameIdx = math.floor(tl.currentTime * tl.fps + 0.5)
        local totalFrames = math.floor(tl.duration * tl.fps + 0.5)
        if frameIdx ~= Timeline._lastFrame or totalFrames ~= Timeline._lastTotal or tl.fps ~= Timeline._lastFps then
            Timeline._lastFrame = frameIdx
            Timeline._lastTotal = totalFrames
            Timeline._lastFps = tl.fps
            Timeline._badgeStr = string.format("%s / %s  |  F %d / %d (%d FPS)",
                Utils.formatTimecode(tl.currentTime),
                Utils.formatTimecode(tl.duration),
                frameIdx,
                totalFrames,
                tl.fps
            )
        end

        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Timeline", Timeline._badgeStr, {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "tl_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.timeline = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_timeline") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 160 or bh < 60 then return end

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        local stripH = 28
        local headerColW = math.clamp(math.floor(bw * 0.24), 210, 260)

        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgPanelAlt)
        render.drawRectFast(bx, by, bw, stripH)
        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
        render.drawLine(bx, by + stripH - 1, bx + bw, by + stripH - 1)
        render.drawLine(bx + headerColW, by, bx + headerColW, by + bh)

        -- Left transport & keyframe controls
        local tx = bx + 6
        local ty = by + 3
        local btnH = 22
        local selNode = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        local hasSelNode = (selNode ~= nil and selNode.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId())

        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_play", tx, ty, 24, btnH, tl.playing and "||" or ">", {
            active = tl.playing,
            tooltip = tl.playing and "Pause playback" or "Play animation sequence",
        }) then
            tl.playing = not tl.playing
        end
        tx = tx + 26
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_stop", tx, ty, 24, btnH, "[]", {
            tooltip = "Stop playback and rewind playhead to 0.00s",
        }) then
            tl.playing = false
            tl.currentTime = 0
            if Animation then Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, 0, state) end
        end
        tx = tx + 26
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_loop", tx, ty, 34, btnH, "Loop", {
            active = tl.loop,
            tooltip = "Toggle loop playback",
        }) then
            tl.loop = not tl.loop
        end
        tx = tx + 36

        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_add_key", tx, ty, 36, btnH, "+Key", {
            disabled = not hasSelNode,
            tooltip = "Add or update keyframe on active track at playhead (Undoable)",
        }) then
            if actions.addKeyframe then
                actions.addKeyframe(selNode.id, tl.selectedTrackKey or "transform.pos", tl.currentTime)
            end
        end
        tx = tx + 38

        local selTrk = (hasSelNode and selNode.tracks) and selNode.tracks[tl.selectedTrackKey or "transform.pos"] or nil
        local selKf = (selTrk and tl.selectedKeyframeIdx) and selTrk.keyframes[tl.selectedKeyframeIdx] or nil
        if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_del_key", tx, ty, 34, btnH, "-Key", {
            disabled = (selKf == nil),
            tooltip = "Delete selected keyframe (Undoable)",
        }) then
            if actions.removeKeyframe and selNode and tl.selectedKeyframeIdx then
                actions.removeKeyframe(selNode.id, tl.selectedTrackKey, tl.selectedKeyframeIdx)
                tl.selectedKeyframeIdx = nil
            end
        end
        tx = tx + 36

        local easeId = (selKf and selKf.easing) or tl.defaultEasing or "linear"
        if tx + 52 <= bx + headerColW - 4 then
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_ease", tx, ty, 48, btnH, easingLabel(easeId), {
                tooltip = selKf and "Cycle easing on selected keyframe" or "Cycle default easing for new keyframes",
            }) then
                local nextE = cycleEasing(easeId).id
                if selKf and actions.setKeyframeEasing then
                    actions.setKeyframeEasing(selNode.id, tl.selectedTrackKey, tl.selectedKeyframeIdx, nextE)
                else
                    tl.defaultEasing = nextE
                end
            end
        end

        -- Right interactive Time Ruler + compact FPS/Dur/Zoom strip on right edge
        local rulerX = bx + headerColW + 1
        local rightToolsW = (bw >= 760) and 172 or 0
        local rulerW = math.max(40, bw - headerColW - 1 - rightToolsW)
        local rulerY = by
        local trackAreaY = by + stripH
        local trackAreaH = math.max(0, bh - stripH)

        if rightToolsW > 0 then
            local rx = bx + bw - rightToolsW + 4
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_fps", rx, ty, 46, btnH, tostring(tl.fps) .. "f", {
                tooltip = "Cycle Timeline Frame Rate (24 / 30 / 60 FPS)",
            }) then
                tl.fps = cycleFps(tl.fps, 1)
            end
            rx = rx + 48
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_dur_minus", rx, ty, 20, btnH, "-", {
                tooltip = "Decrease sequence duration (-5s)",
            }) then
                tl.duration = math.max(2, tl.duration - 5)
                tl.currentTime = math.min(tl.currentTime, tl.duration)
            end
            rx = rx + 21
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_dur_plus", rx, ty, 20, btnH, "+", {
                tooltip = "Increase sequence duration (+5s)",
            }) then
                tl.duration = math.min(120, tl.duration + 5)
            end
            rx = rx + 23
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_zoom_out", rx, ty, 24, btnH, "z-", {
                tooltip = "Zoom Out Timeline",
            }) then
                tl.zoom = math.clamp(tl.zoom * 0.8, 25, 320)
            end
            rx = rx + 25
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_zoom_in", rx, ty, 24, btnH, "z+", {
                tooltip = "Zoom In Timeline",
            }) then
                tl.zoom = math.clamp(tl.zoom * 1.25, 25, 320)
            end
        end

        local trackRulerW = math.max(40, bw - headerColW - 1)

        -- Wheel zoom inside ruler/tracks
        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(rulerX, by, trackRulerW, bh) and ui:isMouseInClip() then
            local factor = ui.wheelDelta > 0 and 1.15 or 0.87
            tl.zoom = math.clamp(tl.zoom * factor, 25, 320)
        end

        -- Ruler scrubbing (click or drag anywhere on ruler)
        local rulerHot, rulerCap = ui:hitTest("tl_ruler", rulerX, rulerY, rulerW, stripH)
        if rulerHot then
            ui:setTooltip("Click or drag to scrub playhead (Mouse wheel to zoom)")
            if ui.leftPressed then
                ui:capture("tl_ruler")
            end
        end
        if rulerCap and ui.leftDown then
            local t = tl.scrollTime + (ui.mx - rulerX - 10) / tl.zoom
            tl.currentTime = math.clamp(math.round(t, 3), 0, tl.duration)
            if Animation then
                Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, tl.currentTime, state)
            end
        end

        -- Handle active keyframe horizontal drag
        if Timeline._dragKf then
            local dk = Timeline._dragKf
            local dNode = sceneGraph:getNode(dk.nodeId)
            local dTrk = dNode and dNode.tracks and dNode.tracks[dk.trackKey]
            local dKf = dTrk and dTrk.keyframes and dTrk.keyframes[dk.kfIdx]
            if dKf and ui.leftDown then
                local t = tl.scrollTime + (ui.mx - rulerX - 10) / tl.zoom
                dKf.time = math.clamp(math.round(t, 2), 0, tl.duration)
                tl.currentTime = dKf.time
                if Animation then Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, tl.currentTime, state) end
            else
                if dTrk and Animation then
                    Animation.sortTrack(dTrk)
                end
                if actions.commitTrackSnapshot and dNode and dk.beforeTracks then
                    actions.commitTrackSnapshot(dk.nodeId, "Move Keyframe", dk.beforeTracks)
                end
                Timeline._dragKf = nil
                ui:release("tl_kf_drag")
            end
        end

        ui:pushClip(rulerX, by, trackRulerW, bh)

        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgInput)
        render.drawRectFast(rulerX, rulerY, rulerW, stripH - 1)

        local originPX = rulerX + 10 - tl.scrollTime * tl.zoom
        local durPX = originPX + tl.duration * tl.zoom

        local spanX1 = math.max(rulerX, originPX)
        local spanX2 = math.min(rulerX + trackRulerW, durPX)
        if spanX2 > spanX1 then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgPanel, 220)
            render.drawRectFast(spanX1, trackAreaY, spanX2 - spanX1, trackAreaH)
        end

        local secStep = tl.zoom < 50 and 2 or 1
        local subDiv = tl.zoom >= 110 and 4 or (tl.zoom >= 65 and 2 or 1)
        local minSec = math.max(0, math.floor((rulerX - 20 - originPX) / tl.zoom))
        local maxSec = math.min(math.ceil(tl.duration), math.ceil((rulerX + trackRulerW + 20 - originPX) / tl.zoom))

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        for sec = minSec, maxSec do
            local sx = originPX + sec * tl.zoom
            if sec % secStep == 0 then
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
                render.drawLine(sx, rulerY + 12, sx, rulerY + stripH - 2)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(sx + 3, rulerY + 4, SEC_LABELS[sec] or (sec .. "s"), TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 110)
                render.drawLine(sx, trackAreaY, sx, trackAreaY + trackAreaH)
            else
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
                render.drawLine(sx, rulerY + 18, sx, rulerY + stripH - 2)
            end
            if subDiv > 1 and sec < tl.duration then
                for sub = 1, subDiv - 1 do
                    local subX = sx + (sub / subDiv) * tl.zoom
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 140)
                    render.drawLine(subX, rulerY + 21, subX, rulerY + stripH - 2)
                end
            end
        end

        if durPX >= rulerX and durPX <= rulerX + trackRulerW then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.warning, 160)
            render.drawLine(durPX, rulerY, durPX, by + bh)
        end

        -- Build track rows: property tracks for selected node, or node summary rows
        local rowH = 22
        local trackRows = {}
        if hasSelNode and Animation then
            local tDefs = Animation.getTrackListForNode(selNode, state)
            for i = 1, #tDefs do
                local td = tDefs[i]
                local trk = selNode.tracks and selNode.tracks[td.key] or nil
                trackRows[#trackRows + 1] = {
                    isProp = true,
                    node = selNode,
                    trackKey = td.key,
                    label = selNode.name .. " : " .. td.label,
                    track = trk,
                    accent = td.color or Theme.C.accent,
                }
            end
        else
            local rows = sceneGraph:getFlatHierarchy("")
            local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
            for _, item in ipairs(rows) do
                if item.node.id ~= rootId then
                    trackRows[#trackRows + 1] = {
                        isProp = false,
                        node = item.node,
                        label = item.node.name,
                    }
                end
            end
        end

        local rowY = trackAreaY + 4
        for idx, trRow in ipairs(trackRows) do
            if rowY + rowH <= by + bh - 2 then
                local isActiveTrack = trRow.isProp and (tl.selectedTrackKey == trRow.trackKey)
                if isActiveTrack then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentSoft, 70)
                    render.drawRectFast(rulerX, rowY, trackRulerW, rowH)
                end
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 90)
                render.drawLine(rulerX, rowY + rowH, rulerX + trackRulerW, rowY + rowH)

                if trRow.isProp and trRow.track and trRow.track.keyframes then
                    local kfs = trRow.track.keyframes
                    local trkEnabled = (trRow.track.enabled ~= false)
                    for ki = 1, #kfs do
                        local kf = kfs[ki]
                        local kx = math.floor(originPX + (kf.time or 0) * tl.zoom)
                        local ky = math.floor(rowY + rowH * 0.5)
                        if kx >= rulerX - 6 and kx <= rulerX + trackRulerW + 6 then
                            local isSelKf = isActiveTrack and (tl.selectedKeyframeIdx == ki)
                            local kHot = ui:hitTest("tl_kf_" .. trRow.trackKey .. "_" .. ki, kx - 5, ky - 6, 11, 13)
                            local col = not trkEnabled and Theme.C.textDisabled
                                or (isSelKf and Theme.C.warning or (kHot and Theme.C.textPrimary or (trRow.accent or Theme.C.accentBright)))
                            drawDiamond(kx, ky, isSelKf and 5 or 4, col, Theme.C.bgApp)
                            if kHot then
                                ui:setTooltip(string.format("%s @ %.2fs (%s) — Drag to move, RMB to delete", trRow.trackKey, kf.time or 0, easingLabel(kf.easing)))
                                if ui.leftPressed then
                                    tl.selectedTrackKey = trRow.trackKey
                                    tl.selectedKeyframeIdx = ki
                                    tl.currentTime = kf.time or 0
                                    if Animation then
                                        Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, tl.currentTime, state)
                                        Timeline._dragKf = {
                                            nodeId = trRow.node.id,
                                            trackKey = trRow.trackKey,
                                            kfIdx = ki,
                                            beforeTracks = Animation.cloneTracksSnapshot(trRow.node.tracks),
                                        }
                                        ui:capture("tl_kf_drag")
                                    end
                                elseif ui.rightPressed and actions.removeKeyframe then
                                    actions.removeKeyframe(trRow.node.id, trRow.trackKey, ki)
                                    tl.selectedKeyframeIdx = nil
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                elseif not trRow.isProp and trRow.node.tracks then
                    -- Summary diamond markers for all tracks of this node
                    for _, trk in pairs(trRow.node.tracks) do
                        if trk.keyframes then
                            for ki = 1, #trk.keyframes do
                                local kx = math.floor(originPX + (trk.keyframes[ki].time or 0) * tl.zoom)
                                if kx >= rulerX and kx <= rulerX + trackRulerW then
                                    drawDiamond(kx, math.floor(rowY + rowH * 0.5), 3, Theme.C.accent, nil)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                rowY = rowY + rowH
            end
        end

        -- Playhead scrubber line + handle (drawn above keyframes)
        local playX = math.floor(originPX + tl.currentTime * tl.zoom)
        if playX >= rulerX and playX <= rulerX + trackRulerW then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accent)
            render.drawLine(playX, rulerY + 4, playX, by + bh)
            render.drawRectFast(playX - 5, rulerY + 3, 11, 10)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawRectOutline(playX - 5, rulerY + 3, 11, 10, 1)
        end

        ui:popClip()

        -- Left track label list (clipped to headerColW)
        ui:pushClip(bx, trackAreaY, headerColW, trackAreaH)
        local lRowY = trackAreaY + 4
        for idx, trRow in ipairs(trackRows) do
            if lRowY + rowH <= by + bh - 2 then
                local isSel = trRow.isProp and (tl.selectedTrackKey == trRow.trackKey)
                    or ((not trRow.isProp) and (trRow.node.id == (selNode and selNode.id)))
                local rHot = ui:hitTest("tl_lrow_" .. idx, bx + 2, lRowY, headerColW - 4, rowH)

                if isSel then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgSelectionRow)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, lRowY, headerColW - 4, rowH)
                elseif rHot then
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.bgControlHover, 120)
                    render.drawRectFast(bx + 2, lRowY, headerColW - 4, rowH)
                end

                if trRow.isProp then
                    local en = (not trRow.track) or (trRow.track.enabled ~= false)
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "tl_ten_" .. trRow.trackKey, bx + 4, lRowY + 2, 22, rowH - 4, en and "On" or "--", {
                        active = en,
                        subtle = true,
                        tooltip = "Enable/disable track evaluation",
                    }) then
                        if actions.toggleTrackEnabled then
                            actions.toggleTrackEnabled(trRow.node.id, trRow.trackKey)
                        end
                    end
                    if rHot and ui.leftPressed then
                        tl.selectedTrackKey = trRow.trackKey
                        tl.selectedKeyframeIdx = nil
                    end
                    Theme.setFont(isSel and Theme.Fonts.BodyBold or Theme.Fonts.Body)
                    Theme.setColor(isSel and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + 30, lRowY + rowH * 0.5, trRow.label, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)

                    local kfCount = (trRow.track and trRow.track.keyframes) and #trRow.track.keyframes or 0
                    if kfCount > 0 then
                        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
                        render.drawSimpleText(bx + headerColW - 8, lRowY + rowH * 0.5, tostring(kfCount), TEXT_ALIGN.RIGHT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
                    end
                else
                    if rHot and ui.leftPressed then
                        sceneGraph:setSelectedId(trRow.node.id)
                    end
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Body)
                    Theme.setColor(isSel and Theme.C.textPrimary or Theme.C.textSecondary)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + 10, lRowY + rowH * 0.5, trRow.label, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
