                end

                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border, 90)
                render.drawLine(bx, lRowY + rowH, bx + headerColW, lRowY + rowH)
                lRowY = lRowY + rowH
            end
        end
        ui:popClip()

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Timeline = Timeline
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 16: Animation system (Phase 8)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local IR        = SOLACE.IR

    local Animation = {
        ready = true,
        phase = 8,
        MAX_KEYFRAMES_PER_TRACK = 256,
    }

    function Animation.applyEasing(u, ease)
        u = math.clamp(u or 0, 0, 1)
        if ease == "step" then
            return (u >= 1) and 1 or 0
        elseif ease == "smooth" then
            return u * u * (3 - 2 * u)
        elseif ease == "easeIn" or ease == "inQuad" then
            return u * u
        elseif ease == "easeOut" or ease == "outQuad" then
            return 1 - (1 - u) * (1 - u)
        elseif ease == "easeInOut" or ease == "inOutQuad" then
            return (u < 0.5) and (2 * u * u) or (1 - ((-2 * u + 2) ^ 2) * 0.5)
        elseif ease == "inCubic" then
            return u * u * u
        elseif ease == "outCubic" then
            return 1 - ((1 - u) ^ 3)
        elseif ease == "inOutCubic" then
            return (u < 0.5) and (4 * u * u * u) or (1 - ((-2 * u + 2) ^ 3) * 0.5)
        end
        return u
    end

    function Animation.cloneValue(v)
        if type(v) == "table" then
            local out = {}
            for i = 1, #v do out[i] = v[i] end
            return out
        end
        return v
    end

    function Animation.valuesEqual(a, b)
        if type(a) ~= type(b) then return false end
        if type(a) == "number" then return math.abs(a - b) <= 1e-4 end
        if type(a) == "table" then
            if #a ~= #b then return false end
            for i = 1, #a do
                if math.abs((a[i] or 0) - (b[i] or 0)) > 1e-4 then return false end
            end
            return true
        end
        return a == b
    end

    local function isAngleTrack(trackKey)
        return trackKey == "transform.ang" or string.sub(trackKey, -4) == ".ang"
    end

    function Animation.interpolateValue(a, b, u, trackKey)
        if type(a) == "number" and type(b) == "number" then
            return a + (b - a) * u
        end
        if type(a) == "table" and type(b) == "table" then
            local out = {}
            local isAng = isAngleTrack(trackKey)
            local isCol = (trackKey == "appearance.color")
            local n = math.max(#a, #b)
            for i = 1, n do
                local va = a[i] or 0
                local vb = b[i] or 0
                if isAng then
                    local diff = math.normalizeAngle(vb - va)
                    out[i] = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(va + diff * u), 2)
                elseif isCol then
                    out[i] = math.clamp(math.floor(va + (vb - va) * u + 0.5), 0, 255)
                else
                    out[i] = math.round(va + (vb - va) * u, 3)
                end
            end
            return out
        end
        return (u >= 1) and b or a
    end

    function Animation.sortTrack(track)
        if not track or not track.keyframes then return end
        table.sort(track.keyframes, function(a, b)
            return (a.time or 0) < (b.time or 0)
        end)
    end

    function Animation.evaluateTrack(track, t, trackKey)
        if not track or track.enabled == false then return nil end
        local kfs = track.keyframes
        if not kfs or #kfs == 0 then return nil end
        if #kfs == 1 or t <= (kfs[1].time or 0) then
            return Animation.cloneValue(kfs[1].value)
        end
        local last = kfs[#kfs]
        if t >= (last.time or 0) then
            return Animation.cloneValue(last.value)
        end
        for i = 1, #kfs - 1 do
            local k1 = kfs[i]
            local k2 = kfs[i + 1]
            local t1 = k1.time or 0
            local t2 = k2.time or 0
            if t >= t1 and t <= t2 then
                local span = t2 - t1
                if span <= 1e-6 then
                    return Animation.cloneValue(k2.value)
                end
                local rawU = (t - t1) / span
                local ease = k1.easing or "linear"
                local u = Animation.applyEasing(rawU, ease)
                return Animation.interpolateValue(k1.value, k2.value, u, trackKey)
            end
        end
        return Animation.cloneValue(last.value)
    end

    function Animation.getTrackListForNode(node, state)
        if not node then return {} end
        local list = {
            { key = "transform.pos",    label = "Position", color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.axisX },
            { key = "transform.ang",    label = "Rotation", color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.axisY },
            { key = "transform.scale",  label = "Scale",    color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.axisZ },
            { key = "appearance.color", label = "Color",    color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.warning },
        }
        if node.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA then
            list[#list + 1] = { key = "camera.fov", label = "Cam FOV", color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.accentBright }
        end
        local seen = {}
        for _, it in ipairs(list) do seen[it.key] = true end

        if state and state.selectedBoneIdx ~= nil then
            local bKey = "bones." .. tostring(state.selectedBoneIdx) .. ".ang"
            seen[bKey] = true
            list[#list + 1] = { key = bKey, label = "Bone #" .. tostring(state.selectedBoneIdx), color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.accent }
        end
        if node.tracks then
            for k in pairs(node.tracks) do
                if not seen[k] then
                    seen[k] = true
                    list[#list + 1] = { key = k, label = k, color = SOLACE.Theme and SOLACE.Theme.C.textSecondary }
                end
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    function Animation.captureNodeValue(node, trackKey)
        if not node then return nil end
        if trackKey == "transform.pos" then return IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pos) end
        if trackKey == "transform.ang" then return IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.ang) end
        if trackKey == "transform.scale" then return IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.scale) end
        if trackKey == "appearance.color" then return IR.cloneColor(node.appearance.color) end
        if trackKey == "camera.fov" and node.camera then return node.camera.fov or 80 end
        local bIdxStr = string.match(trackKey, "^bones%.(%d+)%.ang$")
        if bIdxStr then
            local bi = tonumber(bIdxStr)
            local b = node.bones and (node.bones[bi] or node.bones[bIdxStr])
            return IR.cloneVec3((b and b.ang) or { 0, 0, 0 })
        end
        return nil
    end

    function Animation.applyNodeValue(node, trackKey, val)
        if not node or val == nil then return false end
        if trackKey == "transform.pos" and type(val) == "table" then
            local p = node.transform.pos
            if Animation.valuesEqual(p, val) then return false end
            p[1], p[2], p[3] = val[1] or 0, val[2] or 0, val[3] or 0
            return true
        elseif trackKey == "transform.ang" and type(val) == "table" then
            local a = node.transform.ang
            if Animation.valuesEqual(a, val) then return false end
            a[1], a[2], a[3] = val[1] or 0, val[2] or 0, val[3] or 0
            return true
        elseif trackKey == "transform.scale" and type(val) == "table" then
            local s = node.transform.scale
            if Animation.valuesEqual(s, val) then return false end
            local mn, mx = Constants.MIN_SCALE, Constants.MAX_SCALE
            s[1] = math.clamp(val[1] or 1, mn, mx)
            s[2] = math.clamp(val[2] or 1, mn, mx)
            s[3] = math.clamp(val[3] or 1, mn, mx)
            return true
        elseif trackKey == "appearance.color" and type(val) == "table" then
            local c = node.appearance.color
            if Animation.valuesEqual(c, val) then return false end
            c[1] = math.clamp(math.floor((val[1] or 255) + 0.5), 0, 255)
            c[2] = math.clamp(math.floor((val[2] or 255) + 0.5), 0, 255)
            c[3] = math.clamp(math.floor((val[3] or 255) + 0.5), 0, 255)
            c[4] = math.clamp(math.floor((val[4] or 255) + 0.5), 0, 255)
            return true
        elseif trackKey == "camera.fov" and type(val) == "number" and node.camera then
            local nf = math.clamp(math.round(val, 1), 20, 130)
            if math.abs((node.camera.fov or 80) - nf) <= 1e-3 then return false end
            node.camera.fov = nf
            return true
        end
        local bIdxStr = string.match(trackKey, "^bones%.(%d+)%.ang$")
        if bIdxStr and type(val) == "table" then
            local bi = tonumber(bIdxStr)
            node.bones = node.bones or {}
            local b = node.bones[bi] or { pos = { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = { 0, 0, 0 }, scale = { 1, 1, 1 } }
            node.bones[bi] = b
            if Animation.valuesEqual(b.ang, val) then return false end
            b.ang[1], b.ang[2], b.ang[3] = val[1] or 0, val[2] or 0, val[3] or 0
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    function Animation.cloneTracksSnapshot(tracks)
        local out = {}
        for k, trk in pairs(tracks or {}) do
            local kfs = {}
            for i = 1, #(trk.keyframes or {}) do
                local kf = trk.keyframes[i]
                kfs[i] = {
                    time = kf.time,
                    value = Animation.cloneValue(kf.value),
                    easing = kf.easing or "linear",
                }
            end
            out[k] = {
                property = trk.property or k,
                enabled = trk.enabled ~= false,
                keyframes = kfs,
            }
        end
        return out
    end

    function Animation.addOrUpdateKeyframe(node, trackKey, time, value, easing)
        if not node or not trackKey then return nil end
        node.tracks = node.tracks or {}
        local trk = node.tracks[trackKey]
        if not trk then
            trk = IR.createTrack(trackKey)
            node.tracks[trackKey] = trk
        end
        local t = math.round(math.max(0, time or 0), 3)
        local val = Animation.cloneValue(value ~= nil and value or Animation.captureNodeValue(node, trackKey))
        if val == nil then return nil end

        for i = 1, #trk.keyframes do
            if math.abs((trk.keyframes[i].time or 0) - t) <= 0.015 then
                trk.keyframes[i].time = t
                trk.keyframes[i].value = val
                if easing then trk.keyframes[i].easing = easing end
                Animation.sortTrack(trk)
                return i
            end
        end

        if #trk.keyframes >= Animation.MAX_KEYFRAMES_PER_TRACK then
            return nil
        end
        local kf = IR.createKeyframe(t, val, easing or "linear")
        trk.keyframes[#trk.keyframes + 1] = kf
        Animation.sortTrack(trk)
        for i = 1, #trk.keyframes do
            if trk.keyframes[i] == kf then return i end
        end
        return #trk.keyframes
    end

    function Animation.removeKeyframe(node, trackKey, idx)
        local trk = node and node.tracks and node.tracks[trackKey]
        if not trk or not trk.keyframes or not trk.keyframes[idx] then return false end
        table.remove(trk.keyframes, idx)
        return true
    end

    function Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, t, state)
        if not sceneGraph then return 0 end
        local changedCount = 0
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local nodes = sceneGraph:getAllNodes()
        for id, node in pairs(nodes) do
            if id ~= rootId and node.tracks then
                local nodeChanged = false
                for trackKey, trk in pairs(node.tracks) do
                    if trk and trk.enabled ~= false and trk.keyframes and #trk.keyframes > 0 then
                        local val = Animation.evaluateTrack(trk, t, trackKey)
                        if val ~= nil and Animation.applyNodeValue(node, trackKey, val) then
                            nodeChanged = true
                        end
                    end
                end
                if nodeChanged then
                    sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
                    changedCount = changedCount + 1
                end
            end
        end
        return changedCount
    end

    function Animation.stepPlayback(state, sceneGraph, dt)
        local tl = state and state.timeline
        if not tl then return end
        if tl.playing then
            tl.currentTime = tl.currentTime + dt
            if tl.currentTime >= tl.duration then
                if tl.loop then
                    tl.currentTime = tl.currentTime % math.max(0.01, tl.duration)
                else
                    tl.currentTime = tl.duration
                    tl.playing = false
                end
            end
            Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, tl.currentTime, state)
        end
    end

    function Animation.getCinematicCameraPose(sceneGraph, camId, t)
        local node = sceneGraph and sceneGraph:getNode(camId)
        if not node or node.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA then return nil end
        local wPos, wAng = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, camId)
        local cam = node.camera or {}
        local fov = cam.fov or 80

        if cam.lookAtNodeId then
            local targetNode = sceneGraph:getNode(cam.lookAtNodeId)
            if targetNode then
                local tPos = SOLACE.Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, targetNode.id)
                local dir = tPos - wPos
                if dir:getLength() > 0.01 then
                    local dn = dir:getNormalized()
                    local pitch = math.deg(math.asin(-math.clamp(dn[3], -1, 1)))
                    local yaw = math.deg(math.atan2(dn[2], dn[1]))
                    wAng = Angle(pitch, yaw, wAng[3] or 0)
                end
            end
        end

        local amp = cam.shakeAmplitude or 0
        local freq = cam.shakeFrequency or 8
        local dur = cam.shakeDuration or 0
        if amp > 0 and (dur <= 0 or t <= dur) then
            local sx = math.sin(t * freq * 1.13) * amp
            local sy = math.cos(t * freq * 1.47) * amp * 0.7
            wAng = Angle(wAng[1] + sx, wAng[2] + sy, wAng[3])
        end

        return wPos, wAng, fov
    end

    SOLACE.Animation = Animation
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 17: Asset browser & Incremental Asset Scanner (Phase 6)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local Utils = SOLACE.Utils
    local Theme = SOLACE.Theme
    local Widgets = SOLACE.Widgets

    local AssetScanner = {
        ready = true,
        phase = 6,
        MAX_INDEXED = 600,
        MAX_DEPTH = 3,
        scanning = false,
        status = "Idle (Starter + Scan ready)",
        errorMsg = nil,
        models = {},
        materials = {},
        sprites = {},
        _queue = {},
        _scannedCount = 0,
        _worldRagdolls = {},
    }

    local DEFAULT_SCAN_ROOTS = {
        { dir = "models/props_c17/",     kind = "model",    depth = 0 },
        { dir = "models/props_junk/",    kind = "model",    depth = 0 },
        { dir = "models/hunter/blocks/", kind = "model",    depth = 0 },
        { dir = "models/props_combine/", kind = "model",    depth = 0 },
        { dir = "materials/models/",     kind = "material", depth = 0 },
        { dir = "materials/sprites/",    kind = "sprite",   depth = 0 },
    }

    local function pushIndexed(list, pathStr, kind)
        if #list >= AssetScanner.MAX_INDEXED then return end
        local clean = string.gsub(pathStr, "^/+", "")
        local fname = string.getFileFromFilename(clean) or clean
        local name = string.stripExtension(fname)
        list[#list + 1] = {
            name = (name ~= "" and name or clean),
            path = clean,
            kind = kind,
        }
    end

    function AssetScanner.startScan(customRoot)
        if not file or not file.findInGame then
            AssetScanner.scanning = false
            AssetScanner.errorMsg = "file.findInGame unavailable"
            AssetScanner.status = "Unavailable"
            return false
        end
        if hasPermission and not hasPermission("file.findInGame") then
            AssetScanner.scanning = false
            AssetScanner.errorMsg = "Permission 'file.findInGame' not granted"
            AssetScanner.status = "Permission required"
            return false
        end
        AssetScanner.models = {}
        AssetScanner.materials = {}
        AssetScanner.sprites = {}
        AssetScanner._queue = {}
        AssetScanner._scannedCount = 0
        AssetScanner.errorMsg = nil
        AssetScanner.scanning = true
        AssetScanner.status = "Scanning..."

        local root = string.trim(customRoot or "")
        if root ~= "" then
            root = string.gsub(root, "\\", "/")
            if string.sub(root, -1) ~= "/" then root = root .. "/" end
            local kind = string.find(root, "^materials/sprites/") and "sprite"
                or (string.find(root, "^materials/") and "material" or "model")
            AssetScanner._queue[1] = { dir = root, kind = kind, depth = 0 }
        else
            for i = 1, #DEFAULT_SCAN_ROOTS do
                local r = DEFAULT_SCAN_ROOTS[i]
                AssetScanner._queue[i] = { dir = r.dir, kind = r.kind, depth = r.depth }
            end
        end
        return true
    end

    function AssetScanner.stopScan()
        AssetScanner.scanning = false
        AssetScanner._queue = {}
        AssetScanner.status = string.format("Indexed %d mdl / %d mat / %d spr",
            #AssetScanner.models, #AssetScanner.materials, #AssetScanner.sprites)
    end

    function AssetScanner.stepScan(budgetDirs)
        if not AssetScanner.scanning then return end
        if quotaLeft and quotaLeft() < 0.0025 then return end
        budgetDirs = budgetDirs or 2
        local done = 0
        while done < budgetDirs and #AssetScanner._queue > 0 do
            local item = table.remove(AssetScanner._queue, 1)
            done = done + 1
            AssetScanner._scannedCount = AssetScanner._scannedCount + 1
            local ok, files, dirs = pcall(file.findInGame, item.dir .. "*", "nameasc")
            if not ok then
                AssetScanner.errorMsg = tostring(files)
                AssetScanner.scanning = false
                AssetScanner.status = "Scan stopped: " .. AssetScanner.errorMsg
                return
            end
            if type(files) == "table" then
                for i = 1, #files do
                    local f = files[i]
                    local ext = string.lower(string.getExtensionFromFilename(f) or "")
                    if item.kind == "model" and ext == "mdl" then
                        pushIndexed(AssetScanner.models, item.dir .. f, "model")
                    elseif item.kind == "material" and ext == "vmt" then
                        local matPath = string.stripExtension(string.gsub(item.dir .. f, "^materials/", ""))
                        pushIndexed(AssetScanner.materials, matPath, "material")
                    elseif item.kind == "sprite" and ext == "vmt" then
                        local sprPath = string.stripExtension(string.gsub(item.dir .. f, "^materials/", ""))
                        pushIndexed(AssetScanner.sprites, sprPath, "sprite")
                    end
                end
            end
            if type(dirs) == "table" and item.depth < AssetScanner.MAX_DEPTH and (#AssetScanner._queue < 64) then
                for i = 1, math.min(#dirs, 12) do
                    local d = dirs[i]
                    if d ~= "." and d ~= ".." then
                        AssetScanner._queue[#AssetScanner._queue + 1] = {
                            dir = item.dir .. d .. "/",
                            kind = item.kind,
                            depth = item.depth + 1,
                        }
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if #AssetScanner._queue == 0 then
            AssetScanner.stopScan()
        else
            AssetScanner.status = string.format("Scanning (%d dirs, %d mdl, %d mat)",
                AssetScanner._scannedCount, #AssetScanner.models, #AssetScanner.materials)
        end
    end

    function AssetScanner.scanWorldRagdolls()
        AssetScanner._worldRagdolls = {}
        if not find or not find.byClass then return {} end
        if hasPermission and not hasPermission("find") then return {} end
        local ok, ents = pcall(find.byClass, "prop_ragdoll")
        if ok and type(ents) == "table" then
            for i = 1, math.min(#ents, 24) do
                local e = ents[i]
                if IsValid(e) then
                    local mdl = e:getModel() or "ragdoll"
                    local fname = string.stripExtension(string.getFileFromFilename(mdl) or mdl)
                    AssetScanner._worldRagdolls[#AssetScanner._worldRagdolls + 1] = {
                        ent = e,
                        name = fname,
                        model = mdl,
                    }
                end
            end
        end
        return AssetScanner._worldRagdolls
    end

    SOLACE.AssetScanner = AssetScanner

    local Toolbox = {
        _scrollY = 0,
        _contentH = 0,
        _viewH = 0,
        _lastQuery = nil,
        _filteredModels = nil,
        _filteredSprites = nil,
        _lastCustomPath = nil,
        _lastCustomOk = false,
        _lastCustomName = "Model",
        _scanFilter = "models/props_c17/",
    }

    local function toggleFavorite(state, kind, name, path)
        state.favorites = state.favorites or {}
        for i = #state.favorites, 1, -1 do
            if state.favorites[i].path == path and state.favorites[i].kind == kind then
                table.remove(state.favorites, i)
                return false
            end
        end
        state.favorites[#state.favorites + 1] = { kind = kind, name = name, path = path }
        return true
    end

    local function isFavorite(state, kind, path)
        for _, f in ipairs(state.favorites or {}) do
            if f.kind == kind and f.path == path then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function pushRecent(state, kind, name, path)
        state.recentAssets = state.recentAssets or {}
        for i = #state.recentAssets, 1, -1 do
            if state.recentAssets[i].path == path and state.recentAssets[i].kind == kind then
                table.remove(state.recentAssets, i)
            end
        end
        table.insert(state.recentAssets, 1, { kind = kind, name = name, path = path })
        while #state.recentAssets > 8 do
            table.remove(state.recentAssets)
        end
    end

    local function getFilteredModels(q)
        if Toolbox._lastQuery == q and Toolbox._filteredModels then
            return Toolbox._filteredModels
        end
        local list = {}
        for idx, item in ipairs(Constants.STARTER_MODELS) do
            local matches = (q == "")
                or string.find(string.lower(item.name), q, 1, true) ~= nil
                or string.find(string.lower(item.model), q, 1, true) ~= nil
            if matches and Utils.checkModelMounted(item.model) then
                list[#list + 1] = {
                    id = "tbx_mdl_" .. idx,
                    name = item.name,
                    model = item.model,
                    category = item.category or "Prop",
                    tip = "Spawn " .. item.model .. " in front of Freecam",
                }
            end
        end
        Toolbox._lastQuery = q
        Toolbox._filteredModels = list
        Toolbox._filteredSprites = nil
        return list
    end

    local function getFilteredSprites(q)
        if Toolbox._lastQuery == q and Toolbox._filteredSprites then
            return Toolbox._filteredSprites
        end
        local list = {}
        for idx, item in ipairs(Constants.STARTER_SPRITES) do
            if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(item.name), q, 1, true) ~= nil then
                list[#list + 1] = {
                    id = "tbx_spr_" .. idx,
                    name = item.name,
                    material = item.material,
                    tip = "Spawn 3D Sprite (" .. item.material .. ")",
                }
            end
        end
        Toolbox._lastQuery = q
        Toolbox._filteredSprites = list
        return list
    end

    function Toolbox.draw(ui, state, rect, actions, opts)
        if rect.w <= 0 or rect.h <= 0 then return end

        local bx, by, bw, bh
        if opts and opts.hideFrame then
            bx, by, bw, bh = rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h
        else
            bx, by, bw, bh = Widgets.panelFrame(rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, "Toolbox", "Create", {
                ui = ui,
                undockId = "tbx_undock",
                onUndock = function()
                    if state.floatingPanels then
                        state.floatingPanels.toolbox = true
                        if SOLACE.ImGui then SOLACE.ImGui.bringToFront("win_panel_toolbox") end
                    end
                end,
            })
        end
        if bw < 20 or bh < 40 then return end
        Toolbox._viewH = bh

        if ui.wheelDelta ~= 0 and ui:pointInRect(bx, by, bw, bh) then
            Toolbox._scrollY = Toolbox._scrollY - ui.wheelDelta * 26
        end
        local maxScroll = math.max(0, Toolbox._contentH - bh)
        Toolbox._scrollY = math.clamp(Toolbox._scrollY, 0, maxScroll)

        ui:pushClip(bx, by, bw, bh)

        local pad = Theme.Metrics.pad
        local innerW = bw - pad * 2 - (maxScroll > 0 and 10 or 0)
        local curY = by + pad - Toolbox._scrollY
        local startY = curY
        local visBottom = by + bh

        -- Search input
        local searchGetter = function() return state.toolboxSearch end
        local searchSetter = function(v) state.toolboxSearch = v end
        if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            Widgets.textBox(ui, "toolbox_search", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24,
                searchGetter, searchSetter, "Filter assets...", "Filter models, materials, sprites, effects")
        else
            ui:registerTextInput("toolbox_search", searchGetter, searchSetter)
        end
        curY = curY + 30

        -- Category buttons (2 columns when wide enough)
        local cols = innerW >= 196 and 2 or 1
        local gap = 4
        local cellW = math.floor((innerW - gap * (cols - 1)) / cols)
        local cellH = 24

        for i, cat in ipairs(Constants.ASSET_CATEGORIES) do
            local col = (i - 1) % cols
            local row = math.floor((i - 1) / cols)
            local cx = bx + pad + col * (cellW + gap)
            local cy = curY + row * (cellH + gap)
            local isSelected = (state.activeToolboxCategory == cat.id)

            if cy + cellH >= by and cy <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_cat_" .. cat.id, cx, cy, cellW, cellH, cat.label, {
                    icon = cat.icon,
                    active = isSelected,
                    tooltip = cat.desc,
                }) then
                    state.activeToolboxCategory = cat.id
                    Toolbox._scrollY = 0
                end
            end
        end

        local rows = math.ceil(#Constants.ASSET_CATEGORIES / cols)
        curY = curY + rows * (cellH + gap) + 8

        -- Hierarchy containers quick create
        if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            local halfW = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_new_group", bx + pad, curY, halfW, 22, "+ Group", {
                icon = "layers",
                tooltip = "Create a Group node in the Scene Hierarchy",
            }) then
                actions.spawnGroup()
            end
            if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_new_folder", bx + pad + halfW + 4, curY, innerW - halfW - 4, 22, "+ Folder", {
                icon = "layers",
                tooltip = "Create a Folder node in the Scene Hierarchy",
            }) then
                actions.spawnFolder()
            end
        end
        curY = curY + 28

        if curY >= by and curY <= visBottom then
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.border)
            render.drawLine(bx + pad, curY, bx + pad + innerW, curY)
        end
        curY = curY + 8

        local q = string.lower(string.trim(state.toolboxSearch or ""))

        local function drawAssetRow(btnId, label, pathStr, kind, iconName, tip, onActivate)
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local fav = isFavorite(state, kind, pathStr)
                local starW = 24
                if Widgets.button(ui, btnId, bx + pad, curY, innerW - starW - 2, 24, label, {
                    icon = iconName,
                    subtle = true,
                    tooltip = tip,
                }) then
                    onActivate()
                    pushRecent(state, kind, label, pathStr)
                end
                if Widgets.button(ui, btnId .. "_fav", bx + pad + innerW - starW, curY, starW, 24, fav and "*" or ".", {
                    active = fav,
                    subtle = true,
                    tooltip = fav and "Remove from Favorites" or "Add to Favorites",
                }) then
                    toggleFavorite(state, kind, label, pathStr)
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 26
        end

        -- Favorites & Recent quick strip inside Models/Materials/Sprites
        if (state.activeToolboxCategory == "models" or state.activeToolboxCategory == "materials" or state.activeToolboxCategory == "sprites")
            and state.favorites and #state.favorites > 0
        then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.warning)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Favorites (" .. #state.favorites .. ")", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16
            for fi, fav in ipairs(state.favorites) do
                if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(fav.name), q, 1, true) then
                    if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_fav_" .. fi, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 22, "* " .. fav.name, {
                            subtle = true,
                            tooltip = fav.path,
                        }) then
                            if fav.kind == "model" then
                                actions.spawnQuickModel(fav.name, fav.path)
                            elseif fav.kind == "sprite" then
                                actions.spawnSprite(fav.name, fav.path)
                            elseif fav.kind == "material" and actions.applyMaterialToSelected then
                                actions.applyMaterialToSelected(fav.path)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + 24
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 4
        end

        if state.activeToolboxCategory == "models" then
            -- Custom mounted .mdl path spawner
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Custom Mounted Model (.mdl)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 15

            local customGetter = function() return state.customModelPath end
            local customSetter = function(v) state.customModelPath = v end
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Widgets.textBox(ui, "tbx_custom_mdl", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24,
                    customGetter, customSetter, "models/...",
                    "Enter any mounted GMod model path (e.g. models/props_c17/oildrum001.mdl)")
            else
                ui:registerTextInput("tbx_custom_mdl", customGetter, customSetter)
            end
            curY = curY + 28

            local cpath = state.customModelPath or ""
            if cpath ~= Toolbox._lastCustomPath then
                Toolbox._lastCustomPath = cpath
                Toolbox._lastCustomOk = Utils.checkModelMounted(cpath)
                local fname = string.getFileFromFilename(cpath) or "Model"
                local cleanName = string.stripExtension(fname)
                Toolbox._lastCustomName = (cleanName ~= "" and cleanName or "Model")
            end

            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local customOk = Toolbox._lastCustomOk
                local spawnLabel = customOk and "Spawn Custom Model" or "Model Path Not Found"
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_spawn_custom", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, spawnLabel, {
                    icon = "cube",
                    disabled = not customOk,
                    tooltip = customOk and ("Spawn " .. cpath .. " in front of camera") or "Verify path starts with models/ and exists in GMod",
                }) then
                    actions.spawnQuickModel(Toolbox._lastCustomName, cpath)
                    pushRecent(state, "model", Toolbox._lastCustomName, cpath)
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 30

            -- Incremental Mounted Asset Scanner Controls (Phase 6)
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Mounted Asset Scanner", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 15
            local scanGet = function() return Toolbox._scanFilter end
            local scanSet = function(v) Toolbox._scanFilter = v end
            if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Widgets.textBox(ui, "tbx_scan_dir", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 22, scanGet, scanSet,
                    "models/props_c17/ (blank = all)", "Folder path to scan via file.findInGame")
            else
                ui:registerTextInput("tbx_scan_dir", scanGet, scanSet)
            end
            curY = curY + 25
            if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                local halfW = math.floor((innerW - 4) * 0.5)
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_scan_go", bx + pad, curY, halfW, 22,
                    AssetScanner.scanning and "Scanning..." or "Scan Folder", {
                    active = AssetScanner.scanning,
                    tooltip = "Incrementally scan mounted GMod files via file.findInGame",
                }) then
                    if not AssetScanner.scanning then
                        AssetScanner.startScan(Toolbox._scanFilter)
                    end
                end
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_scan_all", bx + pad + halfW + 4, curY, innerW - halfW - 4, 22,
                    AssetScanner.scanning and "Stop" or "Scan Defaults", {
                    tooltip = AssetScanner.scanning and "Stop background scan" or "Scan starter model/material/sprite directories",
                }) then
                    if AssetScanner.scanning then
                        AssetScanner.stopScan()
                    else
                        AssetScanner.startScan("")
                    end
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 25
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(AssetScanner.errorMsg and Theme.C.danger or Theme.C.textMuted)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, AssetScanner.status, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 18

            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Starter Models (Click to Spawn)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            local list = getFilteredModels(q)
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                drawAssetRow(entry.id, entry.name, entry.model, "model", "cube", entry.tip, function()
                    actions.spawnQuickModel(entry.name, entry.model)
                end)
            end

            if #AssetScanner.models > 0 then
                curY = curY + 4
                if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Scanned Models (" .. #AssetScanner.models .. ")", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
                end
                curY = curY + 16
                local shown = 0
                for idx, entry in ipairs(AssetScanner.models) do
                    if shown >= 60 then break end
                    if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(entry.path), q, 1, true) then
                        shown = shown + 1
                        drawAssetRow("tbx_smdl_" .. idx, entry.name, entry.path, "model", "cube", entry.path, function()
                            actions.spawnQuickModel(entry.name, entry.path)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "holograms" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Hologram Primitives (Click to Spawn)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            local list = getFilteredModels(q)
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                if entry.category == "Primitive" then
                    drawAssetRow("holo_" .. entry.id, entry.name, entry.model, "model", "cube", entry.model, function()
                        actions.spawnQuickModel(entry.name, entry.model)
                    end)
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 4
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "holo_new_grp", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, "+ Spawn Group Container", {
                    icon = "group",
                    subtle = true,
                    tooltip = "Spawn an empty Group container in scene",
                }) then
                    actions.spawnGroup()
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 26

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "sprites" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Starter Sprites (Click to Spawn)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            local list = getFilteredSprites(q)
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                drawAssetRow(entry.id, entry.name, entry.material, "sprite", "sparkle", entry.tip, function()
                    actions.spawnSprite(entry.name, entry.material)
                end)
            end

            if #AssetScanner.sprites > 0 then
                curY = curY + 4
                if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Scanned Sprites (" .. #AssetScanner.sprites .. ")", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
                end
                curY = curY + 16
                local shown = 0
                for idx, entry in ipairs(AssetScanner.sprites) do
                    if shown >= 50 then break end
                    if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(entry.path), q, 1, true) then
                        shown = shown + 1
                        drawAssetRow("tbx_sspr_" .. idx, entry.name, entry.path, "sprite", "sparkle", entry.path, function()
                            actions.spawnSprite(entry.name, entry.path)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "materials" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Materials (Click to Apply to Selected)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            for idx, matItem in ipairs(Constants.STARTER_MATERIALS) do
                if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(matItem.name), q, 1, true)
                    or string.find(string.lower(matItem.material), q, 1, true)
                then
                    drawAssetRow("tbx_mat_" .. idx, matItem.name, matItem.material, "material", "palette",
                        "Apply material: " .. (matItem.material ~= "" and matItem.material or "<Default>"),
                        function()
                            if actions.applyMaterialToSelected then
                                actions.applyMaterialToSelected(matItem.material)
                            end
                        end)
                end
            end

            if #AssetScanner.materials > 0 then
                curY = curY + 4
                if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                    Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
                    render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Scanned Materials (" .. #AssetScanner.materials .. ")", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
                end
                curY = curY + 16
                local shown = 0
                for idx, entry in ipairs(AssetScanner.materials) do
                    if shown >= 60 then break end
                    if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(entry.path), q, 1, true) then
                        shown = shown + 1
                        drawAssetRow("tbx_smat_" .. idx, entry.name, entry.path, "material", "palette", entry.path, function()
                            if actions.applyMaterialToSelected then
                                actions.applyMaterialToSelected(entry.path)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "effects" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "VFX & Lights (Click to Spawn)", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            for idx, preset in ipairs(Constants.VFX_PRESETS) do
                if q == "" or string.find(string.lower(preset.name), q, 1, true) then
                    if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_vfx_" .. idx, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, preset.name, {
                            icon = "sparkle",
                            subtle = true,
                            tooltip = "Spawn " .. preset.mode .. " VFX node",
                        }) then
                            if actions.spawnEffect then
                                actions.spawnEffect(preset.name, preset)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + 26
                end
            end

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "cameras" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Cinematic Cameras", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_cam_new", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, "+ Spawn Camera at View", {
                    icon = "camera",
                    tooltip = "Create a Cinematic Camera node at current Freecam pose",
                }) then
                    if actions.spawnCamera then
                        actions.spawnCamera("Camera")
                    end
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 28
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_cam_exit", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, "Return to Freecam", {
                    active = (state.activeCameraId == nil),
                    subtle = true,
                    tooltip = "Exit cinematic camera preview back to Freecam",
                }) then
                    state.activeCameraId = nil
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 28

        elseif state.activeToolboxCategory == "ragdolls" then
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textSecondary)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Articulated Hologram Actors", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16

            local actors = {
                { name = "Combine Soldier", model = "models/player/combine_soldier.mdl" },
                { name = "Dr. Kleiner",     model = "models/player/kleiner.mdl" },
                { name = "G-Man",           model = "models/player/gman_high.mdl" },
                { name = "Metrocop",        model = "models/player/police.mdl" },
            }
            for idx, act in ipairs(actors) do
                if Utils.checkModelMounted(act.model) then
                    if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                        if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_actor_" .. idx, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, act.name, {
                            icon = "cube",
                            subtle = true,
                            tooltip = "Spawn poseable bone-rigged hologram: " .. act.model,
                        }) then
                            actions.spawnQuickModel(act.name, act.model, Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL)
                        end
                    end
                    curY = curY + 26
                end
            end

            curY = curY + 6
            if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_scan_rag", bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, "Find World prop_ragdoll", {
                    icon = "search",
                    tooltip = "Scan nearby prop_ragdoll entities (requires 'find' permission)",
                }) then
                    AssetScanner.scanWorldRagdolls()
                end
            end
            curY = curY + 28
            for idx, r in ipairs(AssetScanner._worldRagdolls) do
                if curY + 24 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_wrag_" .. idx, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 24, "Link: " .. r.name, {
                        subtle = true,
                        tooltip = "Link world ragdoll entity to Scene Graph (" .. r.model .. ")",
                    }) then
                        if actions.linkWorldRagdoll then
                            actions.linkWorldRagdoll(r.name, r.model, r.ent)
                        end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + 26
            end
        end

        -- Recent Assets footer
        if state.recentAssets and #state.recentAssets > 0 then
            curY = curY + 6
            if curY + 15 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textMuted)
                render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, curY, "Recent Assets", TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
            end
            curY = curY + 16
            for ri, rec in ipairs(state.recentAssets) do
                if ri > 4 then break end
                if curY + 22 >= by and curY <= visBottom then
                    if Widgets.button(ui, "tbx_rec_" .. ri, bx + pad, curY, innerW, 22, rec.name, {
                        subtle = true,
                        tooltip = rec.path,
                    }) then
                        if rec.kind == "model" then
                            actions.spawnQuickModel(rec.name, rec.path)
                        elseif rec.kind == "sprite" then
                            actions.spawnSprite(rec.name, rec.path)
                        elseif rec.kind == "material" and actions.applyMaterialToSelected then
                            actions.applyMaterialToSelected(rec.path)
                        end
                    end
                end
                curY = curY + 24
            end
        end

        Toolbox._contentH = (curY - startY) + pad
        maxScroll = math.max(0, Toolbox._contentH - bh)

        if maxScroll > 0 then
            Toolbox._scrollY = Widgets.scrollbar(
                ui, "tbx_sb",
                bx + bw - 8, by + 2, 6, bh - 4,
                Toolbox._scrollY, Toolbox._contentH, bh
            )
        end

        ui:popClip()
    end

    SOLACE.Toolbox = Toolbox
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 17.5: Floating Dear ImGui windows & undocked panel hosts
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Theme    = SOLACE.Theme
    local ImGui    = SOLACE.ImGui
    local Toolbox  = SOLACE.Toolbox
    local Explorer = SOLACE.Explorer
    local Properties = SOLACE.Properties
    local Timeline = SOLACE.Timeline

    local Windows = {
        _demo = {
            chk = true,
            radio = 1,
            fval = 42.5,
            ival = 16,
            dval = 3.14,
            vec = { 10, 20, 30 },
            comboIdx = 1,
            text = "Hello ImGui!",
            progress = 0.68,
            selIdx = 2,
            clickCount = 0,
        },
        _consoleFilter = "",
        _consoleTab = "All",
    }

    local THEME_KEYS = { "dark", "classic", "light" }
    local THEME_LABELS = { "ImGui Dark", "ImGui Classic", "ImGui Light" }
    local COMBO_PRESETS = { "Default Studio", "Minimal Viewport", "High Contrast", "Cinematic" }
    local SELECTABLE_ITEMS = { "Scene Root", "Cube Hologram", "Key Light", "Camera Rig", "Timeline Track" }

    local function drawDemoWindow(ui, state)
        local d = Windows._demo
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_demo", "Dear ImGui Widget Showcase", state.windows.demo, {
            x = 290, y = 78, w = 390, h = 480, minW = 280, minH = 240,
        })
        if not open then
            state.windows.demo = false
        end
        if not vis then
            if open then ImGui.End() end
            return
        end

        ImGui.TextColored(Theme.C.accentBright, "SOLACE.ImGui Native Immediate-Mode UI")
        ImGui.TextDisabled("Drag titlebar, resize bottom-right grip, click to raise Z-order.")
        if ImGui.SmallButton("Open ImGui Theme & Style (Prefs)") then
            state.windows.settings = true
            ImGui.bringToFront("win_settings")
        end
        ImGui.Separator()

        if ImGui.BeginTabBar("demo_tabs") then
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Widgets") then
                if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Buttons & Toggles", true) then
                    if ImGui.Button("Action Button") then
                        d.clickCount = d.clickCount + 1
                        ImGui.Notify("ImGui Button", "Clicked " .. d.clickCount .. " time(s)", "info", 2.4)
                    end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.SmallButton("Reset##demo_rst") then
                        d.clickCount = 0
                        d.fval = 42.5
                        d.ival = 16
                    end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    ImGui.TextDisabled("Count: " .. d.clickCount)

                    d.chk = ImGui.Checkbox("Enable Feature Checkbox", d.chk)

                    if ImGui.RadioButton("Mode A", d.radio == 1) then d.radio = 1 end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.RadioButton("Mode B", d.radio == 2) then d.radio = 2 end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.RadioButton("Mode C", d.radio == 3) then d.radio = 3 end
                end

                ImGui.Spacing(4)
                if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Sliders, Drag & Dropdowns", true) then
                    d.fval = ImGui.SliderFloat("Float Slider", d.fval, 0, 100, "%.1f")
                    d.ival = ImGui.SliderInt("Int Slider", d.ival, 1, 64, "%d")
                    d.dval = ImGui.DragFloat("Drag Float", d.dval, 0.05, -100, 100, "%.2f")
                    local nx, ny, nz = ImGui.DragFloat3("Sample Vec3", d.vec[1], d.vec[2], d.vec[3], 0.5, "%.1f")
                    d.vec[1], d.vec[2], d.vec[3] = nx, ny, nz
                    d.comboIdx = ImGui.Combo("Preset", d.comboIdx, COMBO_PRESETS, 6)
                    d.text = ImGui.InputText("Text Input", d.text, "Type text...")
                    d.progress = ImGui.SliderFloat("Progress", d.progress, 0, 1, "%.2f")
                    ImGui.ProgressBar(d.progress, 0, 18, string.format("Bake %d%%", math.floor(d.progress * 100 + 0.5)))
                end
                ImGui.EndTabItem()
            end

            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Trees & Lists") then
                ImGui.Text("Hierarchical TreeNode Demo:")
                if ImGui.TreeNode("Studio Scene Root", true) then
                    ImGui.BulletText("Hologram Layer (active)")
                    if ImGui.TreeNode("Lighting Rig", false) then
                        ImGui.BulletText("Key Light (45 deg)")
                        ImGui.BulletText("Rim Light (cool)")
                        ImGui.TreePop()
                    end
                    ImGui.TreePop()
                end
                ImGui.Separator()
                ImGui.Text("Scrollable Child Selectables:")
                if ImGui.BeginChild("demo_list_child", 0, 130, true) then
                    for idx, name in ipairs(SELECTABLE_ITEMS) do
                        if ImGui.Selectable(name .. "##sel_" .. idx, d.selIdx == idx) then
                            d.selIdx = idx
                        end
                    end
                    ImGui.EndChild()
                end
                ImGui.EndTabItem()
            end

            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Notifications") then
                ImGui.TextWrapped("Trigger Dear ImGui toast notifications stacked cleanly over the viewport:")
                ImGui.Spacing(4)
                if ImGui.Button("Info Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Info Notice", "Viewport grid updated cleanly.", "info", 3.2)
                end
                ImGui.SameLine()
                if ImGui.Button("Success Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Operation Complete", "Scene node spawned in 3D space.", "success", 3.2)
                end
                if ImGui.Button("Warning Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Quota Advisory", "CPU budget at 65% threshold.", "warning", 3.5)
                end
                ImGui.SameLine()
                if ImGui.Button("Error Toast", 130) then
                    ImGui.Notify("Validation Error", "Sample error notification test.", "error", 3.5)
                end
                ImGui.EndTabItem()
            end

            ImGui.EndTabBar()
        end

        ImGui.End()
    end

    local function drawSettingsWindow(ui, state, layout)
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_settings", "Studio & ImGui Settings", state.windows.settings, {
            x = 700, y = 78, w = 360, h = 440, minW = 260, minH = 220,
        })
        if not open then
            state.windows.settings = false
        end
        if not vis then
            if open then ImGui.End() end
            return
        end

        if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Appearance & Dear ImGui Style", true) then
            local curThemeIdx = 1
            for i, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do
                if k == state.uiTheme then curThemeIdx = i break end
            end
            local newThemeIdx, themeChanged = ImGui.Combo("Theme", curThemeIdx, THEME_LABELS, 4)
            if themeChanged then
                state.uiTheme = THEME_KEYS[newThemeIdx] or "dark"
                Theme.applyTheme(state.uiTheme, state.uiRounding)
                ImGui.Notify("Theme Changed", "Applied " .. (THEME_LABELS[newThemeIdx] or "ImGui Dark"), "success", 2.5)
            end

            local newRound, roundChanged = ImGui.SliderInt("Rounding", state.uiRounding or 4, 0, 8, "%d px")
            if roundChanged then
                state.uiRounding = newRound
                Theme.applyTheme(state.uiTheme, state.uiRounding)
            end
        end

        ImGui.Spacing(4)
        if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Workspace Panels & Undocking", true) then
            local p = state.panels
            local fp = state.floatingPanels
            p.toolbox = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Toolbox", p.toolbox)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.toolbox = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_tbx", fp.toolbox)

            p.explorer = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Explorer", p.explorer)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.explorer = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_exp", fp.explorer)

            p.properties = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Properties", p.properties)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.properties = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_prop", fp.properties)

            p.timeline = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Timeline", p.timeline)
            ImGui.SameLine(180)
            fp.timeline = ImGui.Checkbox("Float##flt_tl", fp.timeline)

            if ImGui.Button("Dock All & Reset Layout", 200) then
                fp.toolbox = false
                fp.explorer = false
                fp.properties = false
                fp.timeline = false
                layout:reset()
                ImGui.Notify("Layout Restored", "All panels docked to default layout", "info", 2.5)
            end
        end

        ImGui.Spacing(4)
        if ImGui.CollapsingHeader("Freecam & Viewport", true) then
            local cam = state.camera
            cam.speed = ImGui.SliderInt("Cam Speed", cam.speed, 40, 2000, "%d u/s")
            cam.fov = ImGui.SliderInt("Field of View", cam.fov, 45, 110, "%d deg")
            cam.sensitivity = ImGui.SliderFloat("Mouse Sens", cam.sensitivity, 0.04, 0.50, "%.2f")
            local g = state.grid
            g.enabled = ImGui.Checkbox("Show 3D Ground Grid", g.enabled)
            g.showAxes = ImGui.Checkbox("Show 3D Origin Axes", g.showAxes)
            g.showTelemetry = ImGui.Checkbox("Show Telemetry HUD", g.showTelemetry)
            state.debugMode = ImGui.Checkbox("Compact Debug Overlay", state.debugMode)
        end

        ImGui.End()
    end

    local function drawConsoleWindow(ui, state, events)
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_console", "Console & Event Log", state.windows.console, {
            x = 290, y = 576, w = 520, h = 230, minW = 300, minH = 150,
        })
        if not open then
            state.windows.console = false
        end
        if not vis then
            if open then ImGui.End() end
            return
        end

        Windows._consoleFilter = ImGui.InputText("Filter", Windows._consoleFilter, "Filter events...")
        if ImGui.SmallButton("Clear Log") then
            events:clearLogs()
        end
        ImGui.SameLine()
        if ImGui.SmallButton("Emit Test Event") then
            events:log("info", "Manual diagnostic checkpoint logged")
            ImGui.Notify("Console", "Checkpoint added to Event Log", "info", 2.0)
        end

        if ImGui.BeginTabBar("console_tabs") then
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("All") then Windows._consoleTab = "All"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Info") then Windows._consoleTab = "info"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Warn/Err") then Windows._consoleTab = "warn"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            ImGui.EndTabBar()
        end

        if ImGui.BeginChild("console_body", 0, 0, true) then
            local logs = events:getLogs()
            local q = string.lower(Windows._consoleFilter or "")
            local tab = Windows._consoleTab
            for i = #logs, 1, -1 do
                local e = logs[i]
                local matchTab = (tab == "All") or (tab == "info" and e.level == "info") or (tab == "warn" and e.level ~= "info")
                local matchText = (q == "") or (string.find(string.lower(e.text), q, 1, true) ~= nil)
                if matchTab and matchText then
                    local col = Theme.C.textSecondary
                    if e.level == "warning" then col = Theme.C.warning
                    elseif e.level == "error" then col = Theme.C.danger end
                    ImGui.TextColored(col, string.format("[%.1fs] %s", e.time or 0, e.text))
                end
            end
            ImGui.EndChild()
        end

        ImGui.End()
    end

    local _projStatus = ""
    local _projList = nil
    local function refreshProjList()
        _projList = SOLACE.ProjectStorage and SOLACE.ProjectStorage.listProjects() or {}
    end

    local function drawProjectWindow(ui, state, sceneGraph, events, actions)
        if not state.windows or not state.windows.project then return end
        local ps = SOLACE.ProjectStorage
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_project", "Project Manager (sf_filedata/solace/)", true, {
            x = 220, y = 120, w = 460, h = 420, minW = 360, minH = 280,
        })
        if not open then state.windows.project = false end
        if not vis then return end

        local p = state.project
        p.name = ImGui.InputText("Project Name", p.name or "Untitled Project", "Sanitized filename on save")
        p.autosaveEnabled = ImGui.Checkbox("Autosave (_autosave.json)", p.autosaveEnabled)

        if ImGui.Button("Save Project", 110) then
            local ok, res = ps.save(p.name, sceneGraph, state)
            _projStatus = ok and ("Saved: " .. tostring(res)) or ("Save failed: " .. tostring(res))
            refreshProjList()
            if events then events:log(ok and "info" or "error", _projStatus) end
        end
        ImGui.SameLine()
        if ImGui.Button("New Blank", 96) then
            ps.newProject(sceneGraph, state, actions and actions.cmdManager)
            _projStatus = "Started new blank scene."
        end
        ImGui.SameLine()
        if ImGui.Button("Refresh List", 104) then
            refreshProjList()
        end

        if _projStatus ~= "" then
            ImGui.TextColored(Theme.C.accentBright, _projStatus)
        end

        ImGui.Separator()
        ImGui.Text("Saved Projects:")
        if not _projList then refreshProjList() end

        if ImGui.BeginChild("proj_list_child", 0, 0, true) then
            if #_projList == 0 then
                ImGui.TextDisabled("No saved .json projects found in sf_filedata/solace/.")
            else
                for _, fname in ipairs(_projList) do
                    ImGui.Text(fname .. ".json")
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.SmallButton("Load##" .. fname) then
                        local ok, res = ps.load(fname, sceneGraph, state, actions and actions.cmdManager)
                        _projStatus = ok and ("Loaded: " .. fname .. ".json") or ("Load failed: " .. tostring(res))
                        if events then events:log(ok and "info" or "error", _projStatus) end
                    end
                    ImGui.SameLine()
                    if ImGui.SmallButton("Del##" .. fname) then
                        local ok, err = ps.deleteProject(fname)
                        _projStatus = ok and ("Deleted: " .. fname .. ".json") or ("Delete failed: " .. tostring(err))
                        refreshProjList()
                    end
                end
            end
            ImGui.EndChild()
        end
        ImGui.End()
    end

    local _expTab = "lua"
    local _expSelectedOnly = false
    local _expIncludeAnim = true
    local _expPrettyIR = true
    local _expIncludeAI = true
    local _expPreview = ""
    local _expWarnings = {}
    local _expStatus = ""
    local _irImportBuf = ""

    local function drawExporterWindow(ui, state, sceneGraph, events, actions)
        if not state.windows or not state.windows.exporter then return end
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin("win_exporter", "Exporter & AI Scene IR (Phases 13-14)", true, {
            x = 260, y = 110, w = 620, h = 480, minW = 420, minH = 320,
        })
        if not open then state.windows.exporter = false end
        if not vis then return end

        if ImGui.BeginTabBar("exp_tabs") then
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("StarfallEx Lua") then _expTab = "lua"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("AI Scene IR (JSON)") then _expTab = "ir"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            if ImGui.BeginTabItem("Import Scene IR") then _expTab = "import"; ImGui.EndTabItem() end
            ImGui.EndTabBar()
        end

        if _expTab == "lua" then
            _expSelectedOnly = ImGui.Checkbox("Selected Node(s) Only", _expSelectedOnly)
            _expIncludeAnim  = ImGui.Checkbox("Include Keyframe Animations", _expIncludeAnim)
            if ImGui.Button("Generate Lua", 120) then
                local code, warns = SOLACE.LuaExporter.generate(sceneGraph, state, {
                    selectedOnly = _expSelectedOnly,
                    includeAnimation = _expIncludeAnim,
                })
                _expPreview = code or ""
                _expWarnings = warns or {}
                _expStatus = string.format("Generated %d chars (%d warnings)", #_expPreview, #_expWarnings)
            end
            ImGui.SameLine()
            if ImGui.Button("Save to sf_filedata/solace/exports/", 220) then
                if _expPreview == "" then
                    _expPreview, _expWarnings = SOLACE.LuaExporter.generate(sceneGraph, state, {
                        selectedOnly = _expSelectedOnly,
                        includeAnimation = _expIncludeAnim,
                    })
                end
                local ok, pathOrErr = SOLACE.LuaExporter.saveToFile((state.project and state.project.name) or "export", _expPreview)
                _expStatus = ok and ("Exported: " .. tostring(pathOrErr)) or ("Save failed: " .. tostring(pathOrErr))
                if events then events:log(ok and "info" or "error", _expStatus) end
            end
        elseif _expTab == "ir" then
            _expPrettyIR = ImGui.Checkbox("Pretty Print JSON", _expPrettyIR)
            _expIncludeAI = ImGui.Checkbox("Include AI Summary Metadata", _expIncludeAI)
            if ImGui.Button("Generate Scene IR JSON", 170) then
                local str, err = SOLACE.IRInterchange.exportJSON(sceneGraph, state, {
                    pretty = _expPrettyIR,
                    includeDescriptions = _expIncludeAI,
                })
                _expPreview = str or ""
                _expWarnings = err and { err } or {}
                _expStatus = err and ("Error: " .. err) or string.format("Generated IR JSON (%d bytes)", #_expPreview)
            end
            ImGui.SameLine()
            if ImGui.Button("Save IR JSON", 110) then
                local str, err = SOLACE.IRInterchange.exportJSON(sceneGraph, state, {
                    pretty = _expPrettyIR,
                    includeDescriptions = _expIncludeAI,
                })
                if str then
                    local name = ((state.project and state.project.name) or "scene") .. "_ir"
                    local ok, res = SOLACE.ProjectStorage.save(name, sceneGraph, state)
                    _expPreview = str
                    _expStatus = ok and ("Saved IR: " .. tostring(res)) or ("Save error: " .. tostring(res))
                else
                    _expStatus = "Encode failed: " .. tostring(err)
                end
            end
        else
            ImGui.Text("Paste SOLACE Scene IR JSON below to validate and import:")
            _irImportBuf = ImGui.InputText("IR JSON", _irImportBuf, "Paste JSON document here")
            if ImGui.Button("Validate & Import IR", 160) then
                local ok, res = SOLACE.IRInterchange.importJSON(_irImportBuf, sceneGraph, state, actions and actions.cmdManager)
                _expStatus = ok and "Scene IR imported successfully!" or ("Import error: " .. tostring(res))
                _expWarnings = (ok and type(res) == "table") and res or {}
                if events then events:log(ok and "info" or "error", _expStatus) end
            end
        end

        if _expStatus ~= "" then
            ImGui.TextColored(Theme.C.accentBright, _expStatus)
        end
        for _, w in ipairs(_expWarnings) do
            ImGui.TextColored(Theme.C.warning, "Warn: " .. tostring(w))
        end

        if ImGui.BeginChild("exp_preview_child", 0, 0, true) then
            if _expPreview == "" then
                ImGui.TextDisabled("Click Generate to preview StarfallEx Lua or AI-Friendly Scene IR JSON.")
            else
                local shown = 0
                for line in string.gmatch(_expPreview .. "\n", "([^\n]*)\n") do
                    shown = shown + 1
                    if shown > 120 then
                        ImGui.TextDisabled("... preview truncated (save file to inspect full output) ...")
                        break
                    end
                    ImGui.Text(line)
                end
            end
            ImGui.EndChild()
        end
        ImGui.End()
    end

    local function drawFloatingPanel(id, title, key, defaultRect, state, drawInner)
        if not state.panels[key] or not state.floatingPanels[key] then return end
        local vis, open = ImGui.Begin(id, title, true, {
            x = defaultRect.x,
            y = defaultRect.y,
            w = defaultRect.w,
            h = defaultRect.h,
            minW = defaultRect.minW or 200,
            minH = defaultRect.minH or 140,
            onDock = function()
                state.floatingPanels[key] = false
                ImGui.Notify("Panel Docked", title .. " docked back into workspace", "info", 2.0)
            end,
        })
        if not open then
            state.panels[key] = false
        end
        if vis then
            local win = ImGui.getWindow(id)
            local titleH = Theme.Style.titleBarH or 24
            local innerRect = {
                x = win.x + 1,
                y = win.y + titleH + 1,
                w = math.max(0, win.w - 2),
                h = math.max(0, win.h - titleH - 2),
            }
            drawInner(innerRect)
            ImGui.End()
        end
    end

    local WINDOW_SPECS = {
        { id = "win_panel_toolbox", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.toolbox and s.floatingPanels.toolbox end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, _, _, _, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_toolbox", "Toolbox", "toolbox", { x = 24, y = 68, w = 250, h = 480 }, s, function(r)
                  Toolbox.draw(ui, s, r, actions, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_panel_explorer", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.explorer and s.floatingPanels.explorer end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, _, _, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_explorer", "Explorer", "explorer", { x = 1040, y = 68, w = 280, h = 320 }, s, function(r)
                  Explorer.draw(ui, s, sg, r, actions, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_panel_properties", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.properties and s.floatingPanels.properties end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, rt, cam, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_properties", "Properties", "properties", { x = 1040, y = 400, w = 290, h = 380 }, s, function(r)
                  Properties.draw(ui, s, sg, rt, cam, r, actions, { hideFrame = true })
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_panel_timeline", isOpen = function(s) return s.panels.timeline and s.floatingPanels.timeline end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, _, _, _, actions)
              drawFloatingPanel("win_panel_timeline", "Timeline", "timeline", { x = 120, y = 640, w = 860, h = 200, minW = 360 }, s, function(r)
                  Timeline.draw(ui, s, sg, r, { hideFrame = true }, actions)
              end)
          end },
        { id = "win_project", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.project end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, _, _, events, actions) drawProjectWindow(ui, s, sg, events, actions) end },
        { id = "win_exporter", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.exporter end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, sg, _, _, events, actions) drawExporterWindow(ui, s, sg, events, actions) end },
        { id = "win_demo", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.demo end,
          draw = function(ui, s) drawDemoWindow(ui, s) end },
        { id = "win_settings", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.settings end,
          draw = function(ui, s, layout) drawSettingsWindow(ui, s, layout) end },
        { id = "win_console", isOpen = function(s) return s.windows and s.windows.console end,
          draw = function(ui, s, _, _, _, _, events) drawConsoleWindow(ui, s, events) end },
    }

    local SPEC_BY_ID = {}
    for _, sp in ipairs(WINDOW_SPECS) do
        SPEC_BY_ID[sp.id] = sp
    end

    function Windows.drawAll(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)
        local drawn = {}
        local z = ImGui._zOrder
        -- Snapshot Z-order so click-to-front mid-frame doesn't skip or double-draw a window
        local orderCopy = {}
        for i = 1, #z do
            orderCopy[i] = z[i]
        end

        for i = 1, #orderCopy do
            local id = orderCopy[i]
            local sp = SPEC_BY_ID[id]
            if sp and sp.isOpen(state) then
                sp.draw(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)
                drawn[id] = true
            end
        end

        -- Draw any newly opened windows not yet in _zOrder
        for _, sp in ipairs(WINDOW_SPECS) do
            if not drawn[sp.id] and sp.isOpen(state) then
                sp.draw(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)
            end
        end
    end

    SOLACE.Windows = Windows
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 18: Project serialization (Phase 12)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants = SOLACE.Constants
    local IR        = SOLACE.IR

    local ProjectStorage = {
        ready = true,
        phase = 12,
        ROOT_DIR = "solace",
        EXPORT_DIR = "solace/exports",
        MAX_BYTES = 512 * 1024,
        MAX_NODES = 256,
        _lastStatus = nil,
    }

    function ProjectStorage.sanitizeName(raw)
        local s = string.trim(tostring(raw or "untitled"))
        s = string.gsub(s, "[^%w_%- ]", "_")
        s = string.gsub(s, "%s+", "_")
        s = string.gsub(s, "_+", "_")
        if s == "" then s = "untitled" end
        return string.sub(s, 1, 48)
    end

    local function ensureDir(dir)
        if not file or not file.createDir then return false, "file API unavailable" end
        if not file.exists(dir) then
            local ok, err = pcall(file.createDir, dir)
            if not ok then return false, tostring(err) end
        end
        return true
    end

    local function cloneTrackList(tracks)
        local out = {}
        for k, trk in pairs(tracks or {}) do
            local kfs = {}
            for i = 1, #(trk.keyframes or {}) do
                local kf = trk.keyframes[i]
                local val = kf.value
                if type(val) == "table" then
                    local c = {}
                    for j = 1, #val do c[j] = val[j] end
                    val = c
                end
                kfs[i] = { time = kf.time, value = val, easing = kf.easing or "linear" }
            end
            out[tostring(k)] = {
                property = trk.property or tostring(k),
                enabled = trk.enabled ~= false,
                keyframes = kfs,
            }
        end
        return out
    end

    local function serializeNode(node)
        local subs = {}
        for k, v in pairs(node.appearance.submaterials or {}) do
            subs[tostring(k)] = v
        end
        local bones = {}
        for k, b in pairs(node.bones or {}) do
            bones[tostring(k)] = {
                pos = IR.cloneVec3(b.pos),
                ang = IR.cloneVec3(b.ang),
                scale = IR.cloneVec3(b.scale),
            }
        end
        local out = {
            id = node.id,
            name = node.name,
            kind = node.kind,
            parentId = node.parentId,
            visible = node.visible ~= false,
            locked = node.locked == true,
            expanded = node.expanded ~= false,
            children = {},
            transform = {
                pos = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pos),
                ang = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.ang),
                scale = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.scale),
                pivot = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pivot),
                pivotAng = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pivotAng),
            },
            appearance = {
                model = node.appearance.model or "",
                material = node.appearance.material or "",
                color = IR.cloneColor(node.appearance.color),
                alpha = node.appearance.alpha or 255,
                renderMode = node.appearance.renderMode or 0,
                renderFX = node.appearance.renderFX or 0,
                cullMode = node.appearance.cullMode or 0,
                suppressEngineLighting = node.appearance.suppressEngineLighting == true,
                holoAnim = node.appearance.holoAnim or 0,
                holoAnimRate = node.appearance.holoAnimRate or 1,
                submaterials = subs,
            },
            sprite = node.sprite and {
                width = node.sprite.width or 24,
                height = node.sprite.height or 24,
                roll = node.sprite.roll or 0,
                rollSpeed = node.sprite.rollSpeed or 0,
