                fadeIn = node.sprite.fadeIn or 0,
                fadeOut = node.sprite.fadeOut or 0,
                lifetime = node.sprite.lifetime or 0,
            } or nil,
            camera = node.camera and {
                fov = node.camera.fov or 80,
                lookAtNodeId = node.camera.lookAtNodeId,
                focusDist = node.camera.focusDist or 180,
                shakeAmplitude = node.camera.shakeAmplitude or 0,
                shakeFrequency = node.camera.shakeFrequency or 8,
                shakeDuration = node.camera.shakeDuration or 0,
            } or nil,
            effect = node.effect and {
                preset = node.effect.preset or "spark",
                mode = node.effect.mode or "particles",
                material = node.effect.material or "sprites/light_glow02_add",
                rate = node.effect.rate or 12,
                speed = node.effect.speed or 60,
                spread = node.effect.spread or 25,
                gravity = type(node.effect.gravity) == "table" and (node.effect.gravity[3] or -120) or (tonumber(node.effect.gravity) or -120),
                startSize = node.effect.startSize or 6,
                endSize = node.effect.endSize or 0,
                startAlpha = node.effect.startAlpha or 220,
                endAlpha = node.effect.endAlpha or 0,
                lifetime = node.effect.lifetime or 1.0,
                delay = node.effect.delay or 0,
                duration = node.effect.duration or 0,
                beamLen = node.effect.beamLen or node.effect.beamLength or 64,
                lightSize = node.effect.lightSize or 180,
                lightBrightness = node.effect.lightBrightness or 2,
            } or nil,
            bones = next(bones) ~= nil and bones or nil,
            tracks = cloneTrackList(node.tracks),
            meta = node.meta,
        }
        for i = 1, #(node.children or {}) do
            out.children[i] = node.children[i]
        end
        return out
    end

    function ProjectStorage.buildDocument(sceneGraph, state, opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        local nodes = {}
        local orderedIds = {}
        local rawNodes = sceneGraph:getAllNodes()
        for id in pairs(rawNodes) do
            orderedIds[#orderedIds + 1] = id
        end
        table.sort(orderedIds)

        local nodeList = {}
        for _, id in ipairs(orderedIds) do
            local sNode = serializeNode(rawNodes[id])
            nodes[id] = sNode
            nodeList[#nodeList + 1] = sNode
        end

        local doc = {
            schema = "solace.scene.ir",
            schemaVersion = Constants.IR_SCHEMA_VERSION,
            appVersion = Constants.VERSION,
            project = {
                name = (state and state.project and state.project.name) or "Untitled Project",
                author = (state and state.project and state.project.author) or "",
                fps = (state and state.timeline and state.timeline.fps) or 30,
                duration = (state and state.timeline and state.timeline.duration) or 10,
                loop = (state and state.timeline and state.timeline.loop) ~= false,
            },
            scene = {
                rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId(),
                nextNodeIndex = (sceneGraph._project and sceneGraph._project.nextNodeIndex) or 1,
                nodes = nodes,
            },
            camera = (state and state.camera) and (function()
                local camObj = SOLACE._ctx and SOLACE._ctx.camera
                local cp = camObj and camObj:getPos() or Vector(0, 0, 64)
                local ca = camObj and camObj:getAng() or Angle(0, 0, 0)
                return {
                    pos = { cp[1], cp[2], cp[3] },
                    ang = { ca[1], ca[2], ca[3] },
                    fov = state.camera.fov,
                }
            end)() or nil,
            grid = state and state.grid and {
                enabled = state.grid.enabled,
                spacing = state.grid.spacing,
                halfExtents = state.grid.halfExtents,
            } or nil,
        }

        if opts.includeDescriptions then
            local byKind = {}
            local kfTotal = 0
            for _, nd in ipairs(nodeList) do
                byKind[nd.kind] = (byKind[nd.kind] or 0) + 1
                for _, trk in pairs(nd.tracks or {}) do
                    kfTotal = kfTotal + #(trk.keyframes or {})
                end
            end
            doc.aiSummary = {
                format = "SOLACE AI-Friendly Scene IR v" .. tostring(Constants.IR_SCHEMA_VERSION),
                description = "Deterministic hierarchical 3D scene graph with local transforms, pivots, StarfallEx appearance, bone offsets, VFX layers, cinematic cameras, and eased keyframe tracks.",
                coordinateSystem = "Source Engine Z-up right-handed units (Pitch, Yaw, Roll degrees)",
                nodeCount = #nodeList,
                countsByKind = byKind,
                totalKeyframes = kfTotal,
                timelineDurationSec = doc.project.duration,
                timelineFps = doc.project.fps,
            }
        end

        return doc
    end

    local function isFiniteNum(v)
        return type(v) == "number" and v == v and math.abs(v) < 1e9
    end

    local function isVec3(t)
        return type(t) == "table" and isFiniteNum(t[1]) and isFiniteNum(t[2]) and isFiniteNum(t[3])
    end

    function ProjectStorage.validateDocument(doc)
        local warnings = {}
        if type(doc) ~= "table" then return false, "Root JSON must be an object", warnings end
        if doc.schema ~= "solace.scene.ir" then
            return false, "Invalid schema (expected 'solace.scene.ir')", warnings
        end
        local sv = tonumber(doc.schemaVersion) or 0
        if sv < 1 then
            return false, "Missing or invalid schemaVersion", warnings
        elseif sv > Constants.IR_SCHEMA_VERSION then
            warnings[#warnings + 1] = string.format("Document schema v%d is newer than v%d; unknown fields ignored", sv, Constants.IR_SCHEMA_VERSION)
        end
        local scene = doc.scene
        if type(scene) ~= "table" or type(scene.nodes) ~= "table" then
            return false, "Missing scene.nodes table", warnings
        end
        local rootId = scene.rootId or "root"
        if type(scene.nodes[rootId]) ~= "table" then
            return false, "Missing root node '" .. tostring(rootId) .. "'", warnings
        end
        local count = 0
        for id, nd in pairs(scene.nodes) do
            count = count + 1
            if count > ProjectStorage.MAX_NODES then
                return false, "Node count exceeds safe limit (" .. ProjectStorage.MAX_NODES .. ")", warnings
            end
            if type(nd) ~= "table" or type(nd.kind) ~= "string" then
                return false, "Invalid node entry: " .. tostring(id), warnings
            end
            if nd.transform then
                if nd.transform.pos and not isVec3(nd.transform.pos) then return false, "Invalid pos on " .. tostring(id), warnings end
                if nd.transform.ang and not isVec3(nd.transform.ang) then return false, "Invalid ang on " .. tostring(id), warnings end
                if nd.transform.scale and not isVec3(nd.transform.scale) then return false, "Invalid scale on " .. tostring(id), warnings end
            end
        end
        return true, nil, warnings
    end

    local function deserializeNode(raw)
        local node = IR.createNode({
            id = raw.id,
            name = raw.name or "Node",
            kind = raw.kind or Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM,
            parentId = raw.parentId,
            visible = raw.visible ~= false,
            locked = raw.locked == true,
            expanded = raw.expanded ~= false,
            pos = raw.transform and raw.transform.pos,
            ang = raw.transform and raw.transform.ang,
            scale = raw.transform and raw.transform.scale,
            pivot = raw.transform and raw.transform.pivot,
            pivotAng = raw.transform and raw.transform.pivotAng,
            model = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.model or "",
            material = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.material or "",
            color = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.color,
            renderMode = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.renderMode or 0,
            renderFX = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.renderFX or 0,
            cullMode = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.cullMode or 0,
            suppressEngineLighting = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.suppressEngineLighting == true,
            holoAnim = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.holoAnim or 0,
            holoAnimRate = raw.appearance and raw.appearance.holoAnimRate or 1,
            fov = raw.camera and raw.camera.fov,
            lookAtNodeId = raw.camera and raw.camera.lookAtNodeId,
            focusDist = raw.camera and raw.camera.focusDist,
        })
        if raw.appearance and type(raw.appearance.submaterials) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(raw.appearance.submaterials) do
                local nk = tonumber(k)
                node.appearance.submaterials[nk ~= nil and nk or k] = v
            end
        end
        if raw.sprite and node.sprite then
            node.sprite.width = raw.sprite.width or 24
            node.sprite.height = raw.sprite.height or 24
            node.sprite.roll = raw.sprite.roll or 0
            node.sprite.rollSpeed = raw.sprite.rollSpeed or 0
            node.sprite.fadeIn = raw.sprite.fadeIn or 0
            node.sprite.fadeOut = raw.sprite.fadeOut or 0
            node.sprite.lifetime = raw.sprite.lifetime or 0
        end
        if raw.camera and node.camera then
            node.camera.shakeAmplitude = raw.camera.shakeAmplitude or 0
            node.camera.shakeFrequency = raw.camera.shakeFrequency or 8
            node.camera.shakeDuration = raw.camera.shakeDuration or 0
        end
        if raw.effect then
            node.effect = IR.createEffectPayload(raw.effect)
        end
        if type(raw.bones) == "table" then
            node.bones = {}
            for k, b in pairs(raw.bones) do
                local bi = tonumber(k) or k
                node.bones[bi] = {
                    pos = IR.cloneVec3(b.pos),
                    ang = IR.cloneVec3(b.ang),
                    scale = IR.cloneVec3(b.scale or { 1, 1, 1 }),
                }
            end
        end
        if type(raw.tracks) == "table" then
            node.tracks = cloneTrackList(raw.tracks)
        end
        if type(raw.children) == "table" then
            for i = 1, #raw.children do
                node.children[i] = raw.children[i]
            end
        end
        node.meta = raw.meta
        return node
    end

    function ProjectStorage.applyDocument(doc, sceneGraph, state, cmdManager)
        local ok, err, warnings = ProjectStorage.validateDocument(doc)
        if not ok then return false, err, warnings end

        local nodes = {}
        local count = 0
        for id, raw in pairs(doc.scene.nodes) do
            nodes[id] = deserializeNode(raw)
            count = count + 1
        end
        local proj = IR.createProject({ name = (doc.project and doc.project.name) or "Untitled Project" })
        proj.rootId = doc.scene.rootId or "root"
        proj.nextNodeIndex = tonumber(doc.scene.nextNodeIndex or doc.scene.nextId) or (count + 1)
        proj.nodes = nodes
        sceneGraph:replaceProject(proj)

        if state then
            if doc.project then
                state.project.name = doc.project.name or state.project.name
                state.project.author = doc.project.author or ""
                state.project.dirty = false
                if state.timeline then
                    state.timeline.fps = doc.project.fps or 30
                    state.timeline.duration = doc.project.duration or 10
                    state.timeline.loop = doc.project.loop ~= false
                    state.timeline.playing = false
                    state.timeline.currentTime = 0
                end
            end
            if doc.camera and state.camera and SOLACE._ctx and SOLACE._ctx.camera then
                local cam = SOLACE._ctx.camera
                if isVec3(doc.camera.pos) then
                    cam:setPose(Vector(doc.camera.pos[1], doc.camera.pos[2], doc.camera.pos[3]),
                        Angle(doc.camera.ang[1], doc.camera.ang[2], doc.camera.ang[3]), true)
                end
                if doc.camera.fov then state.camera.fov = doc.camera.fov end
            end
            if doc.grid and state.grid then
                if doc.grid.enabled ~= nil then state.grid.enabled = doc.grid.enabled end
                if doc.grid.spacing then state.grid.spacing = doc.grid.spacing end
                if doc.grid.halfExtents then state.grid.halfExtents = doc.grid.halfExtents end
            end
            state.activeCameraId = nil
        end
        if cmdManager and cmdManager.clear then cmdManager:clear() end
        if SOLACE.Animation and state then
            SOLACE.Animation.evaluateScene(sceneGraph, 0, state)
        end
        return true, nil, warnings
    end

    function ProjectStorage.save(name, sceneGraph, state)
        if not file or not file.write or not json or not json.encode then
            return false, "file/json API unavailable"
        end
        local okDir, dErr = ensureDir(ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR)
        if not okDir then return false, dErr end

        local safe = ProjectStorage.sanitizeName(name)
        local path = ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR .. "/" .. safe .. ".json"
        if state and state.project then
            state.project.name = name
        end
        local doc = ProjectStorage.buildDocument(sceneGraph, state, { includeDescriptions = false })
        local okEnc, encoded = pcall(json.encode, doc, true)
        if not okEnc or type(encoded) ~= "string" then
            return false, "JSON encode failed: " .. tostring(encoded)
        end
        if #encoded > ProjectStorage.MAX_BYTES then
            return false, string.format("Project size (%d B) exceeds %d B cap", #encoded, ProjectStorage.MAX_BYTES)
        end
        local okWr, wErr = pcall(file.write, path, encoded)
        if not okWr then return false, "Write failed: " .. tostring(wErr) end
        if state and state.project then
            state.project.dirty = false
            state.project.lastSaveName = safe
            state.project.lastSaveTime = timer.curtime()
        end
        ProjectStorage._lastStatus = "Saved " .. path .. " (" .. #encoded .. " B)"
        return true, path
    end

    function ProjectStorage.load(name, sceneGraph, state, cmdManager)
        if not file or not file.read or not json or not json.decode then
            return false, "file/json API unavailable"
        end
        local safe = ProjectStorage.sanitizeName(name)
        local path = ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR .. "/" .. safe .. ".json"
        if not file.exists(path) then
            return false, "File not found: " .. path
        end
        local raw = file.read(path)
        if not raw or raw == "" then
            return false, "Empty or unreadable file: " .. path
        end
        local okDec, doc = pcall(json.decode, raw)
        if not okDec then
            return false, "JSON parse error: " .. tostring(doc)
        end
        local okApp, err, warnings = ProjectStorage.applyDocument(doc, sceneGraph, state, cmdManager)
        if not okApp then return false, err end
        if state and state.project then
            state.project.lastSaveName = safe
        end
        ProjectStorage._lastStatus = "Loaded " .. path
        return true, warnings
    end

    function ProjectStorage.listProjects()
        if not file or not file.find then return {} end
        local ok, files = pcall(file.find, ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR .. "/*.json", "datedesc")
        if not ok or type(files) ~= "table" then return {} end
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, #files do
            out[#out + 1] = string.stripExtension(files[i])
        end
        return out
    end

    function ProjectStorage.deleteProject(name)
        if not file or not file.delete then return false, "file.delete unavailable" end
        local safe = ProjectStorage.sanitizeName(name)
        local path = ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR .. "/" .. safe .. ".json"
        if not file.exists(path) then return false, "Not found" end
        local ok, err = pcall(file.delete, path)
        if not ok then return false, tostring(err) end
        return true
    end

    function ProjectStorage.newProject(sceneGraph, state, cmdManager)
        local proj = IR.createProject({ name = "Untitled Project" })
        sceneGraph:replaceProject(proj)
        if state then
            state.project.name = "Untitled Project"
            state.project.dirty = false
            state.activeCameraId = nil
            if state.timeline then
                state.timeline.playing = false
                state.timeline.currentTime = 0
            end
        end
        if cmdManager and cmdManager.clear then cmdManager:clear() end
        ProjectStorage._lastStatus = "Started blank project"
        return true
    end

    function ProjectStorage.stepAutosave(dt, sceneGraph, state)
        local p = state and state.project
        if not p or not p.autosaveEnabled or not p.dirty then return end
        p._autosaveAccum = (p._autosaveAccum or 0) + dt
        if p._autosaveAccum >= (p.autosaveInterval or 90) then
            p._autosaveAccum = 0
            ProjectStorage.save("_autosave", sceneGraph, state)
        end
    end

    SOLACE.ProjectStorage = ProjectStorage
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 19: StarfallEx Lua exporter & AI Scene IR Interchange (Phases 13 & 14)
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants      = SOLACE.Constants
    local Math3D         = SOLACE.Math3D
    local ProjectStorage = SOLACE.ProjectStorage

    local LuaExporter = {
        ready = true,
        phase = 13,
    }

    local LUA_KEYWORDS = {
        ["and"] = true, ["break"] = true, ["do"] = true, ["else"] = true, ["elseif"] = true,
        ["end"] = true, ["false"] = true, ["for"] = true, ["function"] = true, ["if"] = true,
        ["in"] = true, ["local"] = true, ["nil"] = true, ["not"] = true, ["or"] = true,
        ["repeat"] = true, ["return"] = true, ["then"] = true, ["true"] = true,
        ["until"] = true, ["while"] = true,
    }

    function LuaExporter.sanitizeIdent(raw, fallback)
        local s = string.gsub(tostring(raw or ""), "[^%w_]", "_")
        if s == "" or string.match(s, "^%d") then
            s = (fallback or "n_") .. s
        end
        if LUA_KEYWORDS[s] then s = s .. "_node" end
        return s
    end

    local function collectExportNodes(sceneGraph, selectedOnly)
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local out = {}
        local seen = {}

        local function visit(id)
            if seen[id] then return end
            local nd = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if not nd then return end
            if id ~= rootId then
                seen[id] = true
                out[#out + 1] = nd
            end
            for _, cid in ipairs(nd.children or {}) do
                visit(cid)
            end
        end

        if selectedOnly then
            local selIds = sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
            for _, sid in ipairs(selIds) do
                visit(sid)
            end
        else
            visit(rootId)
        end
        return out
    end

    function LuaExporter.generate(sceneGraph, state, opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        local nodes = collectExportNodes(sceneGraph, opts.selectedOnly == true)
        local warnings = {}
        local includeAnim = (opts.includeAnimation ~= false)
        local includeCams = (opts.includeCameras ~= false)
        local includeVFX  = (opts.includeVFX ~= false)

        if #nodes == 0 then
            warnings[#warnings + 1] = "No exportable nodes in current selection/scene."
        end

        local needHolo, needRenderProp, needParticle, needLight, needCam = false, false, false, false, false
        local holoCount = 0
        for _, nd in ipairs(nodes) do
            if nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL then
                needHolo = true
                holoCount = holoCount + 1
                if nd.meta and nd.meta.worldEnt then
                    warnings[#warnings + 1] = "Node '" .. nd.name .. "' links a world ragdoll; exported as client hologram."
                end
                if nd.bones and next(nd.bones) ~= nil then
                    needRenderProp = true
                end
            elseif nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA and includeCams then
                needCam = true
            elseif (nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT) and includeVFX then
                if nd.effect and nd.effect.mode == "light" then needLight = true
                elseif nd.effect and nd.effect.mode == "particles" then needParticle = true end
            end
        end
        if holoCount > 128 then
            warnings[#warnings + 1] = string.format("Hologram count (%d) may exceed server sf_holograms_maxquota.", holoCount)
        end

        local lines = {}
        local function add(s) lines[#lines + 1] = s end

        local projName = (state and state.project and state.project.name) or "SOLACE Export"
        add("--@name " .. string.gsub(projName, "[\r\n]", " "))
        add("--@author SOLACE Studio v" .. Constants.VERSION)
        add("--@client")
        add("")
        add("local perms = {")
        if needHolo then add('    "hologram.create",') end
        if needRenderProp then add('    "entities.setRenderProperty",') end
        if needParticle then add('    "particle.create",') end
        if needLight then add('    "light.create",') end
        if needCam then add('    "render.renderView",') end
        add("}")
        add("if #perms > 0 then")
        add('    setupPermissionRequest(perms, "SOLACE exported scene runtime", true)')
        add("end")
        add("")
        add("local ORIGIN = chip():getPos()")
        add("local ORIGIN_ANG = chip():getAngles()")
        add("local nodes = {}")
        add("local sprites = {}")
        add("local emitters = {}")
        add("local cameras = {}")
        add("")
        add("local function toWorld(lPos, lAng)")
        add("    return localToWorld(lPos, lAng, ORIGIN, ORIGIN_ANG)")
        add("end")
        add("")

        if includeAnim then
            add("local function easeVal(u, e)")
            add('    if e == "step" then return u >= 1 and 1 or 0')
            add('    elseif e == "smooth" then return u * u * (3 - 2 * u)')
            add('    elseif e == "easeIn" or e == "inQuad" then return u * u')
            add('    elseif e == "easeOut" or e == "outQuad" then return 1 - (1 - u) ^ 2')
            add('    elseif e == "easeInOut" or e == "inOutQuad" then return u < 0.5 and 2*u*u or 1 - ((-2*u+2)^2)*0.5')
            add("    end")
            add("    return u")
            add("end")
            add("")
            add("local function evalTrack(kfs, t, isAng)")
            add("    if #kfs == 0 then return nil end")
            add("    if #kfs == 1 or t <= kfs[1][1] then return kfs[1][2] end")
            add("    if t >= kfs[#kfs][1] then return kfs[#kfs][2] end")
            add("    for i = 1, #kfs - 1 do")
            add("        local a, b = kfs[i], kfs[i + 1]")
            add("        if t >= a[1] and t <= b[1] then")
            add("            local u = easeVal((t - a[1]) / math.max(1e-5, b[1] - a[1]), a[3])")
            add("            if type(a[2]) == 'number' then return a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * u end")
            add("            local out = {}")
            add("            for k = 1, #a[2] do")
            add("                if isAng then out[k] = math.normalizeAngle(a[2][k] + math.normalizeAngle(b[2][k] - a[2][k]) * u)")
            add("                else out[k] = a[2][k] + (b[2][k] - a[2][k]) * u end")
            add("            end")
            add("            return out")
            add("        end")
            add("    end")
            add("end")
            add("")
        end

        add("local function buildScene()")
        add("    if #perms > 0 and not hasPermission(perms[1]) then return end")
        for idx, nd in ipairs(nodes) do
            local var = LuaExporter.sanitizeIdent(nd.id .. "_" .. nd.name, "node_" .. idx)
            local wPos, wAng = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, nd.id)
            local col = nd.appearance.color or { 255, 255, 255, 255 }
            local pStr = string.format("Vector(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", wPos[1], wPos[2], wPos[3])
            local aStr = string.format("Angle(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)", wAng[1], wAng[2], wAng[3])
            local cStr = string.format("Color(%d, %d, %d, %d)", col[1] or 255, col[2] or 255, col[3] or 255, col[4] or 255)

            if nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.HOLOGRAM or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL then
                local mdl = nd.appearance.model ~= "" and nd.appearance.model or "models/hunter/blocks/cube1x1x1.mdl"
                local sc = nd.transform.scale or { 1, 1, 1 }
                add(string.format("    do -- %s", nd.name))
                add(string.format("        local wp, wa = toWorld(%s, %s)", pStr, aStr))
                add(string.format('        local h = hologram.create(wp, wa, %q, Vector(%.3f, %.3f, %.3f))', mdl, sc[1], sc[2], sc[3]))
                add("        if h then")
                add("            h:setColor(" .. cStr .. ")")
                if nd.appearance.material and nd.appearance.material ~= "" then
                    add(string.format("            h:setMaterial(%q)", nd.appearance.material))
                end
                if (nd.appearance.renderMode or 0) ~= 0 then
                    add("            h:setRenderMode(" .. tostring(nd.appearance.renderMode) .. ")")
                end
                if (nd.appearance.renderFX or 0) ~= 0 then
                    add("            h:setRenderFX(" .. tostring(nd.appearance.renderFX) .. ")")
                end
                if nd.bones then
                    for bi, bd in pairs(nd.bones) do
                        local ba = bd.ang or { 0, 0, 0 }
                        add(string.format("            pcall(function() h:manipulateBoneAngles(%d, Angle(%.2f, %.2f, %.2f)) end)",
                            tonumber(bi) or 0, ba[1], ba[2], ba[3]))
                    end
                end
                add(string.format("            nodes[%q] = { holo = h, basePos = %s, baseAng = %s }", nd.id, pStr, aStr))
                add("        end")
                add("    end")
            elseif nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE then
                local spr = nd.sprite or {}
                local mat = nd.appearance.material ~= "" and nd.appearance.material or "sprites/glow04_noz"
                add(string.format("    table.insert(sprites, { pos = %s, w = %.1f, h = %.1f, mat = Material(%q), col = %s })",
                    pStr, spr.width or 24, spr.height or 24, mat, cStr))
            elseif nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA and includeCams then
                local fov = (nd.camera and nd.camera.fov) or 80
                add(string.format("    table.insert(cameras, { id = %q, pos = %s, ang = %s, fov = %.1f })",
                    nd.id, pStr, aStr, fov))
            elseif (nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.PARTICLE or nd.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT) and includeVFX then
                local fx = nd.effect or {}
                if fx.mode == "light" then
                    add(string.format("    if light and light.create then local wp = toWorld(%s, Angle()); local l = light.create(wp, %.0f, %.2f, %s); if l then l:draw() end end",
                        pStr, fx.lightSize or 256, fx.lightBrightness or 2, cStr))
                else
                    add(string.format("    if particle and particle.create then local wp = toWorld(%s, Angle()); table.insert(emitters, { em = particle.create(wp, false), pos = %s, rate = %.1f, mat = %q, sz = %.1f, life = %.2f, col = %s }) end",
                        pStr, pStr, fx.rate or 12, fx.material or "effects/spark", fx.startSize or 8, fx.lifetime or 1.2, cStr))
                end
            end
            local _ = var
        end
        add("end")
        add("")
        add('hook.add("permissionrequest", "solace_export_perm", buildScene)')
        add("buildScene()")
        add("")
        add("if #sprites > 0 then")
        add('    hook.add("postdrawtranslucentrenderables", "solace_export_sprites", function()')
        add("        for _, s in ipairs(sprites) do")
        add("            local wp = toWorld(s.pos, Angle())")
        add("            render.setMaterial(s.mat); render.setColor(s.col)")
        add("            render.draw3DSprite(wp, s.w, s.h)")
        add("        end")
        add("    end)")
        add("end")

        if includeAnim then
            local hasTracks = false
            for _, nd in ipairs(nodes) do
                if nd.tracks and next(nd.tracks) ~= nil then hasTracks = true break end
            end
            if hasTracks then
                local dur = (state and state.timeline and state.timeline.duration) or 10
                add("")
                add("local TRACKS = {")
                for _, nd in ipairs(nodes) do
                    if nd.tracks and next(nd.tracks) ~= nil then
                        add(string.format("    [%q] = {", nd.id))
                        for tKey, trk in pairs(nd.tracks) do
                            if trk.enabled ~= false and trk.keyframes and #trk.keyframes > 0 then
                                local kfParts = {}
                                for _, kf in ipairs(trk.keyframes) do
                                    local vStr
                                    if type(kf.value) == "table" then
                                        vStr = "{" .. table.concat(kf.value, ",") .. "}"
                                    else
                                        vStr = tostring(kf.value)
                                    end
                                    kfParts[#kfParts + 1] = string.format("{%.3f,%s,%q}", kf.time or 0, vStr, kf.easing or "linear")
                                end
                                add(string.format("        [%q] = { %s },", tKey, table.concat(kfParts, ", ")))
                            end
                        end
                        add("    },")
                    end
                end
                add("}")
                add(string.format("local DURATION = %.2f", dur))
                add('hook.add("think", "solace_export_anim", function()')
                add("    local t = timer.curtime() % DURATION")
                add("    for id, nd in pairs(nodes) do")
                add("        local tr = TRACKS[id]")
                add("        if tr and IsValid(nd.holo) then")
                add('            local p = tr["transform.pos"] and evalTrack(tr["transform.pos"], t, false)')
                add('            local a = tr["transform.ang"] and evalTrack(tr["transform.ang"], t, true)')
                add("            local lp = p and Vector(p[1], p[2], p[3]) or nd.basePos")
                add("            local la = a and Angle(a[1], a[2], a[3]) or nd.baseAng")
                add("            local wp, wa = toWorld(lp, la)")
                add("            nd.holo:setPos(wp); nd.holo:setAngles(wa)")
                add('            local s = tr["transform.scale"] and evalTrack(tr["transform.scale"], t, false)')
                add("            if s then nd.holo:setScale(Vector(s[1], s[2], s[3])) end")
                add('            local c = tr["appearance.color"] and evalTrack(tr["appearance.color"], t, false)')
                add("            if c then nd.holo:setColor(Color(c[1], c[2], c[3], c[4] or 255)) end")
                add("        end")
                add("    end")
                add("end)")
            end
        end

        if needCam then
            add("")
            add('hook.add("CalcView", "solace_export_cam", function()')
            add("    if cameras[1] then")
            add("        local wp, wa = toWorld(cameras[1].pos, cameras[1].ang)")
            add("        return { origin = wp, angles = wa, fov = cameras[1].fov, drawviewer = true }")
            add("    end")
            add("end)")
        end

        return table.concat(lines, "\n"), warnings
    end

    function LuaExporter.saveToFile(name, codeStr)
        if not file or not file.write then return false, "file.write unavailable" end
        if not file.exists(ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR) then pcall(file.createDir, ProjectStorage.ROOT_DIR) end
        if not file.exists(ProjectStorage.EXPORT_DIR) then pcall(file.createDir, ProjectStorage.EXPORT_DIR) end
        local safe = ProjectStorage.sanitizeName(name)
        local path = ProjectStorage.EXPORT_DIR .. "/" .. safe .. ".txt"
        local ok, err = pcall(file.write, path, codeStr)
        if not ok then return false, tostring(err) end
        return true, path
    end

    SOLACE.LuaExporter = LuaExporter

    -- Phase 14: AI-Friendly Scene IR JSON Interchange
    local IRInterchange = {
        ready = true,
        phase = 14,
    }

    function IRInterchange.exportJSON(sceneGraph, state, opts)
        opts = opts or {}
        if not json or not json.encode then return nil, "json API unavailable" end
        local doc = ProjectStorage.buildDocument(sceneGraph, state, {
            includeDescriptions = (opts.includeDescriptions ~= false),
        })
        local ok, out = pcall(json.encode, doc, opts.pretty ~= false)
        if not ok then return nil, tostring(out) end
        return out, nil
    end

    function IRInterchange.importJSON(jsonStr, sceneGraph, state, cmdManager)
        if not json or not json.decode then return false, "json API unavailable" end
        if type(jsonStr) ~= "string" or string.trim(jsonStr) == "" then
            return false, "Empty JSON input"
        end
        local okDec, doc = pcall(json.decode, jsonStr)
        if not okDec then return false, "JSON syntax error: " .. tostring(doc) end
        local okApp, err, warnings = ProjectStorage.applyDocument(doc, sceneGraph, state, cmdManager)
        if not okApp then return false, err end
        return true, warnings
    end

    SOLACE.IRInterchange = IRInterchange
end)(SOLACE) end

-- ============================================================================
-- SECTION 20: Main execution loop
-- ============================================================================
do (function(SOLACE)
    local Constants    = SOLACE.Constants
    local IR           = SOLACE.IR
    local Math3D       = SOLACE.Math3D
    local Events       = SOLACE.Events
    local Commands     = SOLACE.Commands
    local SceneGraph   = SOLACE.SceneGraph
    local SceneRuntime = SOLACE.SceneRuntime
    local Theme        = SOLACE.Theme
    local UIManager    = SOLACE.UIManager
    local Layout       = SOLACE.Layout
    local ViewportGrid = SOLACE.ViewportGrid
    local Freecam      = SOLACE.Freecam
    local Selection    = SOLACE.Selection
    local Gizmos       = SOLACE.Gizmos
    local Toolbar      = SOLACE.Toolbar
    local Toolbox      = SOLACE.Toolbox
    local Explorer     = SOLACE.Explorer
    local Properties   = SOLACE.Properties
    local Timeline     = SOLACE.Timeline
    local ImGui        = SOLACE.ImGui
    local Windows      = SOLACE.Windows




    Theme.initFonts()

    local events     = Events.new()
    local commands   = Commands.new(events, 100)
    local sceneGraph = SceneGraph.new(events)
    local runtime    = SceneRuntime.new(sceneGraph, events)
    local state      = Constants.createDefaultState()
    local ui         = UIManager.new(state)
    local layout     = Layout.new(state)
    local camera     = Freecam.new(state)

    local hudConnected = false
    local lastFrameTime = timer.realtime()
    local fpsSmooth = 60

    local HIDDEN_HUD_ELEMENTS = {
        CHudHealth = true,
        CHudBattery = true,
        CHudAmmo = true,
        CHudSecondaryAmmo = true,
        CHudCrosshair = true,
        CHudWeaponSelection = true,
        CHudDamageIndicator = true,
        CHudSuitPower = true,
        CHudHintDisplay = true,
        CHudZoom = true,
        CHudPoisonDamageIndicator = true,
        CHudSquadStatus = true,
    }

    local f6Down = false
    local drawErrorLogged = false
    local firstDrawLogged = false

    local function isHudActiveSafe()
        if type(render.isHUDActive) == "function" then
            return render.isHUDActive()
        end
        return hasPermission("render.hud")
    end

    local function formatDiagState(prefix)
        return string.format(
            "%s | studioOpen=%s | hudActive=%s | hudConn=%s | perms(hud=%s, calcview=%s, cursor=%s, input=%s, holo=%s)",
            prefix,
            tostring(state.studioOpen),
            tostring(isHudActiveSafe()),
            tostring(hudConnected),
            tostring(hasPermission("render.hud")),
            tostring(hasPermission("render.calcview")),
            tostring(hasPermission("input.enablecursor")),
            tostring(hasPermission("input")),
            tostring(hasPermission("hologram.create"))
        )
    end

    local lowCpuUntil = 0
    local diagState = {
        errorCount = 0,
        lastError = nil,
        lastErrKey = nil,
        lastCacheTime = -1,
        lines = {},
        maxLineW = 240,
    }

    local function recordDiagError(source, err)
        local msg = tostring(err or "unknown error")
        local key = tostring(source) .. ":" .. msg
        diagState.lastError = tostring(source) .. ": " .. msg
        if diagState.lastErrKey ~= key then
            diagState.lastErrKey = key
            diagState.errorCount = diagState.errorCount + 1
            events:log("error", diagState.lastError)
            printConsole("[SOLACE ERR] " .. diagState.lastError)
        end
    end

    local function drawDebugOverlay(vpRect, cpuRatio)
        if not state.debugMode or not state.studioOpen or not vpRect then return end
        if vpRect.w < 240 or vpRect.h < 130 then return end

        local now = timer.realtime()
        if now - diagState.lastCacheTime >= 0.25 or #diagState.lines == 0 then
            diagState.lastCacheTime = now
            local ramMb = ramUsed and (ramUsed() / 1024) or 0
            local holosLeft = (hologram and hologram.hologramsLeft) and hologram.hologramsLeft() or 0
            local holoCount = 0
            local nodeCount = 0
            for _ in pairs(sceneGraph:getAllNodes()) do
                nodeCount = nodeCount + 1
            end
            for _, ent in pairs(runtime:getEntries()) do
                if ent.holo and isValid(ent.holo) then
                    holoCount = holoCount + 1
                end
            end

            local rtOk = (type(render.createRenderTarget) == "function")
            local rtErr = runtime:getLastError()
            local errText = diagState.lastError or rtErr
            if errText and #errText > 38 then
                errText = string.sub(errText, 1, 35) .. "..."
            end

            diagState.lines = {
                { text = string.format("DEBUG | %.0f FPS | CPU %d%% | %.1fMB", fpsSmooth, math.floor(cpuRatio * 100 + 0.5), ramMb), col = Theme.C.accentBright },
                { text = string.format("HUD:%s CV:%s INP:%s CUR:%s HOLO:%s",
                    hudConnected and "OK" or "NO",
                    hasPermission("render.calcview") and "OK" or "NO",
                    hasPermission("input") and "OK" or "NO",
                    hasPermission("input.enablecursor") and "OK" or "NO",
                    hasPermission("hologram.create") and "OK" or "NO"), col = Theme.C.textPrimary },
                { text = string.format("Nodes:%d Holos:%d(L:%d) RT:%s U/R:%d/%d",
                    math.max(0, nodeCount - 1), holoCount, holosLeft,
                    rtOk and "ON" or "OFF",
                    #commands._undoStack, #commands._redoStack), col = Theme.C.textSecondary },
                { text = string.format("Tool:%s Axis:%s Drag:%s Look:%s",
                    tostring(state.activeTool),
                    tostring(Gizmos.activeAxis or Gizmos.hoveredAxis or "-"),
                    Gizmos.dragging and "YES" or "NO",
                    state.viewportLooking and "YES" or "NO"), col = Theme.C.textSecondary },
                { text = errText and string.format("Err(%d): %s", diagState.errorCount, errText) or "Errors: 0 (All subsystems nominal)",
                    col = errText and Theme.C.danger or Theme.C.success },
            }

            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            local mw = 220
            for i = 1, #diagState.lines do
                local tw = render.getTextSize(diagState.lines[i].text) or 180
                if tw > mw then mw = tw end
            end
            diagState.maxLineW = math.min(math.max(220, vpRect.w - 24), mw + 16)
        end

        local lineH = 15
        local pad = 8
        local bw = diagState.maxLineW
        local bh = #diagState.lines * lineH + pad * 2
        local bx = vpRect.x + vpRect.w - bw - 10
        local by = vpRect.y + vpRect.h - bh - 10
        if by < vpRect.y + 44 then return end

        Theme.drawRoundedRect(4, bx, by, bw, bh, Theme.C.hudPill, 225)
        Theme.setColor(diagState.lastError and Theme.C.danger or Theme.C.borderLight)
        render.drawRectOutline(bx, by, bw, bh, 1)

        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
        for i = 1, #diagState.lines do
            local item = diagState.lines[i]
            Theme.setColor(item.col)
            render.drawSimpleText(bx + pad, by + pad + (i - 1) * lineH, item.text, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.TOP)
        end
    end

    local function isOverlayOrUnfocused()
        if game.hasFocus and not game.hasFocus() then return true end
        if game.isPauseMenuVisible and game.isPauseMenuVisible() then return true end
        return false
    end

    local function getCpuRatio()
        local avgFn = cpuAverage or quotaAverage
        local maxFn = cpuMax or quotaMax
        if not avgFn or not maxFn then return 0 end
        local cap = maxFn()
        if cap <= 0 then return 0 end
        local ratio = avgFn() / cap
        if cpuUsed then
            local usedRatio = cpuUsed() / cap
            if usedRatio > ratio then ratio = usedRatio end
        end
        return ratio
    end

    local function syncCursor()
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() then return end
        if not hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then return end
        local wantCursor = hudConnected and state.studioOpen and (not state.viewportLooking)
        if hasPermission("input") then
            if input.getCursorVisible() ~= wantCursor then
                input.enableCursor(wantCursor)
            end
        else
            input.enableCursor(wantCursor)
        end
    end

    local function setStudioOpen(open)
        state.studioOpen = open
        if not open then
            if Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
            end
            state.viewportLooking = false
            ui.focusedInputId = nil
            ui.activeId = nil
            ui:cancelAutoScroll()
        elseif not camera.initialized then
            camera:initFromPlayer()
        end
        syncCursor()
    end

    local function toggleStudio()
        local nextOpen = true
        if hudConnected then
            nextOpen = not state.studioOpen
        end
        if nextOpen and not isHudActiveSafe() and isValid(player()) and player() == owner() and hasPermission("enablehud") then
            pcall(enableHud, nil, true)
        end
        if isHudActiveSafe() then
            hudConnected = true
        elseif nextOpen and not hasPermission("render.hud") then
            print("[SOLACE] HUD not connected yet — press E (Use) on the Starfall chip or link a Starfall HUD component.")
        end
        setStudioOpen(nextOpen)
        printConsole(formatDiagState(nextOpen and "[SOLACE] Studio opened (F6)" or "[SOLACE] Studio minimized (F6)"))
    end

    local function resolveOrigin()
        ViewportGrid.resolveOrigin(state)
        sceneGraph:setOrigin(state.grid.originPos)
    end

    local function getTargetContainerParentId()
        local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        if sel and (sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP or sel.kind == Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER) then
            return sel.id
        end
        return sceneGraph:getRootId()
    end

    local actions = {}
    actions.hasSelection = false
    actions.canUndo = false
    actions.canRedo = false
    actions.undoLabel = nil
    actions.redoLabel = nil
    actions.cmdManager = commands

    function actions.toggleStudio(open)
        setStudioOpen(open)
    end

    function actions.setTool(toolId)
        if state.activeTool == toolId then return end
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
        end
        state.activeTool = toolId
    end

    function actions.undo()
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
        end
        return commands:undo()
    end

    function actions.redo()
        if Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
        end
        return commands:redo()
    end

    function actions.resolveOrigin()
        resolveOrigin()
    end

    function actions.focusSelected()
        camera:focusNode(sceneGraph, runtime)
    end

    local function cloneTransformSnap(t)
        return {
            pos = IR.cloneVec3(t.pos),
            ang = IR.cloneVec3(t.ang),
            scale = IR.cloneVec3(t.scale),
            pivot = IR.cloneVec3(t.pivot),
            pivotAng = IR.cloneVec3(t.pivotAng),
        }
    end

    local function copyList(tbl)
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, #tbl do out[i] = tbl[i] end
        return out
    end

    local function worldSpawnToParentLocal(parentId, wPosTbl, wAngTbl)
        local wp = IR.toVector(wPosTbl or { 0, 0, 0 })
        local wa = IR.toAngle(wAngTbl or { 0, 0, 0 })
        local lPos, lAng = Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, parentId, wp, wa, Vector(1, 1, 1))
        return lPos, lAng
    end

    function actions.spawnQuickModel(name, modelPath, kindOverride)
        local wPosTbl, wAngTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(parentId) then parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId() end
        local lPos, lAng = worldSpawnToParentLocal(parentId, wPosTbl, wAngTbl)
        local kind = kindOverride or Constants.NODE_KINDS.MODEL
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(kind, name or "Model", lPos, lAng, { appearance = { model = modelPath } })
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Spawn " .. (name or "Model"), snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.spawnSprite(name, matPath)
        local wPosTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(parentId) then parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId() end
        local lPos = worldSpawnToParentLocal(parentId, wPosTbl, { 0, 0, 0 })
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(Constants.NODE_KINDS.SPRITE, name or "Sprite", lPos, { 0, 0, 0 }, { appearance = { material = matPath } })
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Spawn " .. (name or "Sprite"), snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.spawnEffect(name, preset)
        local wPosTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = getTargetContainerParentId()
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(parentId) then parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId() end
        local lPos = worldSpawnToParentLocal(parentId, wPosTbl, { 0, 0, 0 })
        local fx = IR.createEffectPayload(preset)
        local kind = (fx.mode == "light") and Constants.NODE_KINDS.LIGHT or Constants.NODE_KINDS.EFFECT
        local col = (preset and preset.color) or { 255, 220, 140, 255 }
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(kind, name or "VFX", lPos, { 0, 0, 0 }, {
            appearance = { material = fx.material, color = col },
            effect = fx,
        })
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Spawn " .. (name or "VFX"), snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.spawnCamera(name)
        local wPos = camera:getPos()
        local wAng = camera:getAng()
        local parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local lPos, lAng = Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, parentId, wPos, wAng, Vector(1, 1, 1))
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA, name or "Camera", lPos, lAng, {
            fov = state.camera.fov or 80,
        })
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Spawn Camera", snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.linkWorldRagdoll(name, modelPath, ent)
        if not IsValid(ent) then return false end
        local wPos = ent:getPos()
        local wAng = ent:getAngles()
        local parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local lPos, lAng = Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, parentId, wPos, wAng, Vector(1, 1, 1))
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(Constants.NODE_KINDS.RAGDOLL, name or "Ragdoll", lPos, lAng, {
            appearance = { model = modelPath },
        })
        snap.root.meta = { worldEnt = ent }
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local ok = commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Link Ragdoll", snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
        if ok then
            runtime:linkWorldEntity(snap.root.id, ent)
        end
        return ok
    end

    function actions.spawnGroup(parentOverride)
        local wPosTbl = camera:getSpawnTransform(state.spawnDistance)
        local parentId = parentOverride or getTargetContainerParentId()
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(parentId) then parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId() end
        local lPos = worldSpawnToParentLocal(parentId, wPosTbl, { 0, 0, 0 })
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP, "Group", lPos, { 0, 0, 0 })
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Create Group", snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.spawnFolder(parentOverride)
        local parentId = parentOverride or getTargetContainerParentId()
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(parentId) then parentId = sceneGraph:getRootId() end
        local snap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER, "Folder", { 0, 0, 0 }, { 0, 0, 0 })
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAddNodeCommand(sceneGraph, "Create Folder", snap, parentId, nil, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.deleteSelected()
        local ids = sceneGraph:getTopLevelSelectedIds()
        if #ids == 0 then return false end
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()

        local specs = {}
        for i = 1, #ids do
            local id = ids[i]
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if node and id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                local pId = node.parentId or sceneGraph:getRootId()
                specs[#specs + 1] = {
                    rootId = id,
                    parentId = pId,
                    index = sceneGraph:getChildIndex(pId, id) or 1,
                    snapshot = sceneGraph:cloneSubtreeSnapshot(id, true),
                }
            end
        end
        if #specs == 0 then return false end
        table.sort(specs, function(a, b) return a.index < b.index end)
        return commands:execute(Commands.createDeleteCommand(sceneGraph, #specs > 1 and "Delete (" .. #specs .. ")" or "Delete", specs, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.duplicateSelected()
        local ids = sceneGraph:getTopLevelSelectedIds()
        if #ids == 0 then return false end
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local off = state.duplicateOffset or Constants.DUPLICATE_OFFSET or { 16, 16, 0 }
        local wOff = Vector(off[1] or 16, off[2] or 16, off[3] or 0)

        local specs = {}
        for i = 1, #ids do
            local id = ids[i]
            local src = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if src and id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                local pId = src.parentId or sceneGraph:getRootId()
                local pNode = sceneGraph:getNode(pId)
                local snap = sceneGraph:cloneSubtreeSnapshot(id, false, " (Copy)")
                if snap and snap.root then
                    if snap.root.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.FOLDER and snap.root.transform then
                        local invRot = Math3D.getParentInverseRotation(sceneGraph, pId)
                        local localOff = Math3D.worldDeltaToParentLocal(invRot, wOff)
                        local pos = snap.root.transform.pos
                        pos[1] = math.round(pos[1] + localOff[1], 3)
                        pos[2] = math.round(pos[2] + localOff[2], 3)
                        pos[3] = math.round(pos[3] + localOff[3], 3)
                    end
                    local idx = sceneGraph:getChildIndex(pId, id)
                    specs[#specs + 1] = {
                        parentId = pId,
                        rootId = snap.root.id,
                        snapshot = snap,
                        insertIndex = idx and (idx + 1) or (pNode and (#pNode.children + 1) or 1),
                    }
                end
            end
        end
        if #specs == 0 then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createDuplicateCommand(sceneGraph, #specs > 1 and "Duplicate (" .. #specs .. ")" or "Duplicate", specs, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.reparentNodes(nodeIds, targetParentId, insertIndex)
        if not nodeIds or #nodeIds == 0 or not targetParentId then return false end
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(targetParentId) then return false end
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()
        local targetParent = sceneGraph:getNode(targetParentId)
        if not targetParent then return false end

        -- Keep only valid non-root nodes not descending from another node in nodeIds
        local idSet = {}
        for i = 1, #nodeIds do idSet[nodeIds[i]] = true end

        local entries = {}
        local baseIdx = insertIndex or (#targetParent.children + 1)
        local anyChanged = false
        for i = 1, #nodeIds do
            local id = nodeIds[i]
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if node and id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() then
                local skipAnc = false
                local cur = node.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(node.parentId) or nil
                while cur and cur.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId() do
                    if idSet[cur.id] then skipAnc = true; break end
                    cur = cur.parentId and sceneGraph:getNode(cur.parentId) or nil
                end
                if not skipAnc and sceneGraph:canReparent(id, targetParentId) then
                    local oldP = node.parentId
                    local oldIdx = sceneGraph:getChildIndex(oldP, id) or 1
                    local reqIdx = baseIdx + #entries
                    local effIdx = reqIdx
                    if oldP == targetParentId and oldIdx < reqIdx then
                        effIdx = effIdx - 1
                    end
                    effIdx = math.clamp(effIdx, 1, #targetParent.children + (oldP == targetParentId and 0 or 1))
                    if oldP ~= targetParentId or oldIdx ~= effIdx then
                        anyChanged = true
                    end

                    local wPos, wAng, wScale = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, id)
                    local lPos, lAng, lScale = Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, targetParentId, wPos, wAng, wScale)
                    entries[#entries + 1] = {
                        id = id,
                        oldParentId = oldP,
                        oldIndex = oldIdx,
                        oldTransform = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform),
                        newParentId = targetParentId,
                        newIndex = reqIdx,
                        newTransform = {
                            pos = lPos,
                            ang = lAng,
                            scale = lScale,
                            pivot = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pivot),
                        },
                    }
                end
            end
        end

        if #entries == 0 or not anyChanged then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createReparentCommand(sceneGraph, "Reparent", entries, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.groupSelected()
        local ids = sceneGraph:getTopLevelSelectedIds()
        if #ids == 0 then return false end
        local prevSel = copyList(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds())
        local prevPrim = sceneGraph:getSelectedId()

        local primary = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        local firstNode = sceneGraph:getNode(ids[1])
        local groupParentId = (primary and primary.parentId) or (firstNode and firstNode.parentId) or sceneGraph:getRootId()
        if not sceneGraph:canAcceptChild(groupParentId) then
            groupParentId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        end

        -- Place the new Group at the average world position of selected nodes
        local sumPos = Vector(0, 0, 0)
        local count = 0
        local worldCache = {}
        for i = 1, #ids do
            local id = ids[i]
            local wPos, wAng, wScale = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, id)
            worldCache[id] = { wPos = wPos, wAng = wAng, wScale = wScale }
            sumPos = sumPos + wPos
            count = count + 1
        end
        local avgWPos = (count > 0) and (sumPos / count) or Vector(0, 0, 0)
        local grpLocalPos = Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, groupParentId, avgWPos, Angle(0, 0, 0), Vector(1, 1, 1))
        local groupSnap = sceneGraph:buildNodeSnapshot(Constants.NODE_KINDS.GROUP, "Group", grpLocalPos, { 0, 0, 0 })

        -- Compute Group's world frame directly so child local transforms preserve world transforms
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local grpWPos, grpWAng, grpWScale
        if groupParentId == rootId then
            grpWPos = Vector(grpLocalPos[1], grpLocalPos[2], grpLocalPos[3])
            grpWAng = Angle(0, 0, 0)
            grpWScale = Vector(1, 1, 1)
        else
            local pWPos, pWAng, pWScale = Math3D.computeWorldTransform(sceneGraph, groupParentId)
            local pMat = Matrix()
            pMat:translate(pWPos)
            pMat:rotate(pWAng)
            grpWPos = pMat * Vector(grpLocalPos[1], grpLocalPos[2], grpLocalPos[3])
            grpWAng = pWAng
            grpWScale = pWScale
        end

        local grpMat = Matrix()
        grpMat:translate(grpWPos)
        grpMat:rotate(grpWAng)
        local grpInv = grpMat:getInverseTR()

        local minS, maxS = Constants.MIN_SCALE or 0.05, Constants.MAX_SCALE or 100.0
        local function safeDiv(a, b)
            if math.abs(b or 1) < 1e-4 then return a end
            return a / b
        end

        local moveEntries = {}
        for i = 1, #ids do
            local id = ids[i]
            local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
            if node and id ~= rootId then
                local wc = worldCache[id]
                local lPos = grpInv * wc.wPos
                local mWorld = Matrix()
                mWorld:setAngles(wc.wAng)
                local lAng = (grpInv * mWorld):getAngles()
                moveEntries[#moveEntries + 1] = {
                    id = id,
                    oldParentId = node.parentId,
                    oldIndex = sceneGraph:getChildIndex(node.parentId, id) or 1,
                    oldTransform = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform),
                    newTransform = {
                        pos = { math.round(lPos[1], 3), math.round(lPos[2], 3), math.round(lPos[3], 3) },
                        ang = { math.round(math.normalizeAngle(lAng[1]), 2), math.round(math.normalizeAngle(lAng[2]), 2), math.round(math.normalizeAngle(lAng[3]), 2) },
                        scale = {
                            math.clamp(math.round(safeDiv(wc.wScale[1], grpWScale[1]), 3), minS, maxS),
                            math.clamp(math.round(safeDiv(wc.wScale[2], grpWScale[2]), 3), minS, maxS),
                            math.clamp(math.round(safeDiv(wc.wScale[3], grpWScale[3]), 3), minS, maxS),
                        },
                        pivot = IR.cloneVec3(node.transform.pivot),
                    },
                }
            end
        end
        if #moveEntries == 0 then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createGroupSelectedCommand(sceneGraph, "Group Selected", groupSnap, groupParentId, moveEntries, prevSel, prevPrim))
    end

    function actions.renameNode(id, newName)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local clean = string.trim(tostring(newName or ""))
        if clean == "" or clean == node.name then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createRenameCommand(sceneGraph, id, node.name, clean))
    end

    function actions.setNodeTransformAxis(id, field, axisIdx, numVal)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.transform or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        if type(numVal) ~= "number" or numVal ~= numVal or math.abs(numVal) == math.huge then
            return false
        end
        local vec = node.transform[field]
        if not vec or axisIdx < 1 or axisIdx > 3 then return false end

        local v = numVal
        if field == "scale" then
            v = math.clamp(math.round(v, 3), Constants.MIN_SCALE or 0.05, Constants.MAX_SCALE or 100.0)
        elseif field == "ang" then
            v = math.round(v, 2)
        else
            v = math.round(v, 3)
        end

        if math.abs((vec[axisIdx] or 0) - v) < 1e-4 then
            return false
        end

        local before = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        local after = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        after[field][axisIdx] = v
        local label = (field == "pos" and "Position") or (field == "ang" and "Rotation") or "Scale"
        return commands:execute(Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, "Edit " .. label, {
            { id = id, before = before, after = after },
        }))
    end

    function actions.toggleVisibility(targetId)
        local ids = {}
        local rootId = sceneGraph:getRootId()
        if targetId then
            if targetId ~= rootId and sceneGraph:getNode(targetId) then
                ids[1] = targetId
            end
        else
            for _, id in ipairs(sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()) do
                if id ~= rootId and sceneGraph:getNode(id) then
                    ids[#ids + 1] = id
                end
            end
        end
        if #ids == 0 then return false end
        local entries = {}
        for i = 1, #ids do
            local n = sceneGraph:getNode(ids[i])
            if n then
                entries[#entries + 1] = {
                    id = n.id,
                    before = { visible = n.visible },
                    after = { visible = not n.visible },
                }
            end
        end
        if #entries == 0 then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAppearanceCommand(sceneGraph, "Toggle Visibility", entries))
    end

    function actions.setNodeColorChannel(id, chanIdx, val)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.appearance or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        if type(val) ~= "number" or val ~= val or math.abs(val) == math.huge then return false end
        if chanIdx < 1 or chanIdx > 4 then return false end
        local c = node.appearance.color or { 255, 255, 255, 255 }
        local clamped = math.clamp(math.floor(val + 0.5), 0, 255)
        if (c[chanIdx] or 255) == clamped then return false end

        local beforeCol = { c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, c[4] or 255 }
        local afterCol = { c[1] or 255, c[2] or 255, c[3] or 255, c[4] or 255 }
        afterCol[chanIdx] = clamped
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAppearanceCommand(sceneGraph, "Edit Color", {
            { id = id, before = { color = beforeCol }, after = { color = afterCol } },
        }))
    end

    function actions.setNodeMaterial(id, rawMat)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.appearance or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then
            return false, "Invalid node"
        end
        local ok, resOrErr = Properties.validateMaterialPath(rawMat)
        if not ok then
            return false, resOrErr
        end
        local oldMat = node.appearance.material or ""
        if resOrErr == oldMat then
            return true, nil
        end
        commands:execute(Commands.createAppearanceCommand(sceneGraph, resOrErr == "" and "Clear Material" or "Set Material", {
            { id = id, before = { material = oldMat }, after = { material = resOrErr } },
        }))
        return true, nil
    end

    function actions.detachNodes(nodeIds)
        return actions.reparentNodes(nodeIds, sceneGraph:getRootId(), nil)
    end

    function actions.setNodePivotAxis(id, field, axisIdx, val)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.transform or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        if type(val) ~= "number" or val ~= val or math.abs(val) == math.huge then return false end
        local vec = node.transform[field]
        if not vec or axisIdx < 1 or axisIdx > 3 then return false end
        local v = (field == "pivotAng") and math.round(math.normalizeAngle(val), 2) or math.round(val, 3)
        if math.abs((vec[axisIdx] or 0) - v) < 1e-4 then return false end
        local before = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        local after = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        after[field][axisIdx] = v
        return commands:execute(Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, "Edit Pivot", {
            { id = id, before = before, after = after },
        }))
    end

    function actions.centerNodePivot(id)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.transform or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local center = Math3D.computeLocalBoundsCenter(sceneGraph, runtime, id)
        local before = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        local after = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        after.pivot = { math.round(center[1], 3), math.round(center[2], 3), math.round(center[3], 3) }
        return commands:execute(Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, "Center Pivot", {
            { id = id, before = before, after = after },
        }))
    end

    function actions.resetNodePivot(id)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.transform or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local before = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        local after = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        after.pivot = { 0, 0, 0 }
        after.pivotAng = { 0, 0, 0 }
        return commands:execute(Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, "Reset Pivot", {
            { id = id, before = before, after = after },
        }))
    end

    function actions.setNodeAppearanceProp(id, prop, val, label)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.appearance or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local oldVal = node.appearance[prop]
        if oldVal == val then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createAppearanceCommand(sceneGraph, label or ("Edit " .. prop), {
            { id = id, before = { [prop] = oldVal }, after = { [prop] = val } },
        }))
    end

    function actions.setNodeSubmaterial(id, slotIdx, matPath)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or not node.appearance or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local clean = string.trim(tostring(matPath or ""))
        if clean ~= "" then
            local ok, resOrErr = Properties.validateMaterialPath(clean)
            if not ok then return false, resOrErr end
            clean = resOrErr
        end
        node.appearance.submaterials = node.appearance.submaterials or {}
        local beforeVal = node.appearance.submaterials[slotIdx] or ""
        if beforeVal == clean then return true end
        local afterVal = (clean ~= "") and clean or nil
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Submaterial #" .. slotIdx,
            function()
                node.appearance.submaterials[slotIdx] = (beforeVal ~= "") and beforeVal or nil
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end,
            function()
                node.appearance.submaterials[slotIdx] = afterVal
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end))
    end

    function actions.applyMaterialToSelected(matPath)
        local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
        if not sel or sel.id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        return actions.setNodeMaterial(sel.id, matPath)
    end

    function actions.setNodeBoneAngleAxis(id, boneIdx, axisIdx, val)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        if type(val) ~= "number" or val ~= val or math.abs(val) == math.huge then return false end
        node.bones = node.bones or {}
        local cur = node.bones[boneIdx] or { pos = { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = { 0, 0, 0 }, scale = { 1, 1, 1 } }
        local oldAng = IR.cloneVec3(cur.ang)
        local newAng = IR.cloneVec3(cur.ang)
        newAng[axisIdx] = math.round(math.normalizeAngle(val), 2)
        if math.abs((oldAng[axisIdx] or 0) - newAng[axisIdx]) < 1e-3 then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Pose Bone #" .. boneIdx,
            function()
                node.bones[boneIdx] = node.bones[boneIdx] or { pos = { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = { 0, 0, 0 }, scale = { 1, 1, 1 } }
                node.bones[boneIdx].ang = IR.cloneVec3(oldAng)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end,
            function()
                node.bones[boneIdx] = node.bones[boneIdx] or { pos = { 0, 0, 0 }, ang = { 0, 0, 0 }, scale = { 1, 1, 1 } }
                node.bones[boneIdx].ang = IR.cloneVec3(newAng)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end))
    end

    function actions.resetNodeBones(id)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local beforeBones = {}
        for k, b in pairs(node.bones or {}) do
            beforeBones[k] = { pos = IR.cloneVec3(b.pos), ang = IR.cloneVec3(b.ang), scale = IR.cloneVec3(b.scale or { 1, 1, 1 }) }
        end
        if next(beforeBones) == nil then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Reset Bones",
            function()
                node.bones = {}
                for k, b in pairs(beforeBones) do
                    node.bones[k] = { pos = IR.cloneVec3(b.pos), ang = IR.cloneVec3(b.ang), scale = IR.cloneVec3(b.scale) }
                end
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end,
            function()
                node.bones = {}
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end))
    end

    function actions.matchCameraToFreecam(camId)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(camId)
        if not node or node.kind ~= Constants.NODE_KINDS.CAMERA then return false end
        local parentId = node.parentId or sceneGraph:getRootId()
        local lPos, lAng = Math3D.worldTransformToParentLocal(sceneGraph, parentId, camera:getPos(), camera:getAng(), Vector(1, 1, 1))
        local before = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        local after = cloneTransformSnap(node.transform)
        after.pos = lPos
        after.ang = lAng
        return commands:execute(Commands.createTransformCommand(sceneGraph, "Match Camera View", {
            { id = camId, before = before, after = after },
        }))
    end

    function actions.addKeyframe(id, trackKey, t)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        local Anim = SOLACE.Animation
        if not node or not Anim or id == sceneGraph:getRootId() then return false end
        local before = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(node.tracks)
        local idx = Anim.addOrUpdateKeyframe(node, trackKey, t, nil, state.timeline.defaultEasing or "linear")
        if not idx then return false end
        local after = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(node.tracks)
        state.timeline.selectedTrackKey = trackKey
        state.timeline.selectedKeyframeIdx = idx
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Add Keyframe",
            function()
                node.tracks = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(before)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end,
            function()
                node.tracks = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(after)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end))
    end

    function actions.removeKeyframe(id, trackKey, idx)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        local Anim = SOLACE.Animation
        if not node or not Anim then return false end
        local before = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(node.tracks)
        if not Anim.removeKeyframe(node, trackKey, idx) then return false end
        local after = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(node.tracks)
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Delete Keyframe",
            function()
                node.tracks = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(before)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end,
            function()
                node.tracks = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(after)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end))
    end

    function actions.setKeyframeEasing(id, trackKey, idx, ease)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        local Anim = SOLACE.Animation
        local trk = node and node.tracks and node.tracks[trackKey]
        local kf = trk and trk.keyframes and trk.keyframes[idx]
        if not kf or not Anim then return false end
        local oldEase = kf.easing or "linear"
        if oldEase == ease then return false end
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Keyframe Easing",
            function() kf.easing = oldEase end,
            function() kf.easing = ease end))
    end

    function actions.toggleTrackEnabled(id, trackKey)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        if not node then return false end
        node.tracks = node.tracks or {}
        if not node.tracks[trackKey] then
            node.tracks[trackKey] = IR.createTrack(trackKey)
        end
        local trk = node.tracks[trackKey]
        local oldEn = trk.enabled ~= false
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand("Toggle Track",
            function() trk.enabled = oldEn end,
            function() trk.enabled = not oldEn end))
    end

    function actions.commitTrackSnapshot(id, label, beforeTracks)
        local node = sceneGraph:getNode(id)
        local Anim = SOLACE.Animation
        if not node or not Anim or not beforeTracks then return false end
        local after = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(node.tracks)
        return commands:execute(Commands.createActionCommand(label or "Edit Keyframe",
            function()
                node.tracks = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(beforeTracks)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end,
            function()
                node.tracks = Anim.cloneTracksSnapshot(after)
                sceneGraph:notifyNodeUpdated(id)
            end))
    end

    SOLACE._ctx = {
        state = state,
        events = events,
        commands = commands,
        sceneGraph = sceneGraph,
        runtime = runtime,
        ui = ui,
        layout = layout,
        camera = camera,
        actions = actions,
    }

    local startupMsg = formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] Loaded")
    print(startupMsg)
    printConsole(startupMsg)

    local permPrompted = false
    local function ensurePermissionsPrompted()
        if permPrompted then return end
        if type(permissionRequestSatisfied) == "function" and type(sendPermissionRequest) == "function" then
            local okSat, sat = pcall(permissionRequestSatisfied)
            if okSat and not sat and isHudActiveSafe() then
                permPrompted = true
                pcall(sendPermissionRequest)
            end
        end
    end

    timer.simple(0.05, function()
        resolveOrigin()
        camera:initFromPlayer()
        if not isHudActiveSafe() and isValid(player()) and player() == owner() and hasPermission("enablehud") then
            pcall(enableHud, nil, true)
        end
        if isHudActiveSafe() then
            hudConnected = true
            syncCursor()
            ensurePermissionsPrompted()
        end
        printConsole(formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] Post-init"))
    end)

    timer.simple(0.35, function()
        if not isHudActiveSafe() and not hudConnected then
            print("[SOLACE] Waiting for HUD connection — press E (Use) on the Starfall chip or link a Starfall HUD component, then press F6.")
        end
    end)

    hook.add("StarfallUsed", "solace_cl_used", function(activator, used)
        if used ~= chip() or activator ~= player() then return end
