        if not isHudActiveSafe() and hasPermission("enablehud") then
            pcall(enableHud, nil, true)
        end
        if isHudActiveSafe() then
            hudConnected = true
            ensurePermissionsPrompted()
        end
        setStudioOpen(true)
        local msg = formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] Chip used (E)")
        print(msg)
        printConsole(msg)
    end)

    hook.add("HUDConnected", "solace_hud_connected", function(ent, ply)
        if ply == player() then
            hudConnected = true
            if not camera.initialized then
                camera:initFromPlayer()
            end
            syncCursor()
            ensurePermissionsPrompted()
            printConsole(formatDiagState("[SOLACE CL] HUDConnected"))
        end
    end)

    hook.add("HUDDisconnected", "solace_hud_disconnected", function(ent, ply)
        if ply == player() then
            hudConnected = false
            if Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
            end
            state.viewportLooking = false
            ui.focusedInputId = nil
            ui.activeId = nil
            if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
                input.enableCursor(false)
            end
        end
    end)

    hook.add("HUDShouldDraw", "solace_hide_game_hud", function(name)
        if state.studioOpen and HIDDEN_HUD_ELEMENTS[name] then
            return false
        end
    end)

    hook.add("CalcView", "solace_freecam_view", function(origin, angles, fov, znear, zfar)
        if not state.studioOpen or not hudConnected then return end
        if not hasPermission("render.calcview") then return end
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() or getCpuRatio() > 0.72 then
            return camera._viewOut.origin and camera._viewOut or nil
        end
        return camera:calcView(origin, angles, fov, znear, zfar, sceneGraph)
    end)

    hook.add("MouseMoved", "solace_freecam_look", function(dx, dy)
        if state.studioOpen and state.viewportLooking then
            camera:onMouseMoved(dx, dy)
        end
    end)

    local lastThinkTime = timer.realtime()
    local lastDirtyVer = sceneGraph._version
    hook.add("Think", "solace_runtime_flush", function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local nowT = timer.realtime()
            local dtT = math.clamp(nowT - lastThinkTime, 0.001, 0.2)
            lastThinkTime = nowT

            if sceneGraph._version ~= lastDirtyVer then
                lastDirtyVer = sceneGraph._version
                if state.project then state.project.dirty = true end
            end

            if state.studioOpen then
                if SOLACE.Animation then
                    SOLACE.Animation.stepPlayback(state, sceneGraph, dtT)
                end
                if SOLACE.AssetScanner then
                    SOLACE.AssetScanner.stepScan(2)
                end
                if SOLACE.ProjectStorage then
                    SOLACE.ProjectStorage.stepAutosave(dtT, sceneGraph, state)
                end
                runtime:stepVFX(state, dtT)
            end

            runtime:flush()
            if hasPermission("input") then
                local isF6 = input.isKeyDown(KEY.F6)
                if isF6 and not f6Down then
                    f6Down = true
                    toggleStudio()
                elseif not isF6 and f6Down then
                    f6Down = false
                end
            end
        end)
        if not ok then
            recordDiagError("Think", err)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("Removed", "solace_cleanup", function()
        runtime:cleanup()
        if hasPermission("input.enablecursor") then
            input.enableCursor(false)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("InputPressed", "solace_input_pressed", function(key)
        if key == KEY.TAB and hasPermission("input") and input.isShiftDown() then
            lowCpuUntil = timer.realtime() + 0.8
            ui:cancelAutoScroll()
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.F6 then
            if not f6Down then
                f6Down = true
                toggleStudio()
            end
            return
        end

        if not state.studioOpen or isOverlayOrUnfocused() then return end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and state.activeCameraId then
            state.activeCameraId = nil
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and Explorer._draggingId then
            Explorer.cancelDrag(ui)
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and ImGui._activePopup then
            ImGui._activePopup = nil
            ImGui._activePopupRect = nil
            ImGui._activePopupCloseRequested = false
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and Gizmos.dragging then
            Gizmos.cancelDrag(sceneGraph)
            ui:release("gizmo_drag")
            return
        end

        if key == KEY.ESCAPE and ui.autoScroll then
            ui:cancelAutoScroll()
            return
        end

        if ui:handleKeyPressed(key) then
            return
        end

        local ctrl = hasPermission("input") and input.isControlDown() or false
        local shift = hasPermission("input") and input.isShiftDown() or false
        if ctrl then
            if key == KEY.Z then
                if shift then
                    actions.redo()
                else
                    actions.undo()
                end
                return
            elseif key == KEY.Y then
                actions.redo()
                return
            elseif key == KEY.D then
                actions.duplicateSelected()
                return
            end
        end

        if key == KEY.G then
            state.grid.enabled = not state.grid.enabled
        elseif key == KEY.X then
            state.transformSpace = (state.transformSpace == "local") and "world" or "local"
        elseif key == KEY.SPACE then
            state.timeline.playing = not state.timeline.playing
        elseif key == KEY.Q or key == KEY.KEY1 or key == KEY["1"] then
            actions.setTool("select")
        elseif key == KEY.KEY2 or key == KEY["2"] then
            actions.setTool("move")
        elseif key == KEY.E or key == KEY.KEY3 or key == KEY["3"] then
            actions.setTool("rotate")
        elseif key == KEY.R or key == KEY.KEY4 or key == KEY["4"] then
            actions.setTool("scale")
        elseif key == KEY.T or key == KEY.KEY5 or key == KEY["5"] then
            actions.setTool("pivot")
        elseif key == KEY.F then
            actions.focusSelected()
        elseif key == KEY.DELETE then
            actions.deleteSelected()
        elseif key == KEY.ESCAPE then
            sceneGraph:clearSelection()
        end
    end)

    hook.add("InputReleased", "solace_input_released", function(key)
        if key == KEY.F6 then
            f6Down = false
        end
    end)

    hook.add("InputBindPressed", "solace_block_binds", function(bind)
        if hudConnected and not isOverlayOrUnfocused() and ui:shouldBlockBind(bind) then
            return true
        end
    end)

    hook.add("MouseWheeled", "solace_mouse_wheel", function(delta)
        if not state.studioOpen or isOverlayOrUnfocused() then return end
        if state.viewportLooking then
            camera:onMouseWheelInViewport(delta)
        else
            ui:onMouseWheeled(delta)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("PostDrawOpaqueRenderables", "solace_viewport_3d", function(depth, skybox, skybox3d)
        if skybox or skybox3d then return end
        if not state.studioOpen or not hudConnected then return end
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() or timer.realtime() < lowCpuUntil or getCpuRatio() > 0.62 then return end
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            Theme.beginFrame()
            ViewportGrid.draw3D(state)
            Selection.draw3D(state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime)
        end)
        if not ok then
            recordDiagError("PostDrawOpaque", err)
        end
    end)

    hook.add("PostDrawTranslucentRenderables", "solace_viewport_sprites", function(depth, skybox, skybox3d)
        if skybox or skybox3d then return end
        if not state.studioOpen or not hudConnected then return end
        if isOverlayOrUnfocused() or timer.realtime() < lowCpuUntil or getCpuRatio() > 0.62 then return end
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            Theme.beginFrame()
            runtime:drawSprites(state, camera:getPos())
            runtime:drawVFX(state)
            Gizmos.draw3D(state, camera, sceneGraph)
        end)
        if not ok then
            recordDiagError("PostDrawTranslucent", err)
        end
    end)

    local rtSupported = (type(render.createRenderTarget) == "function")
        and (type(render.selectRenderTarget) == "function")
        and (type(render.setRenderTargetTexture) == "function")
        and (type(render.drawTexturedRectUV) == "function")

    local panelCaches = {
        toolbar    = { rtName = "sol_rt_tb",   w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        toolbox    = { rtName = "sol_rt_tbx",  w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        explorer   = { rtName = "sol_rt_exp",  w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        properties = { rtName = "sol_rt_prop", w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
        timeline   = { rtName = "sol_rt_tl",   w = 0, h = 0, sig = nil, wasInPanel = false, wasActive = false, lastHotId = nil, lastTooltip = nil, created = false, failed = false, localRect = { x = 0, y = 0, w = 0, h = 0 } },
    }

    local function drawCachedPanel(key, rect, sig, isActiveInPanel, allowRepaint, drawFn)
        local w, h = rect.w, rect.h
        if w <= 0 or h <= 0 then return end

        local c = panelCaches[key]
        local numStrips = math.ceil(w / 1024)
        local totalRTH = numStrips * (h + 2) - 2
        if not rtSupported or c.failed or h > 1024 or totalRTH > 1024 then
            if allowRepaint then
                drawFn(rect)
            end
            return
        end

        if not c.created then
            if not allowRepaint then return end
            local ok = pcall(render.createRenderTarget, c.rtName)
            if not ok then
                c.failed = true
                drawFn(rect)
                return
            end
            c.created = true
        end

        local inPanel = ui:pointInRect(rect.x, rect.y, w, h) and (ui._topHoverWinId == nil)
        local pointerChanged = (ui.dx ~= 0 or ui.dy ~= 0 or ui.leftPressed or ui.leftReleased or ui.leftDown or ui.rightPressed or ui.rightReleased or ui.middlePressed or ui.wheelDelta ~= 0)
        local dirty = (w ~= c.w) or (h ~= c.h) or (sig ~= c.sig)
            or (inPanel and (pointerChanged or not c.wasInPanel))
            or (not inPanel and c.wasInPanel)
            or isActiveInPanel or c.wasActive

        if not allowRepaint and c.w > 0 then
            dirty = false
        end

        if dirty then
            local realMx, realMy = ui.mx, ui.my
            local prevHot = ui.hotId
            local prevTip = ui._tooltipText
            ui.hotId = nil
            ui._tooltipText = nil

            render.selectRenderTarget(c.rtName)
            Theme.beginFrame()
            if numStrips == 1 then
                ui.mx = realMx - rect.x
                ui.my = realMy - rect.y
                ui._rtOffsetX = rect.x
                ui._rtOffsetY = rect.y
                c.localRect.x = 0
                c.localRect.y = 0
                c.localRect.w = w
                c.localRect.h = h
                drawFn(c.localRect)
            else
                local stripHotId, stripTip = nil, nil
                for s = 0, numStrips - 1 do
                    local offX = s * 1024
                    local offY = s * (h + 2)
                    local stripW = math.min(1024, w - offX)
                    ui.hotId = nil
                    ui._tooltipText = nil
                    ui.mx = realMx - rect.x - offX
                    ui.my = realMy - rect.y + offY
                    ui._rtOffsetX = rect.x + offX
                    ui._rtOffsetY = rect.y - offY
                    c.localRect.x = -offX
                    c.localRect.y = offY
                    c.localRect.w = w
                    c.localRect.h = h
                    ui:pushClip(0, offY, stripW, h)
                    drawFn(c.localRect)
                    ui:popClip()
                    if ui.hotId ~= nil then stripHotId = ui.hotId end
                    if ui._tooltipText ~= nil then stripTip = ui._tooltipText end
                end
                ui.hotId = stripHotId
                ui._tooltipText = stripTip
            end
            ui._rtOffsetX = 0
            ui._rtOffsetY = 0
            ui.mx = realMx
            ui.my = realMy
            render.selectRenderTarget(nil)
            Theme.beginFrame()

            c.lastHotId = inPanel and ui.hotId or nil
            c.lastTooltip = inPanel and ui._tooltipText or nil
            if not c.lastHotId then ui.hotId = prevHot end
            if not c.lastTooltip then ui._tooltipText = prevTip end

            c.w = w
            c.h = h
            c.sig = sig
            c.wasInPanel = inPanel
            c.wasActive = isActiveInPanel
        elseif inPanel and not ui.autoScroll then
            if c.lastHotId then ui.hotId = c.lastHotId end
            if c.lastTooltip then ui:setTooltip(c.lastTooltip) end
        end

        render.setRenderTargetTexture(c.rtName)
        Theme.setRGBA(255, 255, 255, 255)
        if numStrips == 1 then
            render.drawTexturedRectUV(rect.x, rect.y, w, h, 0, 0, w / 1024, h / 1024)
        else
            for s = 0, numStrips - 1 do
                local offX = s * 1024
                local offY = s * (h + 2)
                local stripW = math.min(1024, w - offX)
                render.drawTexturedRectUV(
                    rect.x + offX, rect.y, stripW, h,
                    0, offY / 1024, stripW / 1024, (offY + h) / 1024
                )
            end
        end
        render.setRenderTargetTexture(nil)
    end

    local function updatePanelAutoScroll(rects, dt)
        if ui.middlePressed and not state.viewportLooking and not ui.autoScroll and not ui._autoScrollConsumed then
            if rects.toolbox.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.toolbox.x, rects.toolbox.y, rects.toolbox.w, rects.toolbox.h)
                and Toolbox._contentH > Toolbox._viewH and Toolbox._viewH > 0 then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("toolbox", false, true)
            elseif rects.explorer.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.explorer.x, rects.explorer.y, rects.explorer.w, rects.explorer.h)
                and Explorer._contentH > Explorer._viewH and Explorer._viewH > 0 then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("explorer", false, true)
            elseif rects.properties.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.properties.x, rects.properties.y, rects.properties.w, rects.properties.h)
                and Properties._contentH > Properties._viewH and Properties._viewH > 0 then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("properties", false, true)
            elseif rects.timeline.w > 0 and ui:pointInRect(rects.timeline.x, rects.timeline.y, rects.timeline.w, rects.timeline.h) then
                ui:beginAutoScroll("timeline", true, false)
            end
        end

        local a = ui.autoScroll
        if not a then return end

        local dz = 8
        local dy = ui.my - a.originY
        local dx = ui.mx - a.originX

        if a.canV and math.abs(dy) > dz then
            local eff = (dy > 0) and (dy - dz) or (dy + dz)
            local speed = 3.0 + math.min(7.0, math.abs(eff) * 0.04)
            local deltaScroll = eff * speed * dt
            if a.id == "toolbox" then
                local maxS = math.max(0, Toolbox._contentH - Toolbox._viewH)
                Toolbox._scrollY = math.clamp(Toolbox._scrollY + deltaScroll, 0, maxS)
            elseif a.id == "explorer" then
                local maxS = math.max(0, Explorer._contentH - Explorer._viewH)
                Explorer._scrollY = math.clamp(Explorer._scrollY + deltaScroll, 0, maxS)
            elseif a.id == "properties" then
                local maxS = math.max(0, Properties._contentH - Properties._viewH)
                Properties._scrollY = math.clamp(Properties._scrollY + deltaScroll, 0, maxS)
            end
        end

        if a.canH and math.abs(dx) > dz and a.id == "timeline" then
            local effX = (dx > 0) and (dx - dz) or (dx + dz)
            local speed = 2.5 + math.min(6.0, math.abs(effX) * 0.035)
            local tl = state.timeline
            tl.scrollTime = math.clamp(tl.scrollTime + (effX * speed * dt) / math.max(25, tl.zoom), 0, math.max(0, tl.duration - 1))
        end
    end

    hook.add("DrawHUD", "solace_hud_paint", function()
        if not hasPermission("render.hud") then return end
        hudConnected = true

        local now = timer.realtime()
        local rawDt = now - lastFrameTime
        if rawDt > 0.12 then
            lowCpuUntil = math.max(lowCpuUntil, now + 0.6)
        end
        local dt = math.clamp(rawDt, 0.0001, 0.25)
        lastFrameTime = now
        local instFps = 1 / dt
        fpsSmooth = fpsSmooth * 0.92 + instFps * 0.08

        if not firstDrawLogged then
            firstDrawLogged = true
            local sw, sh = render.getResolution()
            printConsole(string.format("[SOLACE CL] DrawHUD active (%dx%d, studioOpen=%s)", sw or 0, sh or 0, tostring(state.studioOpen)))
        end

        local cpuRatio = getCpuRatio()
        if cpuRatio > 0.78 or isOverlayOrUnfocused() then
            if isOverlayOrUnfocused() and state.viewportLooking then
                state.viewportLooking = false
            end
            if Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
                ui:release("gizmo_drag")
            end
            return
        end
        local overlayPaused = (now < lowCpuUntil)

        Theme.beginFrame()

        if not state.studioOpen then
            if overlayPaused then return end
            local scrW = render.getResolution()
            local label = "SOLACE Minimized  |  Press F6 to Open"
            Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
            local tw = render.getTextSize(label)
            local pw = tw + 18
            local px = scrW - pw - 14
            local py = 14
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.hudPill)
            render.drawRectFast(px, py, pw, 24)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.borderLight)
            render.drawRectOutline(px, py, pw, 24, 1)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawSimpleText(px + 9, py + 12, label, TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
            return
        end

        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            syncCursor()
            ui:beginFrame(dt)
            ImGui.beginFrame(ui, state, dt)
            layout:compute(ui.scrW, ui.scrH)

            local rects = layout.rects
            if not overlayPaused then
                ViewportGrid.updateLookMode(state, ui, rects.viewport)
                camera:update(dt, ui, rects.viewport)
                updatePanelAutoScroll(rects, dt)
            end

            local sel = sceneGraph:getSelectedNode()
            local selIds = sceneGraph:getSelectedIds()
            actions.hasSelection = (sel ~= nil and sel.id ~= sceneGraph:getRootId())
            actions.canUndo = commands:canUndo()
            actions.canRedo = commands:canRedo()
            actions.undoLabel = commands:getUndoLabel()
            actions.redoLabel = commands:getRedoLabel()

            local actId = ui.activeId
            local focId = ui.focusedInputId
            local blinkPhase = math.floor(ui._frameTime * 2)
            local actPrefix3 = actId and string.sub(actId, 1, 3) or ""
            local actPrefix4 = actId and string.sub(actId, 1, 4) or ""
            local allowRepaint = (not overlayPaused) and (cpuRatio < 0.60)
            local hasInputEdge = ui.leftPressed or ui.leftReleased or ui.rightPressed or ui.rightReleased
                or (Explorer._draggingId ~= nil) or (Explorer._dragCandidate ~= nil) or (ImGui._activePopup ~= nil)
            local panelAllowRepaint = allowRepaint or hasInputEdge
            local tv = Theme._version or 1

            -- Draw docked studio panels around the live center 3D viewport cutout (1:1 RT cached when idle)
            local winFlags = state.windows and (tostring(state.windows.demo) .. tostring(state.windows.settings) .. tostring(state.windows.console) .. tostring(state.windows.project) .. tostring(state.windows.exporter)) or ""
            local fltFlags = state.floatingPanels and (tostring(state.floatingPanels.toolbox) .. tostring(state.floatingPanels.explorer) .. tostring(state.floatingPanels.properties) .. tostring(state.floatingPanels.timeline)) or ""
            local tbSig = tv .. "|" .. state.activeTool .. ":" .. tostring(state.transformSpace) .. ":" .. tostring(state.pivotMode) .. ":" .. tostring(state.pivotGizmoMode)
                .. ":" .. tostring(actions.hasSelection)
                .. ":" .. tostring(commands._version or 1)
                .. ":" .. tostring(state.grid.enabled) .. tostring(state.grid.showAxes) .. tostring(state.grid.showTelemetry) .. tostring(state.debugMode)
                .. ":" .. tostring(state.panels.toolbox) .. tostring(state.panels.explorer) .. tostring(state.panels.properties) .. tostring(state.panels.timeline)
                .. ":" .. winFlags .. ":" .. fltFlags
            drawCachedPanel("toolbar", rects.toolbar, tbSig, actPrefix3 == "tb_", panelAllowRepaint, function(r)
                Toolbar.draw(ui, state, layout, r, actions)
            end)

            local tbxFocus = (focId == "toolbox_search" or focId == "tbx_custom_mdl" or focId == "tbx_scan_dir")
            local tbxSig = tv .. "|" .. state.activeToolboxCategory .. "|" .. state.toolboxSearch .. "|" .. state.customModelPath
                .. "|" .. tostring(Toolbox._scrollY) .. "|" .. (tbxFocus and (focId .. ":" .. blinkPhase) or "")
                .. "|" .. (SOLACE.AssetScanner and (tostring(SOLACE.AssetScanner.scanning) .. ":" .. #SOLACE.AssetScanner.models .. ":" .. #SOLACE.AssetScanner.materials .. ":" .. #SOLACE.AssetScanner.sprites .. ":" .. tostring(SOLACE.AssetScanner.status)) or "")
                .. "|" .. #(state.favorites or {}) .. ":" .. #(state.recentAssets or {})
            drawCachedPanel("toolbox", rects.toolbox, tbxSig, actPrefix4 == "tbx_" or actPrefix4 == "tool", panelAllowRepaint, function(r)
                Toolbox.draw(ui, state, r, actions)
            end)

            local expFocus = (focId ~= nil and string.sub(focId, 1, 4) == "exp_") or (focId == "explorer_search")
            local expBuf = (expFocus and focId) and tostring(ui._editBuffers[focId] or "") or ""
            local expSig = tv .. "|" .. sceneGraph._version .. "|" .. tostring(sel and sel.id) .. "|" .. #selIds
                .. "|" .. state.explorerSearch .. "|" .. tostring(Explorer._scrollY)
                .. "|" .. tostring(Explorer._renamingId) .. "|" .. tostring(Explorer._draggingId)
                .. "|" .. (expFocus and (tostring(focId) .. ":" .. expBuf .. ":" .. blinkPhase) or "")
            drawCachedPanel("explorer", rects.explorer, expSig, actPrefix4 == "exp_" or actPrefix4 == "expl" or (Explorer._draggingId ~= nil) or (Explorer._dragCandidate ~= nil), panelAllowRepaint, function(r)
                Explorer.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, r, actions)
            end)

            local propFocus = (focId ~= nil and string.sub(focId, 1, 5) == "prop_")
            local propBuf = (propFocus and focId) and tostring(ui._editBuffers[focId] or "") or ""
            local selXform = sel and (sel.transform.pos[1] .. "," .. sel.transform.pos[2] .. "," .. sel.transform.pos[3]
                .. "," .. sel.transform.ang[1] .. "," .. sel.transform.ang[2] .. "," .. sel.transform.ang[3]
                .. "," .. sel.transform.scale[1] .. "," .. sel.transform.scale[2] .. "," .. sel.transform.scale[3]) or ""
            local selApp = (sel and sel.appearance) and (tostring(sel.appearance.material)
                .. ":" .. table.concat(sel.appearance.color or {}, ",")) or ""
            local c = state.camera
            local g = state.grid
            local s = state.sections
            local propSig = tv .. "|" .. sceneGraph._version .. "|" .. state.activeTool .. "|" .. tostring(sel and sel.id) .. "|" .. tostring(sel and sel.name)
                .. "|" .. tostring(sel and sel.visible) .. "|" .. #selIds .. "|" .. selXform .. "|" .. selApp
                .. "|" .. (sel and sel.transform.pivot and (sel.transform.pivot[1] .. "," .. sel.transform.pivot[2] .. "," .. sel.transform.pivot[3] .. "," .. (sel.transform.pivotAng and sel.transform.pivotAng[1] or 0)) or "")
                .. "|" .. (sel and sel.appearance and (tostring(sel.appearance.renderMode) .. ":" .. tostring(sel.appearance.renderFX) .. ":" .. tostring(sel.appearance.cullMode)) or "")
                .. "|" .. tostring(Properties._matError) .. "|" .. tostring(state.selectedBoneIdx) .. "|" .. tostring(state.activeCameraId)
                .. "|" .. c.speed .. ":" .. c.boostMult .. ":" .. c.sensitivity .. ":" .. c.fov .. ":" .. c.smoothing
                .. "|" .. tostring(g.enabled) .. ":" .. g.spacing .. ":" .. g.halfExtents .. ":" .. g.originMode
                .. ":" .. tostring(g.showOrientationGizmo) .. ":" .. tostring(g.showTelemetry) .. ":" .. tostring(state.debugMode)
                .. "|" .. tostring(s.selection) .. tostring(s.transform) .. tostring(s.appearance) .. tostring(s.pivot) .. tostring(s.bones) .. tostring(s.vfx) .. tostring(s.camNode) .. tostring(s.camera) .. tostring(s.grid) .. tostring(s.origin) .. tostring(s.quotas)
                .. "|" .. (s.quotas and math.floor(ui._frameTime * 5) or 0)
                .. "|" .. (propFocus and (tostring(focId) .. ":" .. propBuf .. ":" .. blinkPhase) or "")
                .. "|" .. tostring(Properties._scrollY)
            drawCachedPanel("properties", rects.properties, propSig, actPrefix4 == "prop" or actPrefix4 == "sec_", panelAllowRepaint, function(r)
                Properties.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, r, actions)
            end)

            local tl = state.timeline
            local tlSig = tv .. "|" .. sceneGraph._version .. "|" .. tostring(sel and sel.id)
                .. "|" .. tostring(tl.playing) .. "|" .. tostring(tl.loop) .. "|" .. tostring(tl.selectedTrackKey) .. "|" .. tostring(tl.selectedKeyframeIdx) .. "|" .. tostring(tl.defaultEasing)
                .. "|" .. tostring(state.selectedBoneIdx)
                .. "|" .. tl.currentTime .. "|" .. tl.duration .. "|" .. tl.fps .. "|" .. tl.zoom .. "|" .. tl.scrollTime
            drawCachedPanel("timeline", rects.timeline, tlSig, actPrefix3 == "tl_" or (SOLACE.Timeline._dragKf ~= nil), panelAllowRepaint, function(r)
                Timeline.draw(ui, state, sceneGraph, r, nil, actions)
            end)

            if panelAllowRepaint then
                -- Draw viewport frame, 3D orientation triad & telemetry
                ViewportGrid.drawOverlay(state, ui, rects.viewport, fpsSmooth, camera, sceneGraph)

                if state.activeCameraId then
                    local camNode = sceneGraph:getNode(state.activeCameraId)
                    local vp = rects.viewport
                    if camNode and vp.w >= 260 and vp.h >= 80 then
                        local pillTxt = "CAM PREVIEW: " .. camNode.name .. "  (ESC to Exit)"
                        Theme.setFont(Theme.Fonts.Small)
                        local pw = (render.getTextSize(pillTxt) or 180) + 20
                        local px = vp.x + math.floor((vp.w - pw) * 0.5)
                        local py = vp.y + 10
                        Theme.drawRoundedRect(4, px, py, pw, 22, Theme.C.accentSoft, 220)
                        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.accentBright)
                        render.drawRectOutline(px, py, pw, 22, 1)
                        Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
                        render.drawSimpleText(px + pw * 0.5, py + 11, pillTxt, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
                    end
                end

                -- Draw interactive panel splitters on top of panel borders
                layout:updateAndDrawSplitters(ui)

                -- Draw floating Dear ImGui windows (Z-ordered bottom-to-top) and undocked panels
                Windows.drawAll(ui, state, layout, sceneGraph, runtime, camera, events, actions)

                -- Draw active dropdown popups & toast notifications on top of everything
                ImGui.DrawPopups(ui)
                ImGui.DrawNotifications(ui, layout)

                -- Interactive 3D transform gizmo picking & dragging (before object selection so gizmo handles capture clicks)
                local overAnyFloating = (ui._topHoverWinId ~= nil) or ImGui.isPointOverAnyWindow(ui.mx, ui.my)
                if Gizmos.dragging or not overAnyFloating then
                    Gizmos.update(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, commands, rects.viewport, overlayPaused)
                else
                    Gizmos.hoveredAxis = nil
                end
                Gizmos.drawOverlay(ui, state, rects.viewport)

                -- Viewport raycast hover & click selection (only when no UI widget/gizmo/splitter/floating window/popup is hovered/active)
                if ui.hotId == nil and ui.activeId == nil and not ui.autoScroll and not ui._autoScrollConsumed
                    and not overAnyFloating then
                    Selection.pickAtCursor(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime, rects.viewport)
                    Selection.handleClick(ui, state, camera, sceneGraph, runtime, rects.viewport)
                else
                    Selection.hoveredId = nil
                end

                drawDebugOverlay(rects.viewport, cpuRatio)
            elseif Gizmos.dragging then
                Gizmos.finishDrag(sceneGraph, commands)
                ui:release("gizmo_drag")
            end
        end)

        if not ok then
            if render.isInRenderTarget and render.isInRenderTarget() then
                pcall(render.selectRenderTarget, nil)
            end
            if render.setRenderTargetTexture then
                pcall(render.setRenderTargetTexture, nil)
            end
            while #ui._clipStack > 0 do
                ui:popClip()
            end
            recordDiagError("DrawHUD", err)
            if not drawErrorLogged then
                drawErrorLogged = true
                print("[SOLACE] DrawHUD error: " .. tostring(err))
            end
            local sw = ui.scrW or render.getResolution()
            render.setFont(Theme.Fonts.BodyBold)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.danger, 230)
            render.drawRectFast(12, 12, math.min(sw - 24, 720), 34)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawSimpleText(22, 29, "SOLACE Render Error: " .. tostring(err), TEXT_ALIGN.LEFT, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        -- Permission warning banner if cursor/input/calcview/hologram permissions were restricted
        if not hasPermission("input.enablecursor") or not hasPermission("input") or not hasPermission("render.calcview") or not hasPermission("hologram.create") then
            local rects = layout.rects
            local sw = ui.scrW or render.getResolution()
            local msg = "SOLACE needs Cursor/Input/CalcView/Hologram permissions: Press E (Use) on the Starfall chip to grant."
            render.setFont(Theme.Fonts.BodyBold)
            local mw = render.getTextSize(msg) + 24
            local mx = math.floor((sw - mw) * 0.5)
            local my = (rects and rects.toolbar and rects.toolbar.h or 36) + 12
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.danger, 225)
            render.drawRectFast(mx, my, mw, 28)
            Theme.setColor(Theme.C.textPrimary)
            render.drawSimpleText(mx + mw * 0.5, my + 14, msg, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER, TEXT_ALIGN.CENTER)
        end

        ui:drawAutoScrollIcon()
        ui:drawTooltip()
        ui:endFrame(dt)
    end)
end)(SOLACE) end
